# Chinese terms: Genesis to Revelation

**Status: proposed, awaiting native Chinese reviewers.** Nothing in Chinese
is drafted yet; every form below is a first proposal, written with the
style guide before drafting so that books drafted in parallel agree. Every
Chinese record will be `ai_draft`. If a choice here changes, every passage
drafted under it is revised.

Reviewers: start with
[`docs/review-notes/zh-review-checklist.md`](review-notes/zh-review-checklist.md).

## How this glossary relates to the style guide

`docs/chinese-style.md` is the brief for drafting Chinese (Simplified
characters, Mainland usage) and holds in full: the three levels (直译,
均衡, 易读), register, address, the divine name and the setting spans,
punctuation, numbers, the New Testament's words (section 7) and the
formulas (section 12). This glossary is the single reference for the words,
formulas and names the whole Bible needs beyond those. `docs/hindi-terms.md`,
`docs/russian-terms.md` and `docs/indonesian-terms.md` are its counterparts
in other languages; each book's own terms note (`docs/isaiah-terms.md` and
the others) still settles the meaning, and this file settles only the
Chinese wording.

Where a form is marked *alt.*, that is the main alternative a reviewer
should weigh. Where this translation's New Testament or an earlier book
already has a form, that form wins. Decision text stays in English, quoting
the Chinese words.

Below, *dn* is `{{divine_name:上主|雅威}}` and *gt* is `{{god_term:神|上帝}}`
(style guide section 4). Levels are L (直译), B (均衡) and R (易读).

## Drafting

- Translate from the Hebrew (and the Aramaic in Daniel 2:4-7:28, Ezra
  4:8-6:18 and 7:12-26, and Jeremiah 10:11) and the Greek, using the literal
  gloss, the English and the meaning decisions to see how a sentence was
  read. Never from a reference Bible. **The Chinese Union Version (和合本)
  is for comparison only**, though its text is in the public domain in many
  places; the copyrighted versions (和合本修订版, 新译本, 当代译本, 现代中文译本,
  中文标准译本, 思高本 and the others) likewise. The familiar passages pull
  hardest toward the CUV: Genesis 1-3, Exodus 20, Deuteronomy 6, Numbers
  6:24-26; Psalms 1, 23, 51, 91, 103, 121, 139; Proverbs 3:5-6, 31;
  Ecclesiastes 1-3 and 12; Isaiah 9, 40, 53; Jeremiah 31; Micah 5-6; the
  Sermon on the Mount, John 1 and 3, Romans 8, 1 Corinthians 13. Draft from
  the gloss and the English levels and rewrite any sentence that matches a
  well-known version for more than a short common phrase.
- The vocabulary in this file is the church's shared vocabulary (恩典, 燔祭,
  会幕, 约柜, 舍客勒), and the names are the conventional forms; using them is
  not copying wording. A clause is wording.
- Keep every `\v N ` marker where the English has it. Lines (`\q `): see
  [Poetry conventions](#poetry-conventions).
- Quotation marks “ ” outside and ‘ ’ inside, written curly in the data.
  Each level opens and closes the same quotations as the English level.
- Settings mirror the English spans where Chinese has the same choice:
  `divine_name`, `deity_pronoun` (他 / 祂, 你 / 祢), `units`, `christos`, and
  `gender` where Chinese really has two wordings; and the Chinese-only
  `god_term` for every word naming the one God.
- Narrative: plain past without a 了 on every verb; 了 where the action's
  completion matters, 就 and 于是 for sequence.
- Laws and instructions (Exodus 20-40, Leviticus, much of Numbers,
  Deuteronomy's speeches): 你要…… / 不可…… / 他要把……带来 at L and B;
  你必须…… / 不要…… / 他应当…… at R.
- Rendering decisions: zero to two per sentence, only where Chinese had to
  choose. A book-wide choice is recorded once, in the first passage file of
  each book; later chapters follow it.
- Measures: metric by default where the English converts, in Arabic digits
  (约45厘米), the original unit as the alternative in Chinese numerals;
  Literal keeps the original unit where the English Literal does. Where the
  English has no `units` span, neither does the Chinese. See
  [Land, life and measures](#land-life-and-measures).

## Names of God and divine titles

| Hebrew / Greek (English) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| YHWH (the LORD) | *dn* |
| YHWH *Elohim* (the LORD God) | `{{divine_name:上主\|雅威}}{{god_term:神\|上帝}}` (上主神 by default), the two words side by side as the Chinese Bibles write them |
| the LORD my God; your God; our God | *dn*我的*gt*; *dn*你的*gt* (你们的 for the plural); *dn*我们的*gt* |
| *ʾAdonai* YHWH (the Lord GOD) | 主宰*dn* (主宰上主 / 主宰雅威). *alt.* 主*dn* (主上主), see the style guide section 4 |
| *ʾAdonai* (Lord); *ʾadon* of God | 主 |
| *Elohim*, *El*, *Eloah* (God, of the God of Israel) | *gt* |
| *elohim* of other gods; idols | 神, 神明, 众神, 别神, plain (no span) |
| El Shaddai (God Almighty); *Shaddai* (the Almighty) | 全能的*gt*; 全能者 |
| El Elyon (God Most High); *ʿElyon* (the Most High) | 至高的*gt* (as Hebrews 7:1); 至高者 |
| YHWH *tsevaot* (the LORD of hosts; of Armies at R) | 万军之*dn* (L, B); 统领万军的*dn* (R) |
| *Yah* | `{{divine_name:上主\|雅}}` |
| the angel of the LORD | *dn*的使者 (L, B, R); "angel" as 天使 only where the English level says angel |
| the God of Israel; of Abraham, Isaac and Jacob | 以色列的*gt*; 亚伯拉罕、以撒、雅各的*gt* |
| the Holy One of Israel | 以色列的圣者 (one form, Isaiah throughout) |
| the Mighty One of Jacob | 雅各的大能者 (Genesis 49:24; Isaiah 49:26) |
| Redeemer (*goʾel*, of God) | 救赎主 (L, B); 拯救我们的那一位 / 救主 (R) |
| the Spirit of the LORD; of God | *dn*的灵; *gt*的灵 |
| the man of God | *gt*的人 |
| the LORD's anointed | *dn*所膏立的 (L, B); *dn*所立的王 (R) |
| the Ancient of Days (Daniel 7) | 亘古常在者 (L, B); 那位永远存在的 (R). *alt.* 远古的那一位 |
| "the LORD our righteousness" (Jeremiah 23:6; 33:16) | *dn*是我们的义 (one form) |
| my rock (*tsur*); my crag (*selaʿ*); my fortress (*metsudah*) | 我的磐石; 我的岩石; 我的堡垒 (as 2 Samuel 22, Psalm 18) |
| refuge; shield; stronghold (*misgav*) | 避难所; 盾牌; 高处的堡垒 (L, B), 安全的高处 (R) |
| Shepherd (of God) | 牧者 (L, B), 牧人 (R) |
| King (of God) | 王, 君王 |

- **The name's agreement.** Chinese has no agreement; nothing outside the
  span depends on which option is shown. Check only that 雅威 and 上主 both
  read well before what follows (上主的殿 / 雅威的殿, 上主啊 / 雅威啊).
- **YHWH and *ʾAdonai*.** 上主 and 主 are kept apart exactly as LORD and Lord:
  主 never takes a span; 上主 never stands outside one.
- 耶和华 is not used; Exodus 3:14-15 and 6:2-3, where the name itself is the
  subject, follow the English wording and decisions.
- *Hallelu Yah*: 你们要赞美`{{divine_name:上主|雅}}` (L, B), 要赞美`{{divine_name:上主|雅}}`
  (R). Where the English keeps "Hallelujah" as a word (Revelation 19:1-6):
  哈利路亚, no span.

## Address and pronouns

The style guide section 5 holds in full; in brief:

- **God in the third person**: 他 in a `deity_pronoun` span,
  `{{deity_pronoun:他|祂}}`, wherever the English spans "he / him / his".
- **People speaking or praying to God**: 你 in a span, `{{deity_pronoun:你|祢}}`,
  at every level (David, the psalmists, Job, Moses, the prophets). 您 is not
  used for God.
- **God speaking**: 我, no span; to people 你 / 你们, one person or many;
  also to the Servant in Isaiah 42-53.
- **To a king**: 王啊 (L); 陛下 and 您 (B, R). "My lord the king": 我主我王
  (L, B), 陛下 (R). "Let the king live": 愿王万岁 (L, B), 祝陛下万岁 (R).
- **To a ruler or master**: 你 at L; 您 at B and R. Moses and Aaron say 您
  to Pharaoh at B and R, and Pharaoh says 你们 to them. Joseph's brothers say
  您 to Joseph in Egypt (B, R) until he makes himself known (Genesis 45:4),
  where the change of address is noted.
- **Family and equals**: 你. Isaac to Abraham (Genesis 22:7) says 你 at L,
  您 at B and R; Esau and Jacob say 你. Nathan says 你 to David ("你就是那个人！",
  2 Samuel 12:7, at every level: a prophet's rebuke drops the courtesy).
- **"Your servant" for "I"** (*ʿavdeka*): 仆人 / 你的仆人 at L and where the
  English Balanced keeps it; 我 at R.
- **The psalmist to people and to his enemies**: 你们 / 你. The lovers in the
  Song of Songs: 你.
- **Lines the New Testament quotes keep its forms of address** (Psalm 2:7 in
  Hebrews 1:5; Psalm 45:6-7 in Hebrews 1:8-9).

| bless, blessing; curse | 赐福, 福 / 祝福; 诅咒 (verb and noun). 咒诅 (the CUV's order) is not used |
| holy; holiness | 圣 / 圣洁; 圣洁 |
| clean, unclean (ritually) | 洁净, 不洁净 |
| sin; iniquity (*ʿawon*); transgression (*peshaʿ*) | 罪; 罪孽 (L), 罪 / 罪过 (B, R); 过犯 |
| make atonement for | 为……赎罪 (L, B); 除去……的罪 (R) |
| the righteous; the upright | 义人; 正直的人 |
| righteousness (*tsedaqah*); justice (*mishpat*) | 义 / 公义; 公平 (B, R: 公正) |
| the wicked; evildoers | 恶人; 作恶的人 |
| *ʾemunah*, *ʾemet* (faithfulness, truth) | 信实 where it is God's; 真实 / 真理 |
| salvation; save, deliver | 救恩 / 拯救; 拯救, 救, 解救 |
| the fear of the LORD | 敬畏*dn* (L, B); 尊敬并畏惧*dn* (R) |
| *nefesh* (soul) | 灵魂 / 生命 (L); 心, 生命, or 我 at B and R as the English does |
| "all flesh" | 一切血肉之躯 (L), 所有的人 (B, R) |
| the remnant | 余民 (L, B); 剩下的人 (R) |
| statute; rule; commandment | 律例; 典章 (L), 规定 (B, R); 诫命 |
| a lasting statute | 永远的定例 |
| glory (*kavod*) | 荣耀 |
| repent, turn (*shuv*) | 回转 (L), 回转 / 悔改 (B, R), as the English level |
| idol(s) | 偶像 |
| vow; free-will gift | 许愿; 甘心献的礼物 |

### Hesed, in its three uses

| Use | Chinese |
|---|---|
| God's steadfast love | 慈爱 (L, B); 始终不变的爱 (R). *alt.* 坚定的爱 at every level, closer to "steadfast" |
| Between people (a kindness, loyalty) | 慈爱 (L); 恩情 / 善待 (B, R) as the sentence needs ("show me kindness": 恩待我) |
| Israel's loyalty to God (Hosea 4:1; 6:4, 6; 10:12; 12:7) | 慈爱 (L); 忠诚的爱 (B), 忠心 (R). Hosea 6:6 keeps Matthew 9:13's frame (怜悯) with a footnoted decision |

The refrain "for he is good; his steadfast love is forever" (1 Chronicles
16:34; Psalms 106, 107, 118, 136) has one wording per level, set where it
first appears and copied after. The CUV's wording of it is among the best
known lines in Chinese and is **not** used; draft it afresh from the Hebrew
(a first proposal: 他是美善的，他的慈爱永不止息 at L and B, 他真好，他的爱永远不变
at R), for a reviewer to settle.

### Torah, law

| Where | Chinese |
|---|---|
| the Law of Moses; the book of the law | 律法; 律法书 |
| God's *torah* in the Psalms | 律法 (L, B); 教导 (R) |
| *torah* in the Prophets (God's teaching through the prophets) | 教导 at every level; where the New Testament quotes a line with "law" (Jeremiah 31:33 in Hebrews 8:10), its word 律法 is kept there, with a decision |
| *torah* of father or mother (Proverbs) | 教导 / 教诲 |

Psalm 119's eight words each have one fixed word per level, recorded in
decisions at 119:1-8 and kept through the psalm. Proposed: law 律法, testimonies
见证, precepts 训示, statutes 律例, commandments 诫命, rules 判语 (L, B), word 话,
promise 应许; R as the English Readable groups them. The CUV's set (律法, 法度,
训词, 律例, 命令, 典章, 话, 应许) is shared vocabulary a reviewer may prefer.

### Sheol and the Pit

| Hebrew (English) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| Sheol | 阴间 (L, B), the same word as Hades (Acts 2:27 quotes Psalm 16:10); 死亡之地 (R). *alt.* the transliteration 示阿勒 at L |
| the Pit (*bor*, *shaḥat*) | 坑 (L), 深坑 (B), 坟墓 (R) |
| Abaddon | 亚巴顿 (L), 灭亡之地 (B, R), as the English level |

### Nations and peoples

| Hebrew (English) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| the nations (*goyim*) | 列国 (L), 各国 / 万国 (B, R); 外邦人 where Gentiles are meant as against Israel |
| the peoples (*ʿammim*) | 万民, 各族的人 |
| my people | 我的子民 (L, B), 我的百姓 (R) |
| all the families of the earth | 地上的万族 (L, B), 世上所有的家族 (R) |

The temple furnishings in Ezekiel 40-48 follow 1 Kings 6-7.

## Land, life and measures

Unit words are shared vocabulary (the forms Chinese Bibles use). Values as
the English conversion, written in Arabic digits in the modern option.

| Unit (English) | Chinese | Modern value |
|---|---|---|
| cubit | 肘 | 约45厘米 (Ezekiel 40-48: 约53厘米) |
| handbreadth; span | 掌; 虎口 | 约7.5厘米; 约23厘米 |
| shekel; beka; gerah | 舍客勒; 比加; 季拉 | 11.5克; 5.7克; 0.6克 |
| talent; mina | 他连得; 弥那 | as money, in wages, as the denarius: a talent about twenty years' wages (`{{units:约二十年的工钱\|一他连得}}`, five talents 约一百年的工钱), a mina about a hundred days' wages; the weight (34公斤; 0.6公斤) only where the text weighs it |
| ephah (a tenth of an ephah); omer | 伊法 (伊法的十分之一); 俄梅珥 | 22升 (2.2升); 2.2升 |
| seah; hin; bath | 细亚; 欣; 罢特 | 7升; 3.7升; 22升 |
| homer; cor | 贺梅珥; 柯珥 | 220升 |
| stadion (*stadia*) | 斯塔迪亚 | 约185米 (十五个斯塔迪亚: 约3公里) |
| Roman mile | 罗马里 | 约1.5公里 |
| denarius | 银币 (L: 一个银币, with a decision naming the coin); *alt.* 第纳流斯 | a day's wage: 一天的工钱 |
| drachma; lepton; quadrans | 德拉克马; 小铜钱; 一文钱 | |

- Days, years, ages and counts are not units, and are written in Chinese
  numerals (style guide section 10).
- Hebrew months: the first month 正月 (B, R) / 第一个月 (L), and by name as
  the Hebrew has it: 亚笔月, 西弗月, 西弯月, 以他念月, 布勒月, 基斯流月, 提别月,
  细罢特月, 亚达月, 尼散月, 以禄月.
- Times of day: 早晨, 中午, 傍晚; "the third hour" 第三时 (L), 上午九点左右 (B,
  R), with a decision.

## Recurring formulas

The style guide's section 12 table holds (Thus says the LORD, declares the
LORD, the word of the LORD came, behold, *wayehi*, Woe, Fear not). The rest:

### Kings and Chronicles: the regnal formulas

One wording per level through Kings and Chronicles. B smooths; R may split
the sentence.

- **Accession.** "In the Nth year of X king of Judah, Y son of Z became king
  over Israel in Samaria, and he reigned N years." L: 犹大王X第N年，Z的儿子Y
  在撒玛利亚登基，作以色列的王，共作王N年。 R: X作犹大王的第N年，Z的儿子Y
  在撒玛利亚当了以色列的王，统治了N年。
- **Age and mother (Judah).** 他登基的时候N岁，在耶路撒冷作王N年。他的母亲
  名叫X。
- **Verdict.** "He did what was evil / right in the eyes of the LORD": 他做了
  在*dn*眼中为恶 / 为正的事 (L); 他做了*dn*看为恶 / 看为正的事 (B); 他做了
  *dn*不喜悦 / 喜悦的事 (R).
- **Source.** "Are they not written in the book of the chronicles of the
  kings of Israel?": 这些事不都写在《以色列诸王的史记》上吗？ (L, B); 这些事都
  记在《以色列诸王的史记》里 (R). The title takes 《 》.
- **Death.** "He slept with his fathers and was buried": 他与他的
  `{{gender:祖先|列祖}}`同睡，被埋葬在…… (L, B), where the English has a span;
  他去世了，葬在…… (R).
- **Genealogies (1 Chronicles 1-9)** keep one list formula per level: "the
  sons of X: A, B and C" X的儿子是A、B和C (the enumeration comma 、 between
  names, 和 before the last); "X fathered Y" X生Y (L, as Matthew 1), X是Y的
  父亲 (R). Every name in the forms of Genesis, Numbers and the tables below.

### The Prophets

One wording per level, through all seventeen books.

| Hebrew (English L / R) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| *koh ʾamar YHWH* (Thus says the LORD) | *dn*如此说 (L); *dn*这样说 (B); *dn*说 (R) |
| *neʾum YHWH* (declares the LORD) | 这是*dn*的宣告 (L); 这是*dn*的话 (B); *dn*说 (R) |
| the word of the LORD came to X | *dn*的话临到X (L, B); *dn*对X说 (R) |
| *ʾAdonai YHWH* (the Lord GOD) | 主宰*dn* |
| YHWH of hosts | 万军之*dn* (L, B); 统领万军的*dn* (R) |
| *massa* (oracle / message) | 默示 (L); 信息 (B, R) |
| *massa* as a heading | 关于X的默示 (L); 关于X的信息 (B); 这是关于X的信息 (R) |
| *hoy* (woe / how terrible) | ……有祸了 (L, B); R as the English |
| in that day | 到那日 (L, B); 到那时 (R) |
| the day of the LORD | *dn*的日子 |
| "Son of man" (Ezekiel's address, *ben adam*) | 人子啊 (L); 人哪 (B, R), kept apart from Jesus's title 人子 at B and R |
| "one like a son of man" (Daniel 7:13) | 有一位像人子的, the form the Gospels quote |
| "they will know that I am the LORD" (Ezekiel) | 他们就知道我是*dn* (L, B); 他们就会明白我是*dn* (R) |
| the Servant (*ʿeved YHWH*, Isaiah 42-53) | 我的仆人; where Matthew 12:18-21 or Acts 8:32-33 quotes the line, its wording |
| daughter Zion | 锡安女子 (L, B); 锡安城的人 (R) (one form, Lamentations too) |
| the Branch | 苗裔 (L, B); 后裔 / 新枝 (R) as the English level |

### Psalm titles, musical terms and Selah

| Hebrew (English L / R) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| *lamnatseach* (For the director / For the leader of the music) | 交给指挥 (L, B); 给诗班指挥的 (R) |
| *mizmor* (a psalm) | 诗 (大卫的诗); 歌 (R) |
| *shir* (a song) | 歌 |
| *maskil*, *miktam*, *shiggayon* | where the English transliterates: 马斯基勒, 米克坦, 示迦约; where it translates, translated as the English level |
| *tefillah* (a prayer) | 祷告 |
| *Hallelu Yah* (Praise the LORD) | 你们要赞美`{{divine_name:上主\|雅}}` (L, B); 要赞美`{{divine_name:上主\|雅}}` (R) |
| *shir hammaʿalot* (A song of ascents / for the journey up to Jerusalem) | 上行之诗 (L, B); 上耶路撒冷朝圣时唱的诗 (R) |
| Selah | 细拉 |

- **Titles.** A psalm's title is verse 1 and is translated at every level as
  the English level does it: Literal keeps the Hebrew's words, Readable may
  explain.
- Instruments, tunes and the terms whose meaning is lost (*gittit*,
  *sheminit*, *alamot*, *neginot*, *mahalat*) follow the English level: kept
  as names where it keeps them, transliterated by the rules of the style
  guide section 6, translated where it translates, with the English
  decision's uncertainty carried into the Chinese one.
- **Selah** stays at the end of its verse (or line) at every level, as the
  English has it: 细拉. The first use in each block of psalms gets a decision
  (a word whose meaning is unknown, probably a musical direction).
- **Amen** 阿们 (Psalms 41:14; 72:19; 89:53; 106:48, at the end of each book
  of Psalms; Nehemiah 8:6).

## Poetry conventions

- **Lines follow the English record.** Where an English level has `\q`
  breaks, that Chinese level has the same lines, broken at Chinese's own
  phrase boundaries; where the English level is prose, so is the Chinese.
  A space on both sides of each marker.
- **Parallel lines** are not forced into equal character counts or into
  four-character phrases: Chinese verse habits (对仗, rhyme) are not imposed on
  Hebrew poetry.
- **Acrostics** (Lamentations 1-4, Psalms 9-10, 25, 34, 37, 111, 112, 119,
  145, Proverbs 31:10-31): follow the English; no letter headings unless it
  has them.
- **Personified women.** Wisdom and Folly are women in the Hebrew: 她 and
  她们; Zion and Jerusalem pictured as a woman take 她, as Lamentations.

## Names

**Policy** (style guide section 6). The forms in common use in the
Protestant Chinese Bibles for every well-known name; the modern standard
Chinese form for places that still exist where it is far better known
(大马士革, 塞浦路斯, 克里特, 马耳他); one person, one form, in every book;
names the New Testament also has keep the New Testament's form. New
transliterations by the style guide's rules, added to
[Added while drafting](#added-while-drafting).

- **Two names for one person.** Keep each name where the source has it:
  Abram 亚伯兰 / Abraham 亚伯拉罕; Sarai 撒莱 / Sarah 撒拉; Jacob 雅各 / Israel
  以色列; Uzziah 乌西雅 / Azariah 亚撒利雅 (Kings); Nebuchadnezzar 尼布甲尼撒 /
  Nebuchadrezzar 尼布甲尼撒 (one Chinese form; a decision notes the Hebrew
  spelling where it differs); Saul 扫罗 / Paul 保罗; Simon 西门 / Peter 彼得 /
  Cephas 矶法.
- **Same name, two people.** Chinese Bibles distinguish the apostle Philip
  腓力 from Philip the evangelist 腓利 (Acts 6:5; 8; 21:8); the distinction is
  kept, as readers know it. Otherwise one name, one form (James and Jacob
  are both 雅各).
- Beersheba 别是巴 is kept apart from Bathsheba 拔示巴.

## Parallel passages

**Policy.** Draft once, reuse. Where the source is the same, the later
passage copies the earlier wording at each level; where it differs, the
wording differs, with a decision naming the other passage. Samuel and Kings
are drafted before Chronicles, which retells them.

| Passage | Same as |
|---|---|
| 1-2 Chronicles (where the Hebrew is the same) | Samuel and Kings |
| Psalm 18 | 2 Samuel 22 (22:50 keeps Romans 15:9's wording) |
| Psalm 96; 105:1-15; 106:1, 47-48 | 1 Chronicles 16:8-36 |
| Psalm 53 | Psalm 14 |
| Psalm 70 | Psalm 40:13-17 |
| Psalm 108 | Psalms 57:7-11 and 60:5-12 |
| Isaiah 36:1-39:8 | 2 Kings 18:13-20:19 |
| Jeremiah 52 (and 39:1-10) | 2 Kings 24:18-25:30 |
| Isaiah 2:2-4 | Micah 4:1-3 |
| Obadiah 1-9 | Jeremiah 49:7-16 |
| The Synoptic parallels (Matthew, Mark, Luke) | where the Greek is the same, the same Chinese, as the English does |

## The New Testament quotes the Old

**Policy.** Where the New Testament quotes a line and the Hebrew says the
same, use the New Testament's Chinese wording at the matching level. Where
the New Testament follows the Greek Old Testament and differs, translate the
Hebrew and add a decision naming the New Testament form (Psalm 40:6 "a
body"; Isaiah 7:14 "virgin"; Amos 9:11-12 in Acts 15; and the others listed
in `docs/hindi-terms.md`, "The New Testament quotes the Old"). Quoted lines
keep the New Testament's forms of address.

## Book notes

- **Genesis to Deuteronomy.** Genesis 1:26 "Let us": 我们, no span. YHWH
  *Elohim* in Genesis 2-3: `{{divine_name:上主|雅威}}{{god_term:神|上帝}}`. Exodus 3:14-15 and 6:2-3 follow the
  English on the name. Noah's ark 方舟. Leviticus sets the sacrifices above.
- **Joshua to Ruth.** *shofet* in Judges: 士师 (L, B), 领袖 (R). Ruth's
  *goʾel* above.
- **1-2 Samuel.** "How the mighty have fallen" (2 Samuel 1:19, 25, 27): one
  wording per level, set at 1:19. 2 Samuel 22 sets the rock and Sheol words
  for Psalm 18.
- **1-2 Kings, 1-2 Chronicles.** Regnal formulas above; Chronicles reuses
  Samuel and Kings.
- **Job.** "The satan": 撒但 (L), 控告者 (B, R), as the English level has a
  name or a title. "The sons of God": *gt*的众子 (L), 天使 / 天上的众生 (B, R),
  as the English level.
- **Psalms.** Titles, Selah and parallels above; Psalm 119's eight words.
- **Proverbs.**

  | Hebrew (English) | Chinese |
  |---|---|
  | *ḥokmah* (wisdom) | 智慧 |
  | *binah*, *tevunah* (understanding) | 悟性 (L, B); 明白事理 (R) |
  | *daʿat* (knowledge) | 知识 |
  | *musar* (discipline; correction at R) | 管教 (L, B, whatever the English Balanced word); 纠正 (R) |
  | *kesil*, *ʾewil*, *nabal* (fool) | 愚昧人 (L, B); 愚蠢的人 (R), with a decision naming the Hebrew word |
  | *lets* (mocker) | 好讥笑人的 (L, B); 爱嘲笑别人的人 (R) |
  | *peti* (the simple / naive) | 无知的人 (L, B); 容易受骗的人 (R) |
  | *ʿatsel* (sluggard) | 懒惰的人 |
  | *beni* (my son) | 我儿 (L, B); 孩子 (R) |
  | the strange woman | as the English level: 外女 (L), 不属于你的女人 (B), 别人的妻子 / 放荡的女人 (R) |

- **Ecclesiastes.** Qoheleth: 柯希列 (L, a transliteration, where the English
  Literal keeps the Hebrew title), 传道者 (B, R). *hevel* follows the English
  level, which says "breath" at every level: 气息 (L), 一口气 (B), 像一口气 with
  the sense spelled out (R); the CUV's 虚空的虚空 is not used. "Under the sun": 日光之下 (L, B), 在这世上 (R).
- **Song of Songs.** The lovers say 你. Translated as love poetry; an
  allegorical reading is not added.
- **Isaiah.** "The Holy One of Israel" one form. The Servant passages
  (42:1-9; 49:1-13; 50:4-11; 52:13-53:12): 我的仆人, God addressing him with
  你. 7:14 *ʿalmah*: the meaning decision is "the young woman", so 年轻女子 at
  every level, with a footnoted decision naming Matthew 1:23's 童女 (from the
  Greek *parthenos*).
- **Jeremiah and Lamentations.** "The LORD our righteousness" one form;
  daughter Zion one form; Zion as a woman takes 她.
- **Ezekiel.** "Son of man" as above; "they will know that I am the LORD"
  one wording per level; the temple measurements (40-48) with `units` spans
  where the English has them, the long cubit 约53厘米.
- **Daniel.** Aramaic 2:4-7:28. "The Ancient of Days" above; "the
  abomination of desolation" as Matthew 24:15 where quoted.
- **The Twelve.** Hosea's hesed is Israel's loyalty (above). Jonah 2 and
  Habakkuk 3 are poetry. Malachi 3:1 and 4:5-6 (Hebrew 3:23-24) keep the
  Gospels' wording where the Hebrew agrees.
- **New Testament.** `docs/chinese-style.md` sections 5-8 and 12, and the
  book terms notes. The Gospels' Aramaic words (Mark 5:41; 7:34; 15:34)
  are transliterated by the style guide's rules from the Greek, as the
  English level gives them, with the translation the Greek supplies.

## To be settled by reviewers

The choices in this file a native reviewer is most likely to change, each
with its main alternative:

- 平安祭 / 和好祭 / 团契祭 for *shelamim*.
- 赎罪盖 / 施恩座 for *kapporet*.
- 慈爱 / 坚定的爱 for God's hesed at L and B.
- Psalm 119's eight words: this file's set or the CUV's shared set.
- 阴间 / 示阿勒 for Sheol at L.
- 古列, 大利乌, 亚达薛西 / 居鲁士, 大流士, 阿尔塔薛西斯 for the Persian kings.
- 凯撒, 凯撒利亚 / 该撒, 该撒利亚.
- 人哪 / 人子 for Ezekiel's *ben adam* at B and R.
- 辩护者 / 保惠师 for *paraklētos* (style guide section 7).
- 银币 / 第纳流斯 for the denarius.

appears and the decision that records it.

|---|---|

### Acts

Acts keeps the Gospels' forms for every name and term above, and Luke's
Chinese where its Greek is the same (Psalm 110:1 in 2:34-35 as Luke
20:42-43; Isaiah 6:9-10 in 28:26-27 as Matthew 13:14-15). Repeated
passages inside Acts keep one wording: Jesus's saying in 1:5 and 11:16;
Psalm 16:10 in 2:27 and 13:35 ("decay" 腐朽 at L and B, 腐烂 at R, through
2:31 and 13:34-37); Saul's call in 9:4-5, 22:7-8 and 26:14-15, where only
26:14 adds the goads. Psalm 2:7 (13:33) is to be kept at Hebrews 1:5 and
5:5. Names not in the tables above, as the text has them:

### Romans

Romans keeps the Gospels' and Acts' forms for every name and term above, and
their Chinese wherever the same Old Testament text is quoted in the same
Greek (Joel 2:32 in 10:13 as Acts 2:21; Isaiah 53:1 in 10:16 as John 12:38;
Leviticus 19:18 and the commandments in 13:9 as Matthew 19:18-19, 22:39 and
Mark 12:31). Repeated quotations inside Romans keep one wording: Genesis 15:6
in 4:3, 9, 22-24; Isaiah 28:16 in 9:33 and 10:11 ("不会蒙羞"); "You shall not
covet" in 7:7 and 13:9 (你不可贪图 at L, 不要贪图 at B). Names not in the
tables above, as the text has them:

| English (first verse) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| Illyricum (15:19) | 以利哩古 |
| Phoebe; Cenchreae (16:1) | 非比; 坚革哩 (as Acts 18:18) |
| Prisca (16:3) | 百基, kept apart from Priscilla 百基拉 (Acts 18) because the English keeps the two forms apart; also 1 Corinthians 16:19, 2 Timothy 4:19 |
| Epaenetus (16:5) | 以拜尼土 |
| Mary (16:6) | 马利亚 |
| Andronicus, Junia (16:7) | 安多尼古, 犹尼亚 (a woman, as the meaning decision reads) |
| Ampliatus (16:8) | 暗伯利 |
| Urbanus, Stachys (16:9) | 耳巴奴, 士大古 |
| Apelles; Aristobulus (16:10) | 亚比利; 亚利斯多布 |
| Herodion; Narcissus (16:11) | 希罗天; 拿其数 |
| Tryphaena, Tryphosa; Persis (16:12) | 土非拿, 土富撒; 彼息 |
| Rufus (16:13) | 鲁孚 (as Mark 15:21) |
| Asyncritus, Phlegon, Hermes, Patrobas, Hermas (16:14) | 亚逊其土, 弗勒干, 赫米, 八罗巴, 赫马. The man Hermes is 赫米, kept apart from the god Hermes, 赫耳墨斯 (Acts 14:12) |

### 1 Corinthians

1 Corinthians keeps Romans' forms for Paul's vocabulary and the Gospels' and
Acts' forms for every name: the grace-and-peace greeting of 1:3 word for word
as Romans 1:7 at each level; the Lord's Supper (11:23-25) as Luke 22:19-20
where the Greek is the same ("感谢之后掰开", "你们要这样做，来记念我",
"凭着我的血[所立]的新约"), Paul's own "which is for you" without Luke's
"献出"; the Jeremiah quotation of 1:31 at each level as 2 Corinthians 10:17
("夸口的人，要在主里夸口"; "谁要夸口，就应当在主里夸口"; "谁想要夸口，就只该为主夸口"),
with "主" and no divine_name span. Names not in the tables above, as the text
has them:

| English (first verse) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| Sosthenes (1:1) | 所提尼 (as Acts 18:17) |
| Chloe (1:11) | 革来 |
| Cephas (1:12; 3:22; 9:5; 15:5) | 矶法 (L, B), as John 1:42; 彼得 (R), as the English Readable |
| Crispus; Gaius (1:14) | 基利司布 (as Acts 18:8); 该犹 (as Romans 16:23) |
| Stephanas (1:16; 16:15, 17) | 司提法那, kept apart from Stephen 司提反 (Acts 6-7) |
| Fortunatus, Achaicus (16:17) | 福徒拿都, 亚该古 |
| Apollos (1:12) | 亚波罗 (as Acts 18:24) |
| Aquila and Prisca (16:19) | 亚居拉; 百基 (Prisca, as Romans 16:3), kept apart from Priscilla 百基拉 (Acts 18) |
| Marana tha (16:22) | 玛拉那他 (L), as the English Literal keeps the Aramaic; 我们的主啊，请来！ (B); 我们的主耶稣啊，请来！ (R) |

Terms settled in 1 Corinthians:

| English | Chinese |
|---|---|
| boast, boasting (kauchaomai, kauchēma, kauchēsis) | 夸口 at Literal throughout, as 2 Corinthians and Romans. Where the argument turns on boasting itself, in people or in gifts received (1:29-31; 3:21; 4:7; 5:6; 9:15-16): 夸口 at every level. Proper pride (15:31 "my boasting about you"): 以……为荣 (B, R), 引以为荣 where the thing is not named. 感到自豪, 夸耀, 炫耀, 自夸 are not used. 13:3 follows SBLGNT ("burned"), so it has no boasting |
| puffed up (physioō: 4:6, 18-19; 5:2; 8:1; 13:4) | 自高自大 (L, B); 骄傲 (R). 自我膨胀 is not used |
| ministry; servant (diakonia; diakonos) | 事奉 (L, B) for the ministries the Lord gives (12:5) and the household of Stephanas's service to the saints (16:15, 对圣徒的事奉); the verb 服事 (B, R). 仆人 (every level) for Paul and Apollos (3:5). Not 职事 or 执事 |
| saints, holy ones (hagioi: 1:2; 6:1-2; 14:33; 16:1, 15) | 圣徒 (L, B), also where the English Balanced says "God's holy people" (not 神的圣徒); 属于`{{god_term:神\|上帝}}`的人 (R); 1:2 R 作属于`{{deity_pronoun:他\|祂}}`的人, as Romans 1:7 |
| tongues; interpretation (12-14) | 说方言 at every level through 12, 13 and 14 (Mark 16:17; Acts 10:46; 19:6), since the English Readable keeps "tongues" here; 各种方言 (L), 各种不同的方言 (B, R) for "kinds of tongues". Interpretation 翻译 (L, B, R). "Languages" where the English says so (13:1; 14:10-11) and the Isaiah quotation of 14:21: 语言 (说别种语言的人 L, 说外族语言的人 B, 说其他语言的外族人 R; uncertain). The tongue as organ (14:9): 舌头 |
| raise (egeirō); resurrection (anastasis) | passive 被兴起 (L), as Matthew; active with God as subject 兴起 (L) (6:14; 15:15); 复活 / 使……复活 (B, R); the noun 复活 at every level. Readable often names God (`{{god_term:神\|上帝}}`使……复活) as the English Readable does |
| natural; spiritual (psychikos; pneumatikos) | 天然的人 (2:14) and 天然的身体 (15:44-46) (L, B); 只有天然生命的人 (B, 2:14); Readable as the English Readable (没有`{{god_term:神\|上帝}}`的灵的人; 具有普通人生命的身体). 属灵的 (L, B) for the person, the gifts and the body (2:13-15; 3:1; 12:1; 14:1, 37; 15:44-46); R 有圣灵的人, 由圣灵赐生命的身体. Not 属血气的, 属魂的, 属自然的 |
| fleshly (sarkikos, sarkinos, 3:1-3) | 属肉体的 (L); 只凭肉体的人 (B); 用人的方式思想的人 (R) |
| wisdom; foolishness | 智慧; 愚蠢 (every level, 1-4); not 愚拙 |
| members (melē: 6:15; 12:12-27) | 肢体 (L); 部分, 身体的一部分 (B, R). Not 成员 |
| build up (oikodomeō, 8:1; 14) | 造就 (L, B); 使……刚强, 成长得更坚强 (R) |
| sexual immorality (porneia, 5-7) | 淫乱 (L, B), as Acts 15:20; 性方面的罪 (R); adultery 奸淫 / 通奸 |
| yeast (5:6-8) | 酵 (L); 酵母 (B, R) |
| food offered to idols; idols; gods | 祭过偶像的食物; 偶像; 神明 for the gods, with no span (8:5; 10:19-20) |
| the Lord's Supper (11:20) | 主的晚餐; the bread and the cup 饼, 杯; "new covenant" 新约 (L, B) |
| firstfruits (15:20, 23; 16:15) | 初熟的果子 / 初熟果子 (L, B; 16:15 B 最先收成的果子); R as the English Readable |
| woe (9:16) | 有祸了 (L, B); 真是可悲 (R), as the Gospels |
| the opening greeting (1:3) | as Romans 1:7 and 2 Corinthians 1:2 at each level |
| the closing grace (16:23) | 主耶稣的恩典[与]你们[同在] (L); 愿主耶稣的恩典与你们同在 (B, R), the wish form of Romans 16:20 (the English Readable's deity_pronoun span has no counterpart) |
| holy kiss (16:20) | 圣洁的亲吻, as Romans 16:16 |
| 您 | not used: Paul writes to the church as their father in the faith |

### 2 Corinthians

2 Corinthians keeps Romans' forms for Paul's vocabulary and the Gospels' and
Acts' forms for every name: the grace-and-peace greeting of 1:2 word for word
as Romans 1:7 at each level; reconcile (5:18-20) as Romans 5:10-11; the
Jeremiah quotation of 10:17 at each level as 1 Corinthians 1:31 ("夸口的人，要在主里夸口");
Isaiah 49:8 in 6:2 and the chain of quotations in 6:16-18 with "我" for God
speaking, with no span. Names not in the tables above, as the text has them:

| English (first verse) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| Silvanus (1:19) | 西尔瓦努, kept apart from Silas 西拉 (Acts) because the English keeps the two forms apart, as Prisca 百基 / Priscilla 百基拉 (Romans 16:3); uncertain, for reviewers |
| Troas (2:12) | 特罗亚 (as Acts 16:8) |
| Beliar (6:15) | 彼列; Readable 魔鬼彼列 |
| Eve (11:3) | 夏娃 |
| Aretas (11:32) | 亚哩达; Damascus 大马士革 |

Terms settled in 2 Corinthians:

| English | Chinese |
|---|---|
| boast, boasting (kauchaomai, kauchēma, kauchēsis) | 夸口 at Literal throughout, as Romans and 1 Corinthians. Chapters 10-12, where the argument turns on boasting itself: 夸口 at every level. Elsewhere, proper pride in Paul's conduct or in the church (1:12-14; 5:12; 7:4, 14; 8:24; 9:2-3): 以……为荣 (B, R), 引以为荣 where the thing is not named. 感到自豪, 夸耀, 炫耀 are not used |
| ministry (diakonia) | 事奉 (L, B), as Romans 11:13, for the old covenant's and the new (3:7-9; 4:1; 5:18 "和好的事奉"; 6:3); 工作 or the verb 服事 (R). 职事 is not used |
| the collection (diakonia, 8:4; 9:1, 12-13) | 事奉 (L); 救济 (B); 帮助 (R). The gift itself: 这恩典 (L); 这份恩典的礼物 (B); 这份慷慨的礼物 / 这份礼物 (R) |
| servant (diakonos) | 仆人 (L, B), as Romans 13:4 and 15:8, for God's servants and Satan's alike (3:6 "新约的仆人"; 6:4; 11:15, 23); R as the English Readable, often the verb 服事. Not 执事 (the church office, Romans 16:1) |
| affliction (thlipsis); hardship | 患难 (L); 艰难 (B); 困难 (R), through 1:4-8; 2:4; 4:17; 6:4; 7:4-5; 8:2, 13 |
| grief, grieve (lypē, lypeō) | 忧愁 (L, B); 难过 (R), through 2:1-7; 6:10; 7:8-11; 9:7 (B 舍不得, regret over money given) |
| comfort (paraklēsis, parakaleō) | 安慰 at every level (1:3-7; 2:7; 7:4-13); 13:11 受安慰 (L), 受鼓励 (B), 鼓起勇气 (R) |
| according to the flesh (kata sarka: 1:17; 5:16; 10:2-3; 11:18) | 按着肉体 (L); 凭着纯粹属人的标准 (B); 按人的标准 (R). The adjective: 属肉体的智慧 (L), 纯粹属人的智慧 (B), 人的聪明 (R) (1:12). Romans' Readable 败坏的本性 is kept for the sinful nature and not used here |
| reconcile; ministry and word of reconciliation | 使……与……和好; 和好的事奉, 和好的道 (L, B); 带回到……自己身边 (R), as Romans 5:11 |
| new covenant; old covenant | 新约 (L, B), as Luke 22:20 and 1 Corinthians 11:25 and the style guide; 新的盟约 (R). 旧约 (L, B); 旧的盟约 (R) (3:14) |
| the letter and the Spirit (3:6) | 文字 (L); 写成的条文 (B); 写下来的规条 (R); 圣灵 at every level |
| the living God | 永活`{{god_term:神\|上帝}}` (3:3; 6:16), not 永生神 |
| the god of this age (4:4) | 这个时代的神明 (L, B); 统治现今世界的神明 (R), with no span |
| sufficient (hikanos, 2:16; 3:5-6) | 胜任, 足以胜任 (L, B); 有能力 (R) |
| ambassadors (5:20) | 使节 |
| the Lord Almighty (6:18) | 全能的主 (L, B); 统管万有的主 (R) |
| the God of love and peace (13:11) | 爱与平安的`{{god_term:神\|上帝}}` (L, B); 那爱你们、赐人平安的`{{god_term:神\|上帝}}` (R), after Romans' 平安的神 / 赐人平安的神 |
| the closing blessing (13:13) | 主耶稣`{{christos:基督\|弥赛亚}}`的恩典、`{{god_term:神\|上帝}}`的爱、圣灵的相通，[与]你们众人[同在] (L); 愿……的恩典、……的爱和圣灵的相交，与你们每一个人同在 (B); 圣灵所带来的相交 (R). Not 团契 or 交通 |
| holy kiss (13:12) | 圣洁的亲吻, as Romans 16:16 |
| 您 | not used: Paul writes to the church as equals and as their father in the faith |

### Galatians

Galatians keeps Romans' forms for Paul's vocabulary and the Gospels' and
Acts' forms for every name, and Romans' Chinese wherever the same Old
Testament text is quoted in the same Greek: Genesis 15:6 in 3:6 as Romans
4:3; Habakkuk 2:4 in 3:11 as Romans 1:17 (Literal without Romans' 却, which
answers a "de" Galatians does not have); Leviticus 18:5 in 3:12 close to
Romans 10:5, with 这些 for Paul's plural "them"; Leviticus 19:18 in 5:14 as
Romans 13:9. Isaiah 54:1 (4:27), Genesis 12:3 (3:8), Deuteronomy 27:26
(3:10), 21:23 (3:13) and Genesis 21:10 (4:30) are drafted here from the
Greek. Every name in the letter is already in the tables above (加拉太,
阿拉伯, 大马士革, 叙利亚, 基利家, 矶法, 雅各, 巴拿巴, 提多, 安提阿, 夏甲,
西奈山); Cephas is 矶法 at L and B and 彼得 at R, as the English Readable
says "Peter".

| English | Chinese |
|---|---|
| justify (dikaioō; 2:16-17; 3:8, 11, 24; 5:4) | 称义, 被称义 (L); 称为义, 被称为义, active 称……为义 (B); 被`{{god_term:神\|上帝}}`接纳为义人 (R), as Romans; no deity_pronoun span where the English Readable says "put right with himself" (3:8) |

| believe in (eis, 2:16) | 信入 (L), as Romans 10:14; 信 (B, R) |
| "the faith" as the message believed (1:23; 6:10) | 信仰 at every level (信心 stays for trust) |
| "the faith" as the new era (3:23-25) | 那信, 这信 (L); 信 (B); 信的时代 (R) |
| faithfulness in the fruit of the Spirit (5:22) | 忠信 (L, B); 忠诚 (R); 信实 stays for God's faithfulness |
| mē genoito (2:17; 3:21) | 但愿不会这样！ (L); 绝对不是！ (B); 当然不是！ (R), as Romans; with an infinitive (6:14) 但愿我绝不 (L), 我绝不会 (B, R) |
| flesh (Paul's moral sense, 5:13-24; 6:8) | 肉体 (L, B); 败坏的本性 (R), as Romans. Flesh as human effort (3:3): 自己人的努力 (R); as bodily life or the body (2:20; 4:13-14; 6:12-13): 这个人的身体, 生病, 身体 (R); by human means (4:23, 29): 按着肉体 (L, B), 按普通的方式 (R) |
| the Spirit (pneuma of God) | 圣灵 at every level; "the Spirit of his Son" (4:6) `{{deity_pronoun:他\|祂}}`儿子的灵 |
| Gentiles; Greek | 外邦人 (L, B), 非犹太人 (R); 希腊人 (L, B), 非犹太人 (R) at 3:28; the nations of the promise (3:8) 万国 |

| adoption (huiothesia, 4:5) | 收养……作`{{gender:儿女\|儿子}}`, as Romans 8:15 |

### Ephesians

Ephesians keeps the Gospels', Acts' and Romans' forms for every name and term
above, and their Chinese wherever the same Old Testament text is quoted in the
same Greek (Genesis 2:24 in 5:31 as Matthew 19:5 and Mark 10:7-8, with 1
Corinthians 6:16's "正如圣经所说" at R; the fifth commandment in 6:2 as Matthew
15:4). Psalm 68:19 (4:8), Psalm 4:5 (4:26) and Zechariah 8:16 (4:25) have no
earlier Chinese and are drafted from the Greek. The quotation of unknown source
in 5:14 is introduced with "有话说", not "经上说". The long sentences of 1:3-14,
1:15-21, 3:14-19 and 6:17-20 stay one sentence at L and are divided at B and R,
each verse's content after its own marker. 3:1 and 3:14 open with the same words
(因这缘故 L, B; 所以 R), since Paul breaks off and resumes. No new names: Tychicus
is 推基古, as the tables have him.

| servant of the gospel (diakonos, 3:7) | 仆人; 执事 is kept for the office |
| faith in him (3:12) | 对他的信心 (L); 信他 (B, R), as Romans 3:22 |
| breadth, length, height, depth (3:18) | 宽、长、高、深, in the Greek's order |
| inner person (3:16) | 里面的人 (L); 内在的生命 (B); 内心 (R), no gender span |
| doxology (3:21) | 直到所有的世代，永永远远 (L); 代代相传，直到永永远远 (B); 在每一个世代，直到永永远远 (R); 阿们 |
| prisoner in the Lord (3:1; 4:1) | 囚犯 |
| shepherds (poimenes, 4:11) | 牧人, not 牧师 |
| a mature man (aner teleios, 4:13) | 成熟的`{{gender:人\|男人}}` |
| the old self, the new self (4:22-24) | 旧我; 新我 (新人 kept for 2:15) |
| members of one another (4:25) | 彼此的肢体 (L, B), as Romans 12:5 |
| forgive (charizomai, 4:32) | 宽恕 (L, B); 原谅 (R); 赦免 stays for aphiemi (1:7) |
| fragrant aroma (5:2) | 馨香之气 (L); 馨香 (B); 香甜的气味 (R) |
| saints, holy ones | 圣徒 (L, B); 属于`{{god_term:神\|上帝}}`的人 (R) |
| be filled by the Spirit (5:18) | 被圣灵充满 (L); 让圣灵充满你们 (B); 让`{{god_term:神\|上帝}}`的灵充满你们 (R) |
| submit (hypotasso, 5:21-24) | 顺服 at every level (the verb supplied in 5:22 [要顺服] at L); *alt.* 服从, Romans 13:1's word for the authorities |
| obey (hypakouo, 6:1, 5) | 听从 at every level, kept apart from 顺服 |
| the fear of Christ (5:21); the wife's respect (5:33) | 敬畏; 敬重 (L), 尊重 (B, R), not 惧怕 |
| the church as "her" (5:25-27) | 她 |
| slaves; masters; the Master in heaven (6:5-9) | 奴隶 at every level (as the English); 主人 for the owners, 主 for Christ, keeping the play on one Greek word |
| fear and trembling (6:5) | 敬畏和战兢 (L, B); 非常尊敬，非常用心 (R) |
| training and admonition (6:4) | 管教和劝诫 (L); 培育和纠正 (B); 训练……纠正 (R) |
| the full armor of God (6:11, 13) | `{{god_term:神\|上帝}}`的全副盔甲 (L, B); `{{god_term:神\|上帝}}`赐给你们的全副盔甲 (R); not 全副军装 |
| belt, breastplate, shoes, shield, helmet, sword (6:14-17) | 腰带, 胸甲, 鞋子, 盾牌, 头盔, 剑; burning arrows 燃烧的箭 (L, B), 火箭 (R) |
| wrestling (pale, 6:12) | 摔跤 (L); 争战 (B); 打仗 (R) |
| the word of God (rhema, 6:17) | `{{god_term:神\|上帝}}`的话, kept apart from 道 for logos (1:13) |
| ambassador in a chain (6:20) | 戴着锁链的使节 |

### Philippians

Philippians keeps Romans' forms for Paul's vocabulary and the letter
formulas: the greeting (1:2) as Romans 1:7; "the God of peace" (4:9) as
Romans 15:33 (平安的`{{god_term:神|上帝}}`, R 赐人平安的`{{god_term:神|上帝}}`);
the doxology (4:20) with 直到永永远远 as Romans 16:27; the closing (4:23) as
Romans 16:20 ("with your spirit": 与你们的灵同在); the righteousness from God
through faith in Christ (3:9) as Romans 3:21-22 (对`{{christos:基督|弥赛亚}}`的信心
at L, 信`{{christos:基督|弥赛亚}}` at B and R). Isaiah 45:23 in 2:10-11 keeps
Romans 14:11's wording (每一个膝都要跪下 / 屈下; 每一个舌头都要宣认 at L,
公开承认 at B; R 开口说 as Romans 10:9). Names not in the tables above, as the
text has them:

| English (first verse) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| Epaphroditus (2:25; 4:18) | 以巴弗提, kept apart from Epaphras 以巴弗 |
| Euodia, Syntyche (4:2) | 友阿爹, 循都基 (two women) |
| Clement (4:3) | 革利免 |
| Philippians (4:15) | 腓立比人 |

Terms settled in Philippians:

| English | Chinese |
|---|---|
| overseers and deacons (1:1) | 监督 (as Acts 20:28) and 执事 (as Romans 16:1) at every level; R adds 照管教会的 / 服事教会的 |
| partnership in the gospel (koinōnia; 1:5; 4:15) | 作伙伴 (L), 同作福音的伙伴 (B); R 和我一起传扬好消息; 4:15 在付出和收受的账目上与我合伙 (L, B) |
| the praetorium (1:13) | 禁卫军 (L); 皇家卫队 (B); 皇帝卫队 (R) |
| rejoice, joy (chairō) | 喜乐 (L, B); 满心欢喜 (R, where the English Readable says "full of joy"; 1:18; 3:1; 4:4), 快乐 elsewhere at R |
| flesh as bodily life (1:22, 24) | 肉身 (L); 身体 (B); 在世上 / 活着 (R) |
| flesh as human credentials (3:3-4) | 肉体 (L); 人的资历 (B); 自己是谁或做过什么 (R), not Romans' 败坏的本性 |
| boast, boasting (kauchēma, kauchaomai; 1:26; 2:16; 3:3) | 夸耀 (L, B); 赞美 / 以……为荣 / 自豪 (R) |
| live as citizens; citizenship (1:27; 3:20) | 作公民生活 (L); 作公民，生活要…… (B, R); 公民身份 (L, B), 作为公民所属的国家 (R) |
| the hymn (2:6-11): morphē | 形态 at every level, for both 神的形态 and 奴隶 (L) / 仆人 (B, R) 的形态; 3:21 同形 (L), 同样的形态 (B) |
| harpagmos (2:6) | 可以利用的东西 (L); 为他自己谋利的东西 (B); 利用……为他自己谋好处 (R) |
| kenoō (2:7) | 倒空了`{{deity_pronoun:他\|祂}}`自己 at every level |
| "likeness of human beings" (2:7) | 人, no gender span (the English spans it) |
| empty glory (kenodoxia, 2:3) | 空虚的荣耀 (L, B); 让自己显得重要 (R) |
| work out your salvation (2:12) | 活出……的救恩，直到完成; "fear and trembling" 敬畏和颤抖 (L, B) |
| children of God (tekna, 2:15) | `{{god_term:神\|上帝}}`的儿女, no gender span |
| the word of life (2:16) | 生命的道 (L, B); 带来生命的信息 (R) |
| drink offering (2:17) | 奠祭 (L, B), as the glossary |
| apostolos of Epaphroditus (2:25) | 使者 (L); 代表 (B); not 使徒 |
| the mutilation (katatomē, 3:2) | 残割 (L); 残割身体的人 (B); spelled out at R |
| gains and loss (3:7-8) | 收益 / 有利 and 亏损 (L, B); 损失 (R) |
| dung (skybala, 3:8) | 粪便 (L, B); 不比粪便更好 (R) |
| lay hold / laid hold (3:12) | 抓住 (L); 握住 (B); 抓牢 (R), one verb repeated |
| the goal; the prize (3:14) | 目标 (L), 终点 (B), 终点线 (R); 奖赏 |
| "whose god is the belly" (3:19) | 神明, no god_term span |
| crown (stephanos, 4:1) | 冠冕 (L, B); 得胜的花冠 (R) |
| guard (phroureō, 4:7) | 守卫 (L); 站岗守护 (B); 看守 (R) |

| content (autarkēs, 4:11) | 知足 (L); 心满意足 (B); 感到满足 (R) |
| initiated (myeō, 4:12) | 被引入其中的奥秘 (L); 被引进了这个秘密 (B) |
| receipt wording (apechō, 4:18) | 全数收到 (L); 如数收到 (B); R 就把这当作我的收据吧 |
| Caesar's household (4:22) | 凯撒家 (L); 皇帝家 (B, R) |

### Colossians

Colossians keeps Romans' forms for Paul's vocabulary (flesh, Gentiles,
saints, the greeting and the closing) and the glossary's forms for every
name. Repeated Greek keeps one wording: 1:1 as 2 Corinthians 1:1; 4:18a as
1 Corinthians 16:21. Names not in the tables above, as the text has them:

| English (first verse) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| Laodicea (2:1; 4:13-16) | 老底嘉 (as Revelation 3:14) |
| Scythian; barbarian (3:11) | 西古提人; 野蛮人 (L, B), 荒蛮之地的人 / 外国人 (R) |
| Jesus called Justus (4:11) | 称为犹士都的耶稣 (uncertain; *alt.* the CUV's 耶数) |
| Mark the cousin of Barnabas (4:10) | 巴拿巴的表亲马可 (表亲: older or younger is not known) |
| Hierapolis (4:13) | 希拉波立 |
| Nympha (4:15) | 宁法 (a woman) |
| Archippus (4:17) | 亚基布 (also Philemon 2) |

| English | Chinese |
|---|---|

| firstborn (prōtotokos, 1:15) | 长子 at every level (as Romans 8:29), with what the rank means at B and R; firstborn from the dead (1:18) 从死人中[复生的]长子 (L), 从死人中第一个复生的 (B), 第一个从死里复活、永远不再死的 (R) |
| thrones, dominions, rulers, authorities (1:16) | 宝座、主权、统治者、掌权者 (L, B); 宝座和主宰，统治者和掌权者 (R). archai and exousiai 统治者, 掌权者 again in 2:10, 15 |
| all things (ta panta, 1:16-20) | 万物 (not the CUV's 万有) |
| hold together (1:17) | 在他里面得以维系 (L, B); 使万物维系在一起 (R) |
| the fullness (plērōma, 1:19; 2:9) | 丰满; deity (theotēs, 2:9) `{{god_term:神\|上帝}}`本质, so the span covers it (not 神性) |
| reconcile (1:20-22) | 和好 (L, B); 带回到……自己身边 (R), as 2 Corinthians 5:18 |
| mystery (1:26-27; 2:2; 4:3) | 奥秘 at every level, explained at R as `{{god_term:神\|上帝}}`的计划 |
| stewardship (oikonomia, 1:25) | 管家职分 (L); 任务 (B); 工作 (R) |
| ages and generations (1:26) | 众时代、众世代 (L) |

| the body of the flesh (2:11) | 肉体的身体 (L); 受肉体支配的旧生命 (B); 被罪控制的旧生命 (R). His physical body (1:22): 肉体的身体 (L), 肉身 (B), 人的身体 (R) |
| bond of debt (cheirographon, 2:14) | 手写债据 (L); 债据 (B); explained at R |
| new moon (2:16) | 月朔 (L, B); 每月初一的节期 (R) |
| shadow and body (2:17) | 影子; 身体 (L), 实体 (B, R) |
| disqualify (2:18) | 判……失格 (L); 判……失去奖赏 (B) |
| self-humbling (2:18, 23) | 谦卑 (L); 刻苦己身 (B) |
| the slogans (2:21) | 不要拿，不要尝，不要碰 (L, B); 不要拿这个，不要尝那个，连碰都不要碰 (R) |
| the old self, the new self (3:9-10) | 旧人, 新人 (L, B, as Romans 6:6); 旧的生命, 新的生命 (R) |
| put to death (3:5) | 处死 (L, B); 彻底杀死 (R) |
| forgive one another (charizomai, 3:13) | 饶恕 (L, B); 原谅 (R). 赦免 stays for the forgiveness of sins (1:14) |

| gender spans | none on 人 (1:28; 2:8, 22; 3:22-23), recorded in setting_alternative decisions; L 悖逆的`{{gender:儿女\|儿子}}` (3:6); `{{gender:弟兄姐妹\|弟兄}}` (1:2; 4:15 L, B) |

### 1 Thessalonians

1 Thessalonians keeps the Gospels', Acts' and Romans' forms for every name
and term above (the greeting and closing as Romans 1:7 and 16:20; "the God
of peace" as Romans 15:33; the holy kiss as 2 Corinthians 13:12; "sleep"
for death as 1 Corinthians 15:6, 18, 20; the trumpet as 1 Corinthians 15:52;
parousia as Matthew 24 and 1 Corinthians 15:23). Names not in the tables
above, as the text has them:

| English (first verse) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| Silvanus (1:1) | 西尔瓦努, as 2 Corinthians 1:19, kept apart from Silas 西拉 (Acts) because the English keeps the two forms apart |

Terms settled in 1 Thessalonians:

| English | Chinese |
|---|---|
| the coming (parousia; 2:19; 3:13; 4:15; 5:23) | 来临 (L, B); 回来 (R) |
| the day of the Lord (5:2) | 主的日子 (L, B, as 1 Corinthians 5:5); 主回来的那一天 (R) |
| those who have fallen asleep (4:13-15) | 那些睡着的人 (4:13 L, present participle), 那些已经睡去的人 (L, B); 那些已经死去的信徒 (R). Spiritual sleep (5:6-7) 睡觉 / 沉睡 / 睡着; "awake or asleep" (5:10) 醒着还是睡着 |
| the dead in Christ (4:16) | 那些在`{{christos:基督\|弥赛亚}}`里死了的人 (L); 那些属于`{{christos:基督\|弥赛亚}}`的死人 (B); 那些已经死去的信徒 (R) |
| caught up; to meet the Lord (4:17) | 被卷到云中 (L, B), 被带到云彩里 (R); 迎接主 (L), 迎见主 (B, R) |
| archangel; the trumpet of God (4:16) | 天使长; `{{god_term:神\|上帝}}`的号角 |
| his holy ones (3:13, Zechariah 14:5) | `{{deity_pronoun:他\|祂}}`所有的圣者 at every level (not 圣徒, which would choose believers over angels) |
| "himself" of God or the Lord (3:11; 4:9, 16; 5:23) | `{{deity_pronoun:他\|祂}}`自己, as Romans 8:16 |
| the word (of God, of the Lord) | 道 (L, B); 信息 (R), as Acts; in the contrast "the word of people / of God" (2:13) 话 |
| walk (peripateō, conduct) | 行事为人 (L); 生活 (B, R) |
| vessel (4:4) | 器皿 (L); 身体 (B, R) |
| a single fellow believer (4:6) | `{{gender:弟兄或姐妹\|弟兄}}` (L, B); 别的信徒 (R) |
| sons of light, sons of day (5:5) | 光的`{{gender:儿女\|儿子}}` (L); 光明的`{{gender:儿女\|儿子}}` (B); 属于光明 (R); 白昼的`{{gender:儿女\|儿子}}`. No classical 之子 |
| "Peace and security" (5:3) | “平安和安全” (L); “一切都很平安，很安全” (B); “一切都平平安安，很安全” (R) |
| breastplate; helmet (5:8) | 胸甲; 头盔 |
| the tempter (3:5) | 那试探人的 (L); 那试探者 (B); 撒但，就是那试探人的 (R) |
| the Jews of Judea (2:14) | 犹太人 (L); 犹太地的犹太人 (B); 在犹太那些反对信徒的犹太人 (R), per the meaning decision |

(Silvanus 西尔瓦努, parousia 来临 / 回来, the day of the Lord 主的日子 /
主回来的那一天, the greeting and closing). Terms settled in 2 Thessalonians:

| English | Chinese |
|---|---|
| the revelation of the Lord Jesus (apokalypsis, 1:7) | 显现 (L, B, as 1 Corinthians 1:7); 从天上来 (R) |

| the rebellion (apostasia, 2:3) | 那场背叛 (L); 那场大背叛 (B); 一场背叛`{{god_term:神\|上帝}}`的大叛乱 (R) |
| the man of lawlessness (2:3); the lawless one (2:8) | 那不法的人 (L, B); 那个违背`{{god_term:神\|上帝}}`一切律法的人 (2:3 R), 那个不法的人 (2:8 R). Not 大罪人 or 不法之人 |
| the son of destruction (2:3) | 那灭亡的儿子 (L); 注定要灭亡的那一位 (B); 他正走向灭亡 (R) |
| every so-called god (2:4); he displays himself as God | 一切被称为神明的 (no span); 显示 / 宣称自己就是`{{god_term:神\|上帝}}` |
| the temple of God (naos, 2:4) | `{{god_term:神\|上帝}}`的殿 (L); `{{god_term:神\|上帝}}`的圣殿 (B, R) |
| what restrains; the one who restrains (2:6-7) | 那约束[他]的 (L), 什么在约束他 (B, R); 那约束者 (L), 那现在约束它的 (B), 有一位在约束它 (R) |
| the mystery of lawlessness (2:7) | 那不法的奥秘 at every level |
| the appearing of his coming (epiphaneia, 2:8) | 来临时的显现 (L); 来临时的光辉 (B); 回来时的光辉 (R). The lawless one's parousia (2:9): 来临 (L), 来到 (B, R) |
| signs and wonders of falsehood (2:9) | 虚假的神迹奇事 (L); 虚假的神迹和奇事 (B) |
| firstfruits (2:13) | 初熟的果子 (L, as 1 Corinthians 15:20); 好像收成中最先收的果子 (B) |
| traditions (2:15; 3:6) | 传统 (L, B); 教导 (R) |
| disorderly (ataktōs, 3:6, 7, 11) | 不守规矩 (L); 生活散漫 / 懒散 (B); 不肯工作 / 懒惰 (R) |
| the evil one (3:3) | 那恶者 at every level |
| "If anyone will not work ..." (3:10) | “如果有人不愿意做工，那人也不可吃。” (L); “谁不愿意工作，谁就不该吃饭。” (B); “如果有人不想工作，就不应该吃东西。” (R) |
| the Lord of peace (3:16) | 平安的主 (L, B); 赐人平安的主 (R) |
| "with you all" (3:16, 18) | [与]你们众人[同在] (L); 与你们众人同在 (B); 与你们所有的人同在 (R), as Romans 15:33 |

### 1 Timothy

1 Timothy keeps the Gospels', Acts', Romans' and the Corinthian letters'
forms for every name and term above: overseer 监督 and deacon 执事 as Acts
20:28, Philippians 1:1 and Romans 16:1; diakonia 事奉 (1:12) and diakonos
仆人 where it is a servant of Christ (4:6); the living God 永活`{{god_term:神|上帝}}`
(3:15; 4:10) as 2 Corinthians; mediator 中保 (L) / 中间人 (B) as Galatians
3:19-20; ransom 赎价 as Mark 10:45; "I am telling the truth, I am not lying"
(2:7) as Romans 9:1; epiphaneia 显现 (6:14) as 2 Thessalonians 2:8.
Deuteronomy 25:4 in 5:18 is 1 Corinthians 9:9's Chinese at each level, and
"the worker deserves his wages" is Luke 10:7's; the two or three witnesses
(5:19) take Matthew 18:16's words (见证人 L, 证人 B, R). Names not in the
tables above, as the text has them:

| English (first verse) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| Hymenaeus (1:20) | 许米乃 (also 2 Timothy 2:17) |
| Alexander (1:20) | 亚历山大, as Acts 19:33 |
| Pontius Pilate (6:13) | 本丢彼拉多, as Luke 3:1 and Acts 4:27 |

Terms settled in 1 Timothy:

| English | Chinese |
|---|---|
| "Trustworthy is the saying" (1:15; 3:1; 4:9) | 这话是可信的 (L, B); 这话是真实的 (R), as 2 Timothy 2:11 and Titus 3:8; "worthy of full acceptance" 也配得人完全接受 (L), 值得人完全接受 (B) |
| the greeting (1:2) | 恩典、怜悯、平安从父`{{god_term:神\|上帝}}`和`{{christos:基督\|弥赛亚}}`耶稣我们的主归给你 (L); 愿你从……得着恩典、怜悯与平安 (B); 我祈求……向你施恩，怜悯你，赐给你平安 (R), as 2 Timothy 1:2. "God the Father" 父`{{god_term:神\|上帝}}`, never 神父 |
| the faith (as what is believed: 1:2, 19; 3:9; 4:1, 6; 5:8; 6:10, 12, 21) | 信仰, as Galatians 1:23; 信心 for trust (1:5, 14; 2:15; 4:12; 6:11) |
| godliness (eusebeia: 2:2; 3:16; 4:7-8; 6:3-11; the verb 5:4) | 敬虔 (L, B); R as the English Readable (尊敬`{{god_term:神\|上帝}}`, 过讨`{{god_term:神\|上帝}}`喜悦的生活, 为`{{god_term:神\|上帝}}`而活, 服事`{{god_term:神\|上帝}}`). theosebeia (2:10) 敬畏`{{god_term:神\|上帝}}` |
| sound teaching, words (1:10; 6:3) | 纯正的教导 / 话语 (L, B); 正确的教导 / 话 (R), as Titus 1:9 and 2 Timothy 4:3; the sick craving (6:4) 病态的 (L), 不健康的 (B) |
| a different doctrine (heterodidaskaleō, 1:3; 6:3) | 教导不同的教义 (L, B); 教导不真实的东西 (R). Not 异教, 异端 |
| myths; genealogies (1:4; 4:7) | 无稽的传说 (L), 荒诞的传说 (B), 编造的故事 (R), as 2 Timothy and Titus; not 神话. 无止境的家谱 (L), 没完没了的祖先名单 (B, R) |
| stewardship of God (oikonomia, 1:4) | 管家职分 (L, as Ephesians 3:2); 计划 (B); 工作 (R) |
| teachers of the law (1:7) | 律法教师, as Luke 5:17 |
| men who lie with males (1:10) | 与男人同寝的男人 (L); 与男人发生性关系的男人 (B, R); not 同性恋者 |
| the blessed God (makarios, 1:11; 6:15) | 当受称颂的`{{god_term:神\|上帝}}` / 掌权者 |
| child (teknon, 1:2, 18) | 孩子 (L); 儿子 (B, R) |
| patience (makrothymia, 1:16); endurance (hypomonē, 6:11) | 忍耐; 坚忍 |
| fight the good fight (strateia, 1:18) | 参与那美好的征战 (L); 打一场美好的战役 (B); 像一个好兵那样为主好好作战 (R) |
| the good contest of faith (agōn, 6:12) | 竞赛 (L, as 2 Timothy 4:7 L); 比赛 (B); 像赛跑的运动员 (R) |
| confession (homologia, 6:12-13) | 宣认 |
| King of the ages (1:17); King of kings and Lord of lords (6:15) | 万代的君王 (L), 历世历代的君王 (B), 永远掌权的君王 (R); 万王之王、万主之主 (L, B), 统管众王的王，统管众主的主 (R) |
| the men; the women (2:8-12) | 男人(们); 女人(们), with no gender span: gynē may be "wife" (meaning decision, footnoted); 妻子 not chosen |
| quietness (hēsychia, 2:2, 11-12) | 安静 at every level; not 沉默 or 沉静 |
| to have authority over (authenteō, 2:12) | 对男人掌权 (L); 对男人行使权柄 (B); 有权管他 (R). Not 辖管 or 压制; uncertain |
| saved through childbearing (2:15) | 借着生育得救 (L, B), 借着生儿育女而得救 (R); L and B keep 她……她们 |
| husband of one wife (3:2, 12); wife of one husband (5:9) | 一个妻子的丈夫 / 一个丈夫的妻子 (L); 忠于自己唯一的妻子 / 忠于自己的丈夫 (B); 只能有一个妻子 (R), 忠于她的丈夫 (R, 5:9), as the English levels; uncertain |
| "women" in 3:11 | 女人们 (L); 妇女们 (B, R). Not 女执事 or 执事的妻子; uncertain |
| serve (diakoneō, 3:10, 13) | 服事 |
| new convert (3:6); puffed up | 新近入教的人 (L), 刚信主不久的人 (B), 刚刚信主的人 (R); 自高自大 (L), 骄傲自大 (B), 骄傲 (R) |
| the devil; slanderers (3:6-7, 11) | 魔鬼; 毁谤人 / 说别人的坏话 |
| mystery (3:9, 16) | 奥秘; "the mystery of godliness" 敬虔的奥秘 (L), 我们信仰的奥秘 (B, R) |
| household of God (3:15) | `{{god_term:神\|上帝}}`的家 |
| vindicated in the Spirit (3:16) | 在圣灵里被证明为义 (L); 被圣灵证明为义 (B); R as the English Readable; uncertain |
| demons (4:1) | 鬼 (L); 邪灵 (B, R) |
| train; bodily training (4:7-8) | 锻炼 (L), 训练 (B, R); 锻炼身体 |
| council of elders (4:14) | 长老团 (L); 长老们 (B); 教会的领袖们 (R) |
| older man (5:1) | 年长的男人 (L, B), 老年男人 (R), not 长老; younger men and women as 兄弟, 姐妹 with no span |
| widows; truly widows; the list (5:3-16) | 寡妇; 真正是寡妇的 (L), 真正有需要的寡妇 (B), 没有别人照顾的寡妇 (R); 名册 (L), 名单 (B, R) |
| first pledge (5:12) | 起初的誓约 (L, B); 承诺 (R); not 信心 |
| elders (5:17-19) | 长老 at every level; double honor 双倍的敬重 (L, B) |
| "the Scripture says" (5:18) | 经上说 (L, B); 圣经上说 (R) |
| slaves; masters (6:1-2) | 奴隶 at every level; 主人 |
| man of God (6:11) | `{{god_term:神\|上帝}}`的人 (L, B); 属于`{{god_term:神\|上帝}}`的人 (R). Not 神人 |
| this present age (6:17) | 现今这个时代 (L); 今世 (B); 这世上 (R) |
| the deposit (6:20) | 所托付的 (L); 交托给你的 (B) |
| the closing (6:21) | 恩典[与]你们众人[同在] (L); 愿恩典与你们众人同在 (B); 愿`{{god_term:神\|上帝}}`的恩典与你们所有的人同在 (R) |
| 您 | not used: Paul writes to Timothy as his son |

### 2 Timothy

2 Timothy keeps Romans' forms for Paul's vocabulary and the Gospels' and
Acts' forms for every name: the opening of 1:1 as Colossians 1:1 at each
level; the greeting of 1:2 in Romans 1:7's form with "mercy" added
(恩典、怜悯、平安……归给你 L; 愿你……得着恩典、怜悯与平安 B; 我祈求……向你施恩，怜悯你，赐给你平安 R);
"deny" in 2:12 as Matthew 10:33 (不认 L, B; 说不认识 R); Numbers 16:5 in 2:19 with
"主" and no divine_name span; the drink offering of 4:6 as Philippians 2:17
(奠祭). Names not in the tables above, as the text has them:

| English (first verse) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| Lois; Eunice (1:5) | 罗以; 友尼基. mammē 祖母 (not 外祖母: the Greek does not say whose mother Lois was) |
| Phygelus, Hermogenes (1:15) | 腓吉路, 黑摩其尼 |
| Onesiphorus (1:16; 4:19) | 阿尼色弗, kept apart from Onesimus 阿尼西母 |
| Hymenaeus, Philetus (2:17) | 许米乃 (also 1 Timothy 1:20), 腓理徒 |
| Jannes, Jambres (3:8) | 雅尼, 佯庇 |
| Antioch, Iconium, Lystra (3:11) | 安提阿, 以哥念, 路司得 (as Acts) |
| Crescens; Dalmatia (4:10) | 革勒士; 达尔马提亚 (the modern standard form; uncertain, alt. 挞马太) |
| Carpus (4:13) | 加布 |
| Alexander the coppersmith (4:14) | 铜匠亚历山大 (L), 金属匠亚历山大 (B) |
| Prisca, Aquila (4:19) | 百基, 亚居拉 (as Romans 16:3) |
| Erastus, Trophimus (4:20) | 以拉都, 特罗非摩 (as Acts) |
| Eubulus, Pudens, Linus, Claudia (4:21) | 友布罗, 布田, 利奴, 革老底亚 |

Terms settled in 2 Timothy (and Titus):

| English | Chinese |
|---|---|
| "Trustworthy is the saying" (2:11; Titus 3:8) | 这话是可信的 (L, B, also where the English Balanced says "a saying you can rely on"); 这话是真实的 / 这些话是真实的 (R) |
| sound teaching, sound words (hygiainō: 1:13; 4:3; Titus 1:9, 13; 2:1-2) | 纯正的教导 (L, B), 纯正言语 (1:13 L); 正确的教导 (R); "sound in the faith" 在信心上纯正 (L, B) |
| godliness (eusebeia: 3:5; Titus 1:1; 2:12) | 敬虔 (L, B, and R where natural); R explains it as the English Readable (为神而活) |
| theopneustos (3:16) | {{god_term:神\|上帝}}所呼出的 (L, B); 从{{god_term:神\|上帝}}的气息而来的 (R); footnoted decision. 默示 is not used: it loses the picture of breath. "All Scripture" 一切经文 (L), 全部圣经 (B, R) |
| the man of God (3:17) | {{god_term:神\|上帝}}的人 (L); 属于{{god_term:神\|上帝}}的人 (B, R). 神人 is not used (does not read with 上帝) |
| the sacred writings (3:15) | 神圣的经书 (L, as Romans 1:2); 圣经 (B, R) |
| the deposit (parathēkē: 1:12, 14) | 我所存放的 / 那美好的托付 (L); 交托给……的 (B, R) |
| appearing (epiphaneia: 1:10; 4:1, 8; Titus 2:13) | 显现, kept apart from parousia 来临 |
| myths (4:4; Titus 1:14) | 无稽的传说 (L); 荒诞的传说 (B); 编造的故事 (R). 神话 is not used (its 神 would read as God beside the god_term setting) |
| the good fight (4:7) | 那美好的竞赛 (agōn as the athlete's contest; not 仗) |
| herald (kēryx: 1:11) | 宣讲者 (传道者 is kept for Qoheleth) |
| ministry (diakonia, 4:5, 11) | 事奉 (L, B), as Romans and 2 Corinthians; 工作 (R) |
| makrothymia; hypomonē (3:10; Titus 2:2) | 忍耐; 坚忍 |
| the Lord's slave (2:24); slave of God (Titus 1:1) | 奴隶 (L); 仆人 (B, R) |
| child / son (teknon, 1:2; 2:1; Titus 1:4) | 孩子 (L); 儿子 (B, R) |
| closing "with you all" (4:22; Titus 3:15) | [与]你们[众人][同在] (L); 愿恩典与你们众人同在 (B); 我祈求{{god_term:神\|上帝}}向你们所有的人施恩 (R, a prayer as the English Readable) |
| 您 | not used: Paul writes to Timothy and Titus as his sons in the faith |

### Titus

Titus keeps 2 Timothy's forms above for the shared phrases (the saying,
sound teaching, godliness, appearing, myths) and Romans' for justify
(3:7 被称义 L, 被称为义 B, 接纳……成为义人 R). The greeting of 1:4 in Romans 1:7's
form to one person (归给你). Names not in the tables above:

| English (first verse) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| Crete; Cretans (1:5, 12) | 克里特 (as Acts 27); 克里特人; R 克里特岛 |
| Artemas (3:12) | 亚提马 |
| Nicopolis (3:12) | 尼哥波立 |
| Zenas the lawyer (3:13) | 律师西纳 |
| Apollos, Tychicus | 亚波罗, 推基古 (as Acts) |

Terms settled in Titus:

| English | Chinese |
|---|---|
| elders (1:5) | 长老 (L, B); 教会的领袖 (R), as Acts 11:30 |
| overseer (episkopos, 1:7) | 监督 (L, B), as Acts 20:28; 教会的领袖 (R), the same office as the elders. Not 主教 |
| God our Savior; our great God and Savior Jesus Christ (2:13) | {{god_term:神\|上帝}}我们的救主; 我们伟大的{{god_term:神\|上帝}}和救主耶稣{{christos:基督\|弥赛亚}} (one person, with the god_term span) |
| those of the circumcision (1:10) | 受割礼[一派]的人 (L); 受割礼派的人 (B); R as the English Readable |
| the washing of regeneration (3:5) | 重生的洗涤 (L); 新生的洗 (B); R as the English Readable |
| heirs (3:7) | 后嗣 (L, B) |
| slaves and masters (2:9) | 奴隶 at every level (slavery itself is the point); 主人 |
| pistis as trustworthiness (2:10) | 忠诚 (L); 可靠 (B); 可以信赖 (R), not 信心 |
| unspanned English "He" for God or Christ (2:13-14 R; 3:5 B, R) | the Chinese names God (神) or Jesus (耶稣, 这一位), so every pronoun for God or Christ is in a deity_pronoun span |

### Philemon

Philemon keeps Colossians 4's forms for every name (Onesimus 阿尼西母,
Archippus 亚基布, Epaphras 以巴弗, Mark 马可, Aristarchus 亚里达古, Demas 底马,
Luke 路加), Philemon 腓利门, and "fellow soldier" 战友 as Philippians 2:25.
The greeting of verse 3 as Romans 1:7 (L, B), Readable as 2 Thessalonians 1:2
with 我; the closing of verse 25 as Romans 16:20, with 你们 plural. Names not
in the tables above:

| English (first verse) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| Apphia (2) | 亚腓亚 (a woman; uncertain) |

Terms settled in Philemon:

| English | Chinese |
|---|---|
| the play on Onesimus's name (11) | 无用 / 有用 (L); 没有用处 / 有用处 (B); R explains: 他的名字意思是“有用”, as the English Readable. Verse 20 (onaimēn) 得些益处 (L), 得着这份益处 (B); the play is not marked |
| slave (16) | 奴隶 at every level |
| in the flesh and in the Lord (16) | 在肉体上 (L); 在日常生活中 (B); 作为你家里的人 (R); not Romans' 败坏的本性 |
| the hearts (splanchna, 7, 12, 20) | 心 (心……得了舒畅); "my very heart" 我自己的心 |
| charge it to my account (18) | 记在我的账上 (L, R); 算在我的账上 (B) |
| I am sending him back (12, epistolary aorist) | 我已经把他送回给你 (L); 我现在打发他回到你那里去 (B, R), as Colossians 4:8 |
| 您 | not used: Paul writes to Philemon as a brother and partner |

### Hebrews

Hebrews keeps the Gospels', Acts' and Paul's forms for every name and term
above, and their Chinese wherever the same Old Testament text is quoted in
the same Greek: Psalm 2:7 in 1:5 and 5:5 as Acts 13:33; Psalm 110:1 in 1:13
as Luke 20:42-43 and Acts 2:34-35 (10:12-13 the same, turned to the third
person); Deuteronomy 32:35 in 10:30 as Romans 12:19 (without "says the
Lord"); Genesis 21:12 in 11:18 as Romans 9:7; Habakkuk 2:4 in 10:38 as
Romans 1:17, with "我的义人" for the Greek's "my righteous one". Repeated
quotations inside Hebrews keep one wording per level: Psalm 95:7-11 (3:7-11;
3:15; 4:3, 5, 7); Psalm 110:4 (5:6; 7:17, 21); Jeremiah 31:31-34 (8:8-12;
10:16-17, changing only where the Greek does); Psalm 40:6-8 (10:5-7 and
10:8-9). God speaking says "我" with no span, though the English spans "My";
the Son addressed takes `{{deity_pronoun:你|祢}}` (1:5-13; 5:5-6), and
"O God" said to the Son (1:8) is `{{god_term:神|上帝}}啊`. kyrios in the
quotations is 主, with no divine_name span (1:10; 7:21; 8:8-11; 12:5).
Names, as the text has them:

| English (first verse) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| Melchizedek; Salem (7:1) | 麦基洗德; 撒冷 |
| Abel; Enoch; Noah (11:4-7) | 亚伯; 以诺; 挪亚 |
| Sarah; Isaac; Jacob; Esau (11:11-20) | 撒拉; 以撒; 雅各; 以扫 |
| Gideon, Barak, Samson, Jephthah, Samuel (11:32) | as the Old Testament tables |
| Rahab (11:31) | 喇合 (as Matthew 1:5) |
| Mount Sinai; Mount Zion (12:18-22) | 西奈山; 锡安山 |
| Timothy (13:23) | 提摩太 |

Terms settled in Hebrews:

| English | Chinese |
|---|---|
| high priest | 大祭司, every level |
| according to the order of Melchizedek (Psalm 110:4: 5:6, 10; 6:20; 7:11, 17, 21) | 按着麦基洗德的品级 (L); 属麦基洗德的品级 (B); 像麦基洗德那样的祭司 (R). The quotation: `{{deity_pronoun:你\|祢}}`是永远的祭司，按着麦基洗德的品级 (L); `{{deity_pronoun:你\|祢}}`永远是祭司，属麦基洗德的品级 (B); `{{deity_pronoun:你\|祢}}`永远是祭司，是像麦基洗德那样的祭司 (R). Aaron's order (7:11) the same pattern. 等次 is not used |
| perfect, make perfect (teleioō, teleiōsis) | 使……成为完全 / 成为完全, noun 完全 (L, B), for Jesus and for people alike (2:10; 5:9; 7:11, 19, 28; 9:9; 10:1, 14; 11:40; 12:23); at R 完全合格 of Jesus (fit for his office), 变得完全 of people and things. 得以完全 is not used. Maturity (teleios, teleiotēs, 5:14; 6:1): 完全 (L), 成熟 (B), 成年人 / 长大成熟 (R). The perfecter of faith (12:2): 成全者 (L) |
| rest (katapausis, 3:11-4:11) | 安息, every level; sabbatismos (4:9) 安息日的安息 (L, B), 像安息日那样的安息 (R) |
| swear; oath (omnyō; horkos, horkōmosia) | 起誓; 誓言 (3:11, 18; 4:3; 6:13-18; 7:20-28). 发誓 is not used |
| covenant (diathēkē) | 约 (L, B); 盟约 (R). New covenant 新约 (L, B), 新的盟约 (R) (8:8, 13; 9:15; 12:24), as 1 and 2 Corinthians. The first covenant 第一个约 / 第一个盟约. The ark and tablets 约柜, 约版 (L, B), 刻着盟约的石版 (R). A will (9:16-17) 遗嘱. The eternal covenant (13:20) 永远之约 (L, B), 永远的盟约 (R) |
| mediator (mesitēs: 8:6; 9:15; 12:24) | 中保 (L, B); R says what he does (带来新的盟约) |
| guarantor (engyos, 7:22) | 保证人 (L); 作保证, 保证 (B, R); kept apart from 中保 |
| heavenly (epouranios) | 属天的 (L, every occurrence: 3:1; 6:4; 8:5; 9:23; 11:16; 12:22); B and R 从天上来的 (a calling, a gift) and 天上的 (things, homeland, Jerusalem) |
| once for all (ephapax: 7:27; 9:12; 10:10) | 一次永远地 (L, B); 只……一次，而这一次永远有效 (R) |
| curtain (katapetasma: 6:19; 9:3; 10:20) | 帘幕, every level. 幔子 is not used |
| the mercy seat (hilastērion, 9:5) | 赎罪盖, every level, the glossary's word for the ark's lid; R explains 约柜的盖子. Romans 3:25 has the same Greek word for Christ as 赎罪的途径 (L, B) |
| the Most Holy Place | 至圣所 (L too, for "Holy of Holies"); the holy place, sanctuary 圣所; tabernacle, tent 帐幕 at every level |

| boldness, confidence (parrēsia: 3:6; 4:16; 10:19, 35) | 胆量 (L); 勇气 (B, R). 信心 is kept for faith; 确信 for hypostasis / plērophoria (3:14; 6:11; 10:22; 11:1) |
| approach, draw near (proserchomai: 4:16; 7:25; 10:1, 22; 11:6; 12:18, 22) | 来到……面前 (L, B); 走近 (R). engizō (7:19) 亲近 (L), 走近 (B, R) |
| the Majesty (1:3; 8:1) | 至尊者 (高处至尊者; 天上至尊者宝座) (L, B); R as the English Readable |
| the living God (3:12; 9:14; 10:31; 12:22) | 永活的`{{god_term:神\|上帝}}`; 永活`{{god_term:神\|上帝}}` where another 的 follows (3:12 R), as 2 Corinthians 3:3. 永生神 is not used |
| better (kreittōn, 1:4 to 12:24) | 更好 (L, B), every occurrence, so the book's key word can be followed; R as the English Readable (伟大, 好得多) |
| pioneer (archēgos: 2:10; 12:2) | 开路者 |
| son of man in Psalm 8 (2:6) | 人子 (L, the Greek's two words, not the title); `{{gender:凡人\|人的儿子}}` (B), `{{gender:渺小的凡人\|人的儿子}}` (R): both options mean a human being, so the gender setting does not turn the line into Jesus' title |
| sons; a son (huioi, huios, 2:10; 12:5-8) | `{{gender:儿女\|儿子}}` where the English has a span; one child `{{gender:孩子\|儿子}}` (12:7, L, B); 我儿 (L, B), 我的孩子 (R) for Proverbs 3:11 |
| the sons of Israel (11:22) | `{{gender:以色列的子孙\|以色列的儿子}}` (L); 以色列人 (B, R) |
| a fellow member of the people (8:11) | `{{gender:弟兄或姐妹\|弟兄}}` (L, B), as Matthew |
| brothers and sisters; beloved (6:9) | `{{gender:弟兄姐妹\|弟兄}}`们; 亲爱的 (L), 亲爱的朋友们 (B, R) |
| hyssop (9:19) | 牛膝草, as John 19:29 |
| the festal gathering; the assembly of the firstborn (12:22-23) | 欢庆的聚会 (L, B); 众长子的会众 (L, B), not 教会 |
| 您 | not used: the author writes to the church as a fellow believer |

### James

James keeps Romans' forms for the vocabulary it shares with Paul and the
Gospels' and Acts' forms for every name. Faith and works (2:14-26) is worded
so it can be set beside Romans 3-4: justify 被称义 (L), 被称为义 (B),
被`{{god_term:神|上帝}}`接纳为义人 (R, where the English Readable says "counted
righteous"); works 行为 (L, B). Genesis 15:6 in 2:23 word for word as Romans
4:3 and Galatians 3:6 at each level; Leviticus 19:18 in 2:8 as Romans 13:9;
the commandments of 2:11 as Romans 13:9, with Literal's 你 left out because
James has the prohibition form ("不可通奸", "不可杀人"); the Lord of Sabaoth
(5:4) as Romans 9:29 (主撒巴俄 L, 万军之主 B, 统领天上万军的主 R); Jesus'
saying on oaths (5:12) as Matthew 5:34-37 where the Greek is the same, with
Matthew 5:37's Balanced "你们说是，就要真是；说不，就要真不"; moth and rust
(5:2-3) as Matthew 6:19-20 (蛀虫, 锈蚀 / 锈); the peacemakers (3:18) as
Matthew 5:9. Names not in the tables above, as the text has them:

| English (first verse) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| James (1:1) | 雅各, the same as Jacob, as the glossary has it |
| Rahab (2:25) | 喇合 |
| Job (5:11) | 约伯 |
| Elijah (5:17) | 以利亚 |

Terms settled in James:

| English | Chinese |
|---|---|
| the letter greeting (chairein, 1:1) | 问安 (L, as Acts 23:26); 愿你们安好 (B); 向你们问好！ (R) |
| the twelve tribes in the dispersion (1:1) | 在分散中的十二个支派 (L); 分散在各国的十二个支派 (B); R as the English Readable |
| trials; tempt, temptation (peirasmos, peirazō) | 试炼 for the trials that test faith (1:2, 12); 试探 for the pull toward sin from 1:13 on |
| endurance, endure (hypomonē, hypomenō: 1:3-4, 12; 5:11) | 坚忍, 坚忍到底 (L, B), as Romans 5:3; 坚持下去 (R) |
| patience, be patient (makrothymia: 5:7-10) | 忍耐 (L); 耐心 (B, R). Kept apart from 坚忍 |
| perfect (teleios) | 完全 (L, and 3:2 L, B); 成熟 / 长大成熟 for people (1:4 B, R) |
| double-minded (dipsychos: 1:8; 4:8) | 怀二心的人 (L, B); R as the English Readable (心里被两边拉扯着; 在神和世界之间摇摆的人) |
| doubt, waver (diakrinomai, 1:6) | 疑惑 (L); 摇摆不定 (B); 怀疑 (R) |

| blessed (makarios: 1:12, 25; 5:11) | 有福, as Matthew 5 |
| the word (logos: 1:18, 21-23) | 道 (L, B); 信息 (R), as Acts; the word of truth 真理的道 |

### 1 Peter

1 Peter keeps Romans' and Paul's letters' forms for shared vocabulary and the Gospels' and Acts' forms for every name. Isaiah 28:16 (2:6) is worded as Romans 9:33 / 10:11, Psalm 118:22 (2:7) as Matthew 21:42, Isaiah 8:14 (2:8) as Romans 9:33, and Proverbs 3:34 (5:5) as James 4:6, wherever the Greek is the same. Isaiah 53:9, 4-6 (2:22-25) is not the passage Acts 8:32-33 quotes (53:7-8), so it is drafted afresh. Psalm 34:12-16 (3:10-12) and Isaiah 40:6-8 (1:24-25) are drafted afresh, with "主" for kyrios (no divine_name span). Names not in the tables above, as the text has them:

| English (first verse) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| Pontus, Galatia, Cappadocia, Asia, Bithynia (1:1) | 本都, 加拉太, 加帕多家, 亚细亚, 庇推尼 (as Acts) |
| Sarah, Abraham (3:6) | 撒拉, 亚伯拉罕 |
| Noah (3:20) | 挪亚 |
| Silvanus (5:12) | 西尔瓦努 (as 2 Corinthians 1:19), kept apart from Silas 西拉; uncertain |
| Babylon; Mark (5:13) | 巴比伦; 马可 |

| grace and peace be multiplied (1:2) | 愿恩典与平安在你们身上不断增多 (L); 愿你们得着越来越多的恩典与平安 (B); 愿神赐给你们越来越多的恩典和平安 (R). Also 2 Peter 1:2, Jude 2 |

2 Peter keeps 1 Peter's forms (beloved, the greeting, slave/servant, 敬虔). The transfiguration voice (1:17) is worded as Matthew 17:5 at B and R, Peter's word order at L. 2 Peter 2 and Jude share wording where the Greek is the same (marked "= Jude"). Names not in the tables above:

| Noah, Sodom, Gomorrah, Lot (2:5-7) | 挪亚, 所多玛, 蛾摩拉, 罗得 |
| Balaam son of Bosor (2:15) | 巴兰; 波索珥 (a new transliteration of SBLGNT's Bosor, kept apart from Numbers' Beor 比珥); uncertain |
| Paul (3:15) | 保罗 |

### 1 John

1 John keeps John's Gospel's forms for every key term (道, 永生, 留在……里面,
世界, 信入 / 信, 光 / 黑暗, 真理, 辩护者) and its Chinese where 1 John echoes
the Gospel in the same Greek: "passed from death to life" (3:14) as John 5:24
at each level; "no one has ever seen God" (4:12) as John 1:18; "his one and
only Son" (4:9) as John 3:16; "take away sins" (3:5) with John 1:29's 除去;
"lay down his life" (3:16) as John 10:11 (舍弃 L, B; 献出 R); "the Spirit of
truth" (4:6) as John 14:17; "the evil one" as John 17:15; "come in the flesh"
(4:2) with John 1:14's 肉身, and at R 成为一个真正的人. Names not in the
tables above, as the text has them:

| English (first verse) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| Cain (3:12) | 该隐; Abel (3:12 R) 亚伯, "his brother" 弟弟 (the younger) |

| children (paidia: 2:14, 18) | 孩子们 at every level |
