| righteous (dikaios, of God and of Jesus: 1:9; 2:1, 29; 3:7) | 公义的 at every level; "the Righteous One" 那公义的[一位] (L); not 义者. Doing righteousness 行义 (L, B), 做正确的事 / 过公义生活 (R) |
| unrighteousness (adikia, 1:9; 5:17) | 不义 (L); 错事 (B, R) |
| forgive | 赦免 (L, B); 饶恕 (R) |
| liar | 说谎者 (as John 8:44); "make him a liar" 使祂成为说谎者 (L), 说祂是说谎者 (B, R) |
| advocate (paraklētos, 2:1) | 辩护者 at every level (R adds 在父面前为我们说话), since the sense is a court advocate; John 14-16 has 帮助者 at R for the Spirit |
| atoning sacrifice (hilasmos, 2:2; 4:10) | 赎罪的祭物 (L, B), sharing 赎罪 with Romans 3:25's 赎罪的途径 (hilastērion); R spells it out (除去我们的罪、使我们与神和好的祭物 2:2; 作祭物，除去我们的罪 4:10). Not 挽回祭, 赎罪祭. Uncertain; note Romans 8:3 also has 赎罪的祭物 (peri hamartias) |
| command (entolē) | 命令 at every level (not 诫命), as John 13:34; new command 新命令 |
| keep (tēreō) commands, word | 持守 (L, as John 15:10); 遵守 (B); 听从 (R). "Do" (poieō) his commands (5:2) 遵行 (L, B) |
| remain (menō) | 留在……里面 at every level; "remains forever" (2:17) 永远存留; "would have remained with us" (2:19) 留下来 |
| perfected, complete (teleioō, teleios: 2:5; 4:12, 17-18) | 成全, 已被成全, 成全的爱 (L); 达到目标 / 达到完满, 完满的爱 (B); 完全发挥作用, 完全的爱 (R) |
| boldness (parrēsia: 2:28; 3:21; 4:17; 5:14) | 胆量 (L); B and R by context (勇敢地面对, 放胆来到神面前, 信心; R 不必害怕, 有把握). Not 坦然无惧 |
| born of God | 由神所生 (L, B; also R at 2:29; 3:9; 5:1); R often 神的儿女 (4:7; 5:4, 18). Not 从神生的, 由神而生 |
| children of God (tekna theou) | `{{god_term:神\|上帝}}`的儿女, no gender span |
| from God; of the world (of people: 4:4-6; 5:19) | 出于神 / 出于世界 (L); 属于神 / 属于世界 (B, R) |
| antichrist (2:18, 22; 4:3) | 敌基督 at every level, no christos span (as the English); R explains 与`{{christos:基督\|弥赛亚}}`为敌的那一位 |
| the last hour (2:18) | 末后的时刻 (L, B); 末后的时候 (R) |
| anointing (chrisma, 2:20, 27) | 膏抹 (noun at L; verb at B, R); not 恩膏; not called 它 (the Spirit) |
| the evil one; the devil | 那恶者 (R adds 就是魔鬼); 魔鬼 |
| overcome (nikaō) | 胜过 (L, B); 打败 (B, 2:13-14, "defeated"); 战胜 (R) |
| lawlessness (3:4) | 不法 (L, B); 反抗神的律法 (R) |
| doing sin, sinning (present tense, 3:4-9) | 行罪 (L, as John 8:34); 继续犯罪 (B); 一直犯罪 (R) |
| murderer (3:15) | 杀人凶手 (as John 8:44); R 心里就是杀人凶手 |
| one fellow believer (adelphos) | `{{gender:弟兄或姐妹\|弟兄}}` (L, B); 主内的弟兄姐妹, no span (R, where the English Readable says "a fellow believer"); plural `{{gender:弟兄姐妹\|弟兄}}`(们) |
| the true one; the true God (5:20); the true light (2:8) | 那真实的[一位] (L), 那位真实的 (B), 那真实的神 (R); 真实的神; 真实的光. Not 真神, 真光. "This one" (5:20b) 这一位 |
| sin (not) leading to death (5:16-17) | 通向死亡的罪 / 不通向死亡的罪 (L); 会导致死亡的罪 / 不会导致死亡的罪 (B, R). Not 至于死. Ask (aiteō) 祈求, R 祷告; request (erōtaō) 请求 |
| understanding (dianoia, 5:20) | 领悟力 (L, B); 帮助我们明白 (R) |
| idols; false gods (5:21) | 偶像; 假神, no span |
| 您 | not used: the writer speaks as a father in the faith; generic "you" at R (5:10) 你 |

### 2 John

2 John keeps 1 John's forms: 长老, 真理, 留在……里面, 命令, 新命令 (5, as 1 John
2:7-8), 在真理中行 (L) / 按照真理生活 (B, R), 敌基督 (7, R explains it with
the christos span), "come in the flesh" (7) at B and R in 1 John 4:2's words
(L 在肉身中来临 for the present participle), 父 and 子 / 儿子 (9), joy made
complete (12) as 1 John 1:4 (得以完满 L; 完满 B; 满心喜悦 R). No new names.

| English | Chinese |
|---|---|
| the elder (1) | 长老 at every level |
| the chosen lady (1, 5); your chosen sister (13) | 蒙拣选的夫人 (L, B), 神所拣选的夫人 (R); "dear lady" (5 R) 亲爱的夫人; 你那蒙拣选的姐妹. Uncertain: 夫人 keeps open whether a woman or a church is meant |
| her / your children | 儿女 |
| grace, mercy, peace will be with us (3) | 恩典、怜悯、平安会从父`{{god_term:神\|上帝}}`、也从父的儿子耶稣`{{christos:基督\|弥赛亚}}`，在真理和爱中与我们同在 (L); B and R the same promise form (会……与我们同在), not Paul's wish form |
| deceiver(s) (planos, 7) | 迷惑人的 (L, B); 使人走迷路的人 (R), keeping 那迷惑人的 for the title |
| goes ahead (9) | 跑到前面去 (L); 走得太远 (B); 越过 (R) |
| "Greetings" (chairein, 10-11) | “愿你安好” (L, in quotation marks as the English Literal); 问候 (B); 用问候来欢迎 / 欢迎 (R) |
| mouth to mouth (12) | 口对口 (L); 面对面 (B); 当面 (R) |
| greet (13) | 问候 (L, B); 向……问好 (R), as Romans 16 |
| 你 / 您 | 你 for the lady (singular in 4-5, 13), 你们 for the church (6, 8, 10, 12); 您 not used |

### 3 John

3 John keeps 1 and 2 John's forms (长老, 真理, 在真理中行 / 照着真理生活, 见证,
"mouth to mouth" as 2 John 12, greetings 问候 / 问好). "Our testimony is true"
(12) keeps John 19:35's 见证是真实的 (L, B). Names not in the tables above, as
the text has them:

| English (first verse) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| Gaius (1) | 该犹 (as Acts 19:29; Romans 16:23) |
| Diotrephes (9) | 丢特腓 (new to the glossary; uncertain) |
| Demetrius (12) | 低米丢 (as the man of Acts 19:24: the same Greek name) |

Terms settled in 3 John:

| English | Chinese |
|---|---|
| beloved (agapētos, 1, 2, 5, 11) | 蒙爱的 / 蒙爱的人哪 (L); 我亲爱的朋友 / 亲爱的朋友 (B, R) |
| it goes well; be in good health (2) | 一切顺利, 身体健康 (L); 都好, 安好 (B, R); not 兴盛 (wealth) |
| rejoice (3); joy (4) | 欢喜 (L), 高兴 (B), 快乐 (R); 喜乐 |
| my children (4) | 我的孩子们 |
| send them forward (6) | 送行 (L); 供应他们，帮助他们踏上下一段旅程 (B); 供给他们旅途余下所需要的一切 (R) |
| the Name (7) | 那名 (L, B); 耶稣的名 (R) |
| the Gentiles (ethnikoi, 7) | 外邦人 (L); 异教徒 (B); 不信主的人 (R): unbelievers, so not Romans' Readable 非犹太人 |
| support (hypolambanō, 8); fellow workers | 支持 (L); 接待并供应 (B); 欢迎并帮助 (R); 同工 |
| who loves to be first (9) | 喜爱居首位的 (L); 喜欢占首位的 (B); 喜欢……当最重要的人 (R) |
| brothers (3, 5, 10) | `{{gender:弟兄姐妹\|弟兄}}`们 (L, B); 信徒, 旅行的信徒 (R) |
| imitate (11) | 模仿 (L); 学 (B); 以……为榜样 (R) |
| Peace to you (15) | 平安[归给]你 (L); 愿平安与你同在 (B); 愿`{{god_term:神\|上帝}}`赐给你平安 (R) |
| by name (15) | 按着名字 (L); 按着名字一一 (B); 一个一个地 (R) |
| 您 | not used: the elder writes to Gaius as a father to his child |

### Revelation

Revelation keeps the Gospels', Acts' and the letters' forms for every name
and term above. Repeated formulas inside the book keep one wording per
level: the call that closes each letter (2:7, 11, 17, 29; 3:6, 13, 22) and
13:9; the promise to the one who overcomes; the angel's refusal of worship
(19:10 and 22:9); the three laments over Babylon (18:10, 16, 19). Names not
in the tables above, as the text has them:

| English (first verse) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| Patmos (1:9); Asia (1:4) | 拔摩; 亚细亚 (亚细亚省 at B, R) |
| the seven churches (1:11) | 以弗所, 士每拿, 别迦摩, 推雅推喇, 撒狄, 非拉铁非, 老底嘉 |
| the Alpha and the Omega (1:8; 21:6; 22:13) | 阿尔法和欧米伽, the modern names of the letters |
| the Nicolaitans (2:6, 15) | 尼哥拉党人 (Nicolaus, Acts 6:5: 尼哥拉) |
| Antipas (2:13) | 安提帕 |
| Balaam, Balak (2:14) | 巴兰, 巴勒 (巴勒王 at R) |
| Jezebel (2:20) | 耶洗别 |
| the key of David (3:7) | 大卫的钥匙 (L, B); 大卫王的钥匙 (R) |
| the Lion of the tribe of Judah, the Root of David (5:5; 22:16) | 犹大支派的狮子, 大卫的根 |
| choenix (6:6) | 科尼克斯 (L); `{{units:约1升\|一科尼克斯}}` (B, R) |
| the twelve tribes (7:5-8) | 犹大, 流便, 迦得, 亚设, 拿弗他利, 玛拿西, 西缅, 利未, 以萨迦, 西布伦, 约瑟, 便雅悯 |
| Abaddon, Apollyon (9:11) | 亚巴顿, 亚玻伦 (a name here, with the language named: 希伯来话, 希腊话) |
| the Euphrates (9:14; 16:12) | 幼发拉底河 |
| Sodom (11:8) | 所多玛 |
| Michael (12:7) | 米迦勒 |
| Armageddon (16:16) | 哈米吉多顿 |
| Gog and Magog (20:8) | 歌革和玛各 |

Terms settled in Revelation:

| English | Chinese |
|---|---|
| Lord God the Almighty (4:8; 11:17; 15:3; 16:7; 19:6; 21:22) | 主`{{god_term:神\|上帝}}`，全能者 (L); 全能的主`{{god_term:神\|上帝}}` (B); 统管万有的主`{{god_term:神\|上帝}}` (R). God the Almighty (16:14; 19:15): 全能者`{{god_term:神\|上帝}}` (L), 全能的`{{god_term:神\|上帝}}` (B), 统管万有的`{{god_term:神\|上帝}}` (R). The Almighty alone (1:8): 全能者 (L, B), 统管万有的那一位 (R) |
| the one who is and who was and who is coming (1:4, 8) | 今在、昔在、将要来的那一位 (L); 那位今在、昔在、将要来临的 (B); 那位现在就在、过去一直都在、将来还要来的 (R). The parts keep the Greek order where it changes (4:8: 那昔在、今在、将要来的 / 那位昔在、今在、将要来临的 / 过去一直都在……现在就在……将来还要来) and drop "is coming" with the Greek (11:17; 16:5: 那今在、昔在的 / 那位今在、昔在的 / 现在就在、过去一直都在) |
| seal (the seven seals, 5:1-8:1) | 印 (L, B); 封印 (R); opening them 揭开 (L, B), 打开 (R); "the scroll" is opened 展开 |
| God's seal; sealing; the sealed (7:2-8; 9:4) | the seal itself 印; to seal 印上 (L), 盖印 (B), 盖上印记 (R); the sealed 受印的 (L, B), 被盖上印记的 (R); the seal on the forehead 印记 at every level (9:4) |
| the beast's mark (charagma; 13:16-17; 14:9-11; 16:2; 19:20; 20:4) | 记号, kept apart from God's 印记 |
| the image of the beast (13:14-15; 14:9-11; 15:2; 16:2; 19:20; 20:4) | 兽的像 / 兽像 (L, B); 兽的雕像 (R) |
| sign (sēmeion) | 征兆 for a sign seen in heaven (12:1, 3; 15:1); 神迹 for a sign performed (13:13-14; 16:14; 19:20), R adding 奇事 where the English Readable says "miracles, called signs" |
| Woe (8:13; 12:12; 18:10, 16, 19) | 有祸了 (L, B); 真是可悲, the cry 可悲啊 (R). The woes as events (9:12; 11:14): 祸 (L, B), 可怕的事 (R) |
| the abyss (9:1-11; 11:7; 17:8; 20:1-3) | 无底坑 (L, B); 无底深坑 (R); its shaft 井口 (L), 竖井 (B), 入口 (R) |
| the dragon, the ancient serpent (12:9; 20:2) | 龙 (pronoun 他); 古蛇 (L, B), 古老的蛇 (R); 魔鬼, 撒但 |
| Babylon the great (14:8; 16:19; 17:5; 18:2) | 大巴比伦 (L, B); 巴比伦大城 (R) |
| the second death (2:11; 20:6, 14; 21:8) | 第二次死 (L); 第二次的死 (B, R) |
| the book of life (3:5; 13:8; 17:8; 20:12, 15; 21:27) | 生命册 |
| the sons of Israel (2:14; 7:4; 21:12) | `{{gender:以色列的子孙\|以色列的儿子}}` (L); 以色列人 (B, R) |
| generic pronouns the English spans (that person / him; their / his) | 人, 这人, 那人, 他, 自己 with no gender span (style guide section 8), recorded once per chapter in a setting_alternative decision; spans only for 弟兄姐妹 (6:11; 12:10-11; 19:10; 22:9) and 儿女 (21:7) |
| Holy, holy, holy (4:8) | 圣哉，圣哉，圣哉 (L); 圣哉！圣哉！圣哉！ (B); ……是圣洁的，圣洁的，圣洁的！ (R) |
| Worthy are you / worthy is the Lamb (4:11; 5:9, 12) | 配得 (领受……), 配 + a verb (5:9: 配拿) (L, B); 应当得到 (R, receiving glory), 配 + a verb (R, 5:9) |
| Hallelujah (19:1-6) | 哈利路亚, no span; R adds 要赞美主 as the English Readable |
| blessing (eulogia, 5:12-13; 7:12) | 颂赞 (L, B); 赞美 (R) |
| every tribe and language and people and nation (5:9; 7:9; 10:11; 11:9; 13:7; 14:6; 17:15) | 各部族、各语言、各民族、各邦国 (L, B), the Greek's order where it changes |
| 您 (B, R) | John to the elder (7:14, 先生，您知道), the only address to a heavenly figure; the angels say 你 to John (10:9; 19:10; 22:9) |
| measures | `{{units:约1升\|一科尼克斯}}`, denarius in wages (6:6); `{{units:约300公里\|一千六百个斯塔迪亚}}` (14:20); `{{units:34公斤\|一他连得}}` (16:21); `{{units:约2200公里\|一万二千个斯塔迪亚}}`, `{{units:约65米\|一百四十四肘}}` (21:16-17); Literal keeps the original unit in Chinese numerals |

### Genesis

Genesis keeps the glossary's forms for every name and term above, and the
New Testament's Chinese wherever it quotes the same Hebrew: 1:27 and 2:24 as
Matthew 19:4-5 (他们 for "they", since the Hebrew has no "two"); 2:2 as
Hebrews 4:4; 2:7 "成了活的生灵" as 1 Corinthians 15:45; 12:1 as Acts 7:3;
12:3, 18:18 and 22:18 as Galatians 3:8 and Acts 3:25 (万族 / 万国 as the
Hebrew has families or nations); 15:5 as Romans 4:18; 15:6 as Romans 4:3
(with the Hebrew's active "he counted it"); 17:5 "我已经立你作多国的父" as
Romans 4:17; 18:10, 14 as Romans 9:9; 21:10 as Galatians 4:30; 21:12 as
Romans 9:7 and Hebrews 11:18; 22:17 as Hebrews 6:14; 25:23 as Romans 9:12;
28:12 as John 1:51. 47:31 keeps the Hebrew "bed" (床头), with a decision
naming Hebrews 11:21's "staff" (杖头, from the Greek). Names shared with Luke
3:34-38 and Matthew 1:2-3 keep their forms (塞特, 以挪士, 该南, 玛勒列, 雅列,
以诺, 玛土撒拉, 拉麦, 闪, 亚法撒, 沙拉, 希伯, 法勒, 拉吴, 西鹿, 拿鹤, 他拉;
法勒斯, 谢拉, 希斯仑, 他玛).

Terms settled in Genesis:

| English | Chinese |
|---|---|
| toledot, "these are the generations of" (2:4; 5:1; 6:9; 10:1; 11:10, 27; 25:12, 19; 36:1, 9; 37:2) | 这些是……的世系 (L, every heading; 5:1 世系的册子); B and R as the English level: "story" 故事, "account of the family (line)" 家族的记录 / 家族世系的记录, "list" 名单 |
| genealogy refrains (chapters 5 and 11, one wording for both) | X活了N年，就生了Y / X生了Y以后，又活了N年，也生了儿子和女儿 / X所有的日子是N年，他就死了 (L); X活到N岁时，成了Y的父亲 / Y出生以后，X又活了N年，还生了其他的儿女 / X的一生总共有N年，然后他死了 (B); X N岁的时候，生了一个儿子，名叫Y / ……他还有其他的儿子和女儿 / X活到N岁，然后就死了 (R) |
| "be fruitful and multiply" (1:22, 28; 8:17; 9:1, 7; 35:11; 17:20; 28:3; 47:27; 48:4) | 要生育，要繁增 / 使你生育，使你繁增 (L); 繁衍增多 (B, as Acts 7:17); R as the English Readable. 生养众多 (the Union Version's) is not used |
| offspring (zera) in the promises | 后裔 (L); 后代 (B, R), as Galatians 3:16; NT quotations keep their own wording (15:5; 21:12) |
| "and it came to pass" (wayehi) | 那时 or a time clause (……的时候, ……以后) (L); left out or a time clause (B, R) |
| behold (hinneh) | 看哪 (L); 看 / 你看, or left out (B); left out, 看 (R) |
| "lifted up his eyes and saw" | 举目观看 / 举目看见 (L); 抬起眼来 ("raised his eyes"), 抬头一看 ("looked up") (B); 抬头一看, 往四周一看 (R) |
| "Here I am" (hinneni) | 我在这里 (L; B where the English says "Here I am"); 是，我在这里 ("Yes, I am here") |
| "answered and said" | 回答说 (L); 回答 (B, R) |
| "fell on his face" (17:3, 17) | 脸伏于地 (L); 俯伏在地 (B); 跪下，脸贴着地 (R) |
| "Fear not" | 不要怕 (L, B); 不要害怕 (R) |
| "gathered to his people" (25:8, 17; 35:29; 49:29, 33) | 被收聚到他的族人那里 (L); 归到他的族人那里 (B); 去和（先他而死的）祖先团聚了 (R). "Breathed his last" 断了气 (L), 气息断绝 (B) |
| "knew" (yada, sexual) | 认识 (L, as Matthew 1:25); 有了夫妻关系 (B, "was intimate"); 同房 elsewhere |
| "went in to" (bo el) | 进到她那里 (L); 同房 (B, R) |
| "lay with" (shakhav im) | 同寝 (L); B and R as the English level (同寝, 同房, 睡) |
| the angel of the LORD; of God | `{{divine_name:上主\|雅威}}`的使者; *gt*的使者 (L, B, R). Angels in the plural (19; 28:12, as John 1:51; 32:2 B, R) 天使; 32:2 L 使者们 (the wordplay with the messengers of 32:4); 48:16 "the angel who redeemed me" 天使 |
| "the word of the LORD came" (15:1, 4) | *dn*的话临到 (L, B); *dn*对……说 (R) |
| YHWH Elohim (2:4-3:23) | `{{divine_name:上主\|雅威}}{{god_term:神\|上帝}}` |
| El Shaddai; Shaddai (49:25) | 全能的*gt*; 全能者 |
| El Elyon (14:18-22) | 至高的*gt* (as Hebrews 7:1) |
| El Olam (21:33) | 永恒的*gt* (L, B); 永远活着的*gt* (R) |
| El Roi (16:13) | 看见人的*gt* (L); 看见我的*gt* (B, R) |
| YHWH-yireh (22:14) | *dn*会预备 (L, R); *dn*会看顾 (B), the verbs of 22:8 |
| names with El- | 伊勒 throughout: 伊勒巴兰 (14:6), 伊勒伊罗伊以色列 (33:20), 伊勒伯特利 (35:7) |
| the God of Nahor (31:53) | 拿鹤的*gt*, with the span as the English writes "God"; a decision notes the plural verb (Laban may mean two gods) |
| the sons of God (6:2, 4) | *gt*的众子 at every level, as every English level keeps the phrase |
| pronouns for God | every Chinese pronoun for God in a `deity_pronoun` span, also where the English pronoun opens a sentence (its capital hides the span) or where Chinese adds one (求`{{deity_pronoun:你\|祢}}`, 32:12); God is not named instead of the pronoun; God's "I" is 我 |
| address (您) | 你 at L everywhere, 我主 for "my lord". 您 at B and R: servants to their master (24; 39:8-9; 40), sons to Isaac (27; 22:7), hosts and strangers in courtesy (18:3; 19:18-20; 23; 24), Jacob to Esau (32-33), Rachel to Laban (31:35), subjects to Pharaoh (40-41; 46:34-47:9; 50:5), the brothers and the Egyptians to Joseph and his steward with 大人 (42-44; 47:15-25) until 45:4. 你 between Jacob and Laban, from Esau to Jacob, and from the brothers to Joseph after 45:4. Strangers addressed as "sir" at R: 先生 (18:3; 19:18; 23) |
| Sheol (37:35; 42:38; 44:29, 31) | 阴间 (L, B); 死亡之地 (R), where the English Balanced and Readable say "the grave" |
| units | shekel 舍客勒 (L; 11.5克 in `units` spans at B and R: 十舍客勒 约115克, 二十舍客勒银子 约230克银子, 四百舍客勒 约4.6公斤, 一千舍客勒 约11.5公斤); beka 比加 (约5.7克); cubit 肘 (约45厘米); seah 细亚 (三细亚 约21升) |
| Noah's ark; gopher wood | 方舟 (as Hebrews 11:7); 歌斐木 (L, B) |
| the Negev | 内盖夫 at every level |
| Nephilim | 拿非利人 (R: 巨人，叫作拿非利人) |
| household gods (teraphim, 31) | 家中的神像, then 神像 (no span) |
| birthright | 长子的名分 |

Names spelled like a better-known person (one decision text, marked
uncertain for a reviewer): Elishah 以利沙 (10:4) like the prophet Elisha; Iob
约伯 (46:13) like Job; Eri 以利 (46:16) like Eli the priest; Serah 西拉
(46:17) like Silas. Paran 巴兰 (21:21) is spelled like Balaam, as in the
Union Version, noted once. Haran the man and Haran the city are both 哈兰, as
the English spells them alike (the Hebrew differs; 11:26, 31).

### Exodus

Exodus keeps the glossary's forms for every name and term above, and Genesis's
Chinese for the names the two books share (Levi's sons 革顺, 哥辖, 米拉利, and the
sons of Reuben and Simeon in 6:14-15 as Genesis 46:9-11). Where the New
Testament quotes Exodus and the Hebrew says the same, its Chinese is kept at
each level: 3:6 as Matthew 22:32; 20:12-17 as Matthew 19:18-19, Mark 7:10 and
Romans 13:9 (Balanced keeps the law's "不可" before their verbs); 21:24 as
Matthew 5:38; "the blood of the covenant" 24:8 as Matthew 26:28 and Mark 14:24;
16:18 as 2 Corinthians 8:15; 32:6 as 1 Corinthians 10:7; 33:19 as Romans 9:15
for the clause where the Greek and the Hebrew agree (恩待 for the Hebrew "be
gracious", where Romans has 怜悯). The tabernacle chapters 35-40 repeat the
wording of 25-31 wherever the Hebrew repeats, with only the verbs changed from
command to report. Names not in the tables above, as the text has them:

| English (first verse) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| Pithom, Rameses (1:11) | 比东, 兰塞 |
| Shiphrah, Puah (1:15) | 施弗拉, 普阿 |
| Reuel (2:18) | 流珥 (Jethro's other name; each kept where the Hebrew has it) |
| Gershom (2:22); Eliezer (18:4) | 革舜 (kept apart from Levi's son Gershon 革顺); 以利以谢 |
| Hanoch, Pallu, Hezron, Carmi (6:14) | 哈诺, 法路, 希斯仑, 迦米 (as Genesis 46:9) |
| Jemuel, Jamin, Ohad, Jakin, Zohar, Shaul (6:15) | 耶母利, 雅悯, 阿辖, 雅斤, 琐辖, 扫罗 (as Genesis 46:10) |
| Libni, Shimei (6:17) | 立尼, 示每 |
| Amram, Izhar, Hebron, Uzziel (6:18) | 暗兰, 以斯哈, 希伯仑 (the man, spelled as the city), 乌薛 |
| Mahli, Mushi (6:19); Jochebed (6:20) | 抹利, 母示; 约基别 |
| Korah, Nepheg, Zikri (6:21) | 可拉, 尼斐, 细基利 |
| Mishael, Elzaphan, Sithri (6:22) | 米沙利, 以利撒反, 西提利 |
| Elisheba, Amminadab, Nahshon (6:23) | 以利沙巴, 亚米拿达, 拿顺 |
| Nadab, Abihu, Ithamar (6:23) | 拿答, 亚比户, 以他玛 |
| Assir, Elkanah, Abiasaph (6:24); Putiel (6:25) | 亚惜, 以利加拿, 亚比亚撒; 普铁 |
| Succoth (12:37); Etham (13:20) | 疏割; 以倘 |
| Pi-hahiroth, Migdol, Baal-zephon (14:2) | 比哈希录, 密夺, 巴力洗分 |
| Shur, Marah, Elim (15:22-27) | 书珥, 玛拉, 以琳 |
| the wilderness of Sin (16:1); Rephidim (17:1) | 汛的旷野; 利非订 |
| Massah, Meribah (17:7) | 玛撒, 米利巴 |
| Amalek (17:8) | 亚玛力, 亚玛力人 |
| Hur (17:10) | 户珥 (also Bezalel's grandfather, 31:2) |
| Nun (33:11) | 嫩 (约书亚的父亲) |
| Bezalel, Uri (31:2) | 比撒列, 乌利 |
| Oholiab, Ahisamach (31:6) | 亚何利亚伯, 亚希撒抹 |

| Pharaoh hardens his own heart (8:11, 28; 9:34; 13:15) | 使自己的心沉重 (L; 使[自己的心]刚硬 for qashah, 13:15); 使自己的心顽固起来 (B); 固执起来 (R) |

| "I AM WHO I AM"; "I AM has sent me" (3:14) | 我是我所是 (L, B), 我是我所是的那一位 (R); 我是者 (L, B), 那位称为我是的 (R). The Union Version's 我是自有永有的 is not used |
| ʾAdonai YHWH (23:17; 34:23) | 主宰*dn* |

| address | Moses and Aaron, the foremen, Miriam to Pharaoh's daughter, Aaron to Moses (32:22) say 您 at B and R; Pharaoh says 你 / 你们; God's words relayed to Pharaoh keep 你 |

| curtains (yeriʿot); the veil (parokhet); screen (masakh); courtyard hangings (qelaʿim) | 幕; 帘幕; 门帘; 帷幔 |

| atonement cover (kapporet) | 赎罪盖 (L, B); 约柜的盖子，就是遮盖罪的地方 (R) |
| altar; altar of burnt offering; altar of incense | 坛 (L), 祭坛 (B, R; 坛 for the short form inside a description); 燔祭坛 (L, B), 献全烧祭的祭坛 (R); 香坛 (L, B), 烧香用的坛 (R); the gold altar 金坛 |
| basin (kiyyor) and its stand | 水盆和盆座 (L, B); 大铜盆和它的座 (R) |
| lampstand; table; bread of the Presence | 灯台; 桌子; 陈设饼 (L, B), 摆在*gt*面前的饼 (R) |
| ephod; breastpiece (of judgment); robe; tunic; turban; sash; the gold plate | 以弗得; (决断的)胸牌; 外袍; 内袍; 头巾; 腰带; 纯金的牌子 |
| woven garments (bigde haserad) | 编织的衣服 (L); 精工织成的衣服 (B); 精心织成的衣服 (R) |
| acacia wood | 金合欢木 |
| contribution (terumah) | 举祭 (L, B); 献给*dn*的礼物 (R) |
| anointing oil; holy anointing oil | 膏抹的油 (L), 膏油 (B), 膏抹用的油 (R); 膏抹用的圣油 (L, R), 圣膏油 (B) |
| consecrate (qiddash) | 使……成圣 (L); 使……分别为圣 (B); 使……分别出来，成为圣洁 (R), or as the English Readable says ("make holy" 使……成为圣洁; "set apart for God" 分别出来归给*gt*) |
| ordain ("fill the hand"); ram of ordination | 充满……的手 (L), 授圣职 (B), 担任祭司的职分 (R); 授职的公羊 (L, B) |
| a lasting statute | 永远的定例 (L, B); 必须永远遵守的规定 (R) |
| "as the LORD commanded Moses" (35-40) | 照着*dn*吩咐摩西的 (L); 正如*dn*吩咐摩西的 (B); 都是照着*dn*吩咐摩西的做的 (R) |

| the Ten Words (34:28) | 十句话 |
| the Festival of Unleavened Bread; of Harvest; of Ingathering; of Weeks | 除酵节; 收割节; 收藏节; 七七节 |
| "YHWH is my banner" (17:15) | “*dn*是我的旗帜” |

### Leviticus

Leviticus keeps the glossary's forms for every name and term above, and
Exodus's Chinese for the tabernacle, its furniture and the priests' garments.
The ordination of Aaron and his sons (8) repeats Exodus 29's Chinese wherever
the Hebrew repeats it, with only the verbs changed from command to report
(8:30 keeps Exodus 29:21's 弹 / 洒). Where the New Testament quotes Leviticus
and the Hebrew says the same, its Chinese is kept at each level: "You shall be
holy, for I am holy" (11:44-45; 19:2; 20:26) as 1 Peter 1:16; 18:5 as
Galatians 3:12 and Romans 10:5 (L, B; Readable explains "live by them");
19:18 as Matthew 22:39 and Romans 13:9; 24:20 as Matthew 5:38 and Exodus
21:24. Names not in the tables above, as the text has them:

| English (first verse) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| Nadab, Abihu, Eleazar, Ithamar (10:1, 6) | 拿答, 亚比户, 以利亚撒, 以他玛 (as Exodus 6:23) |
| Mishael, Elzaphan, Uzziel (10:4) | 米沙利, 以利撒反, 乌薛 (as Exodus 6:18, 22) |
| Azazel (16:8) | 阿撒泻勒 |
| Shelomith, Dibri (24:11) | 示罗密, 底伯利 |
| Molech (18:21) | 摩洛, no span; 摩洛这个神明 (R) |
| the goat-demons (se'irim, 17:7) | 山羊鬼魔 (L, B); 山羊形状的鬼魔 (R), no span (uncertain) |

The rite and sacrifice formulas, one wording per level through the book
(*dn* = `{{divine_name:上主|雅威}}`):

| English | Literal | Balanced | Readable |
|---|---|---|---|
| "a burnt offering, a food offering, a soothing aroma to the LORD" | 这是燔祭，是火祭，是献给*dn*的馨香之气 | 这是燔祭，是火祭，是使*dn*喜悦的馨香之气 | 这是全烧的祭，是用火献上的祭，是让*dn*喜悦的香气 |
| "the priest shall make atonement for him, and he shall be forgiven" | 祭司要为他赎罪，他就要蒙赦免 | 祭司要这样为他赎罪，他就会得到赦免 | 祭司这样除去他的罪，他就会得到饶恕 |
| make atonement (kipper) for a person's sin | 为……赎罪 | 为……赎罪 | 除去……的罪 |
| kipper removing uncleanness that is no sin (12:7-8; 14:18-31; 15:15, 30), and the rite named without a person (16:17, 27) | 为……赎罪 | 为……赎罪 | 为……举行赎罪的仪式 |
| kipper for a thing: the altar, a house, the sanctuary and the tent (8:15; 14:53; 16:16-20, 33) | 为……赎罪 | 为……赎罪 | 为……行洁净礼 |
| most holy | 至圣的; 至圣之物 for "a most holy [thing]" | 至圣的 | 最圣洁的 |
| make smoke (on the altar) | 在坛上烧成烟 | 放在祭坛上烧，使烟上升 | 烧掉 |
| throw the blood against the altar; sprinkle | 泼在坛的周围; 弹 | 泼在祭坛的四周; 弹 | 泼在祭坛的四周; 弹 (洒 where the blood and water are sprinkled with hyssop, 14:7, 51, at every level, and 8:30 R as Exodus 29:21) |
| a statute forever (for your generations) | 永远的定例 | 永久的定例 | 必须永远遵守的规定 |
| a portion forever | 永远的份 / 永远应得的份 | 永久应得的份 | as the English Readable |
| cut off from his people | 从他的族人中被剪除 | 从百姓中被剪除 | 从百姓中被除掉 |
| bear his iniquity | 担当他的罪孽 | 承担自己的罪责 | 必须为此负责 |
| in error, unintentionally (shegagah) | 误犯 | 无意中犯了罪 | 不是故意地犯了罪 |
| offering (qorban); bring near | 供物; 奉上 | 祭物; 献 | 祭物; 献 |
| surely be put to death | 一定要被处死 | 必须被处死 | 必须被处死 |
| "The LORD spoke to Moses, saying" | *dn*对摩西说话，说： | *dn*对摩西说： | *dn*对摩西说： |
| "I am the LORD (your God)" | 我是*dn*(你们的*gt*) | as L | as L |
| "I am the LORD who makes you holy" | 我是使你们成圣的*dn* | as L | 我是*dn*，是我使你们圣洁 |
| "for holy am I, the LORD" (19:2; 20:26; 21:8) | 因为我圣洁，我*dn* | 因为我*dn*……是圣洁的 | 因为我是圣洁的。我是*dn* |

野兔, 猪; 鹰, 胡兀鹫, 秃鹫, 赤鸢, 黑鸢, 乌鸦 (R 渡鸦和乌鸦), 鸵鸟, 角鸮, 海鸥,

秃头蝗, 蟋蟀, 蚱蜢; 黄鼠狼, 老鼠, 大蜥蜴, 壁虎, 巨蜥, 壁蜥, 沙蜥, 变色龙.

### Numbers

Numbers keeps the glossary's forms for every name and term above, Exodus's
Chinese for the tabernacle, its furniture and the Levite clans (革顺, 哥辖,
米拉利; 立尼, 示每, 暗兰, 以斯哈, 希伯仑, 乌薛, 抹利, 母示, as Exodus 6:16-22),
Genesis 46's forms for the clans of chapter 26, and Leviticus's rite and
sacrifice formulas (the table in the Leviticus section) in 5-6, 8, 15, 18-19
and 28-29. Where the Hebrew repeats a paragraph, the Chinese repeats it,
changing only names and numbers: the census of each tribe (1:20-43; 26), the
camps (2), the twelve leaders' offerings (7:12-83), the festival offerings
(28-29), the stages of the journey (33:5-49). Where the New Testament recalls
Numbers and the Hebrew says the same, its Chinese is kept at each level: 12:7
as Hebrews 3:2, 5 ("在我的全家是忠心的"); 14:18 as Exodus 34:6-7; 14:29, 32 as
Hebrews 3:17 ("尸体倒毙在旷野" L, "横卧在旷野" B, "留在荒野里" R); 21:8-9 with
John 3:14 ("杆子", "铜蛇"); 19:2 as Hebrews 9:13 ("母牛犊"); 17:20-25 as Hebrews
9:4 ("杖", "发芽"). 16:5 follows the Hebrew, with a decision naming 2 Timothy
2:19's Greek form ("主认识属他的人"); 25:9 keeps the Hebrew's 两万四千, with a
decision naming 1 Corinthians 10:8's 两万三千. Names not in the tables above,
as the text has them (the conventional Chinese Bible forms):

| English (first verse) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| the tribal leaders (1:5-15; again in 2, 7, 10) | 示丢珥的儿子以利蓿; 苏利沙代的儿子示路蔑; 亚米拿达的儿子拿顺; 苏押的儿子拿坦业; 希伦的儿子以利押; 亚米忽的儿子以利沙玛; 比大蓿的儿子迦玛列 (as Acts 5:34; 比大苏 is not used); 基多尼的儿子亚比但; 亚米沙代的儿子亚希以谢; 俄兰的儿子帕结; 丢珥 (Deuel, 1:14; 7:42; 10:20) / 流珥 (Reuel, 2:14) 的儿子以利雅萨, as each verse spells the father; 以南的儿子亚希拉 |

| address | 你 at L everywhere. 您 at B and R: Aaron to Moses (12:11-12), Joshua to Moses (11:28), the people pleading with Moses (21:7), the officers and the Gadites and Reubenites (31:49; 32), the clan leaders (36:2), messengers to the king of Edom and to Sihon (20:14-19; 21:22), Balaam to Balak in prose (22:38; 23-24); Balaam's oracles, Balak to Balaam and the quarrels of 16 and 20 say 你 |
| "my lord" (ʾadoni) to Moses | 我主 (L, B); 您 or 主人 (R) |
| units | Literal the Hebrew unit; Balanced and Readable a `units` span, metric first: shekel 11.5克 (five shekels 约57克银子; ten 约115克; seventy 约800克; 130 约1.5公斤; 1,365 约15.7公斤; 2,400 约27.6公斤; 16,750 约190公斤); a tenth of an ephah 约2.2升, two tenths 约4.4升, three tenths 约6.6升; hin: half 约1.9升, a third 约1.2升, a quarter 约0.9升; homer 约220升 (ten homers 约2200升); cubit 约45厘米 (two cubits 约90厘米, a thousand 约450米, two thousand 约900米) |

### Deuteronomy

Deuteronomy keeps the glossary's forms for every name and term above, and
the Chinese of Exodus, Leviticus and Numbers for what it retells: the Ten
Words (5:6-21) are Exodus 20:2-17's Chinese wherever the Hebrew is the same
and differ only where Deuteronomy's Hebrew does (5:12 "keep" for "remember";
5:14-15 the ox and the donkey, and the Sabbath grounded in the exodus; 5:20
"empty" witness; 5:21 the wife first and "desire" for the house); the
journeys of 1-3 and 10:6-7 keep Numbers 20-21 and 33's names; the clean and
unclean animals of 14:3-21 are Leviticus 11's list; the feasts of 16 keep
逾越节, 除酵节, 七七节, 住棚节. Where the New Testament quotes Deuteronomy and
the Hebrew says the same, its Chinese is kept at each level, with
`{{divine_name:上主|雅威}}` where the Greek has "Lord": 5:16 (Ephesians 6:2-3),
5:17-21 (Matthew 19:18; Romans 13:9), 6:4-5 (Mark 12:29-30, without "mind"),
6:13 (Matthew 4:10, with the Hebrew "fear"), 6:16 (Matthew 4:7: 试验, where
Exodus 17:2, 7 has 试探), 8:3 (Matthew 4:4), 18:15 (Acts 3:22), 19:15
(Matthew 18:16; 2 Corinthians 13:1), 21:23 (Galatians 3:13's frame, with the
glossary's 诅咒 for Galatians' 咒诅 and the Hebrew's "of God"), 25:4
(1 Corinthians 9:9), 27:26 (Galatians 3:10), 30:12, 14 (Romans 10:6, 8; 30:13
keeps the Hebrew "beyond the sea"), 31:6 (Hebrews 13:5's verbs), 32:35
(Romans 12:19; Hebrews 10:30), 32:43 (Romans 15:10's frame). 18:19 follows
the Hebrew ("I will require it"), with a decision naming Acts 3:23. Names not
in the tables above, as the text has them:

| English (first verse) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| Suph, Tophel, Laban, Hazeroth, Di-zahab (1:1) | 苏弗, 陀弗, 拉班, 哈洗录 (as Numbers 11:35), 底撒哈 |
| the Arabah; the Sea of the Arabah, the Salt Sea (1:1; 3:17) | 亚拉巴; 亚拉巴海, 盐海 |
| Ashtaroth, Edrei (1:4) | 亚斯他录 (kept apart from Ashteroth-karnaim, Genesis 14:5), 以得来 (as Numbers 21:33) |
| the Shephelah; the Negev (1:7) | 示非拉 (L), 丘陵 (B), 西边的低矮丘陵 (R); 内盖夫 (L, B), 南方的荒漠 (R) |
| the valley of Eshcol (1:24) | 以实各谷 (as Numbers 13:23) |
| Hormah (1:44) | 何珥玛 (as Numbers 14:45) |

| Mount Paran (33:2) | 巴兰山 |

### Joshua

Joshua keeps the forms of Genesis to Numbers and Deuteronomy for every name
and term above: the charge of Deuteronomy 31, the ban (herem), the
possession and inheritance words, the clans and the tabernacle. The altar on
Mount Ebal (8:31) uses the words of Deuteronomy 27:5-6. Joshua 15:15-19 and
Judges 1:11-15 retell one story in nearly the same Hebrew, so each level
keeps one Chinese wording where the Hebrew is the same. Joshua 15:17 B is
drafted fresh. Rahab keeps the form of Hebrews 11:31 and James 2:25. Names
not in the tables above, as the text has them:

|---|---|

| Gilgal; the Hill of Foreskins (5:3) | 吉甲; 包皮山 |
| Achan son of Carmi, son of Zabdi, son of Zerah (7:1); the Valley of Achor (7:26) | 亚干, 迦米, 撒底, 谢拉; 亚割谷 |
| Ai; Beth-aven | 艾城 (every time); 伯亚文 |
| Mount Ebal; Mount Gerizim (8:30, 33) | 以巴路山; 基利心山 |
| Gibeon, Chephirah, Beeroth, Kiriath-jearim (9:17) | 基遍, 基非拉, 比录, 基列耶琳 |
| the Book of Jashar (10:13) | 《雅煞珥书》 |
| Makkedah; Hazor; the waters of Merom | 玛基大; 夏琐; 米伦水 |
| Kiriath-arba (14:15), Kiriath-sepher (15:15), Kiriath-baal, Kiriath-sannah, Kiriathaim (13:19), Kiriath (18:28) | 基列亚巴, 基列西弗, 基列巴力, 基列萨拿, 基列亭, 基列: the conventional forms; "基列" is also Gilead, a different Hebrew name (decision at 18:28) |
| Bamoth-baal (13:17) | 巴末巴力, in the order of the Hebrew parts; Numbers 22:41 has 巴力巴末 for the same name (uncertain, for a reviewer) |
| Beth-haram (13:27) | 伯亚兰, kept apart from the Beth-haran of Numbers 32:36 (伯哈兰), as the Hebrew spells them |
| Rumah (15:52) | 路玛, as the Hebrew read here; *alt.* 度玛 (Dumah) |
| Othniel son of Kenaz; Achsah (15:16-17) | 俄陀聂, 基纳斯; 押撒 (as Judges 1:12-13) |

| Kohath, Gershon, Merari (21:4-7) | 哥辖, 革顺, 米拉利 (as Numbers 3) |

| Gibeah of Phinehas (24:33) | 他儿子非尼哈的基比亚 (L); 基比亚，就是他儿子非尼哈的山城 (B) |
| the Salt Sea; the Sea of Arabah | 盐海 (L), 死海 (B, R); 亚拉巴海 |
| the Sea of Reeds (2:10; 4:23; 24:6) | 芦苇海 (L), 红海 (B, R), as the English level |

### Judges

Judges keeps the glossary's forms for every name and term above, and the
forms of Genesis to Deuteronomy for places, tribes and clans (Numbers and
Deuteronomy for the Transjordan, Joshua's lists for the towns of Canaan).
1:11-15 retells Joshua 15:15-19 in nearly the same Hebrew and follows its
Chinese closely at each level, differing where the Hebrew differs ("went" for
"went up", "the younger than he", "to him") and in a few Balanced phrases
(1:13 攻下了那城 / 许配给他; 1:15 你已经把内盖夫的地给了我，现在也请给我几处水泉),
for a reviewer to bring into line with whichever is settled. The book's refrains have one
wording per level wherever they return (*dn* = `{{divine_name:上主|雅威}}`,
*gt* = `{{god_term:神|上帝}}`):

| English | Literal | Balanced | Readable |
|---|---|---|---|
| did evil in the eyes of the LORD (2:11; 3:7, 12; 4:1; 6:1; 10:6; 13:1) | 做了在*dn*眼中为恶的事 | 做了*dn*看为恶的事 | 做了*dn*认为邪恶的事 |
| served the Baals / the Ashtaroth / the Asherahs | 事奉众巴力 / 亚斯他录 / 众亚舍拉 | 事奉众巴力 / 亚斯他录 / 众亚舍拉 | 拜众巴力 / 称为亚斯他录的女神 / 称为亚舍拉的女神 |
| the anger of the LORD burned against Israel (2:14, 20; 3:8; 10:7) | *dn*的怒气向以色列发作 | *dn*向以色列大发烈怒 | *dn*对以色列非常生气 |
| sold them into the hand of (2:14; 3:8; 4:2; 10:7) | 卖在……的手中 | 卖在……的权下 | 交给 |
| cried out to the LORD | 向*dn*呼求 | 向*dn*呼求 | 向*dn*呼求帮助 |
| raised up a deliverer; judges | 兴起一位拯救者; 兴起士师 | 兴起一位拯救者; 兴起士师 | as the English; 兴起一些领袖，称为士师 (2:16), then 领袖 |
| judged Israel N years (3:10; 4:4; 10:2-3; 12:7-14; 15:20; 16:31) | 作以色列的士师N年 | 作以色列的士师N年 | 领导以色列N年 |
| the land had rest N years (3:11, 30; 5:31; 8:28) | 这地安静了N年 | 这地太平了N年 | 这地有N年太平的日子 |
| the Spirit of the LORD was upon (3:10; 11:29) | *dn*的灵在……身上 | 降在……身上 | 临到……身上 |
| the Spirit clothed Gideon (6:34) | 以基甸为衣 | 像衣服一样披在基甸身上 | 降在基甸身上，掌管了他 |
| the Spirit rushed upon Samson (14:6, 19; 15:14) | *dn*的灵猛然降在他身上 (every level) | | |
| In those days there was no king in Israel (17:6; 18:1; 19:1; 21:25) | 在那些日子，以色列中没有王 | 那时以色列没有王 | 那个时候，以色列没有王 |
| each did what was right in his own eyes (17:6; 21:25) | 各人都做自己眼中看为正的事 (as Deuteronomy 12:8; Samson's "right in my eyes", 14:3, 7: 在我眼中看为正) | 人人都做自己认为对的事 | 每个人都照自己觉得对的去做 |

您 (B, R); Jael to Sisera 大人 and 您 (B), 先生 (R); Zebul's message to

takes him for a prophet; Gideon to the angel 先生 (6:13), and 你 in the
deity_pronoun span once the English spans "you" (6:17-18); Jephthah's
daughter to her father 您 (B, R); Jephthah and the Ammonite king 你 (one
ruler to another); Israel's request to Edom and Sihon (11:17-19) as Numbers
20:17 with 您.

49:15. Shaalabbin 沙拉宾 (Joshua 19:42) and Shaalbim 沙宾 (Judges 1:35),
Aphek 亚弗 and Aphik 亚弗革, Timnath-serah 亭拿西拉 and Timnath-heres 亭拿希烈
follow the two spellings of the Hebrew.

### Ruth

Ruth keeps the forms of Genesis to Judges for every name and term above (士师 for "the judges judged", 1:1; the oath of Judges 8:19 at 3:13; Leviticus 19:9 and 25 for gleaning and redeeming; Deuteronomy 25:5-10 for the levirate and the sandal; Genesis 29, 35 and 38 and Matthew 1:3-6 for the names of 4:11-22), and settles these:

| Ephrathites (1:2); Ephrathah (4:11) | 以法他人; 以法他 (as Genesis 35:19) |
| the fields of Moab | 摩押的田野 (L, as Genesis 36:35); 摩押地 (B, R) |
| Mara (1:20) | 玛拉, the name of Exodus 15:23's bitter water; R explains both names in the text (‘甜美’, ‘苦’), with a footnoted decision |
| Shaddai (1:20-21) | 全能者 (L, B); 全能的*gt* (R), as Numbers 24:4 |
| mother-in-law; daughter-in-law; sister-in-law (1:15) | 婆婆; 儿媳; 妯娌 |
| "my daughter(s)" (Naomi, Boaz) | 我的女儿(们) (L); 女儿(们) (B, R) |
| people (1:10, 15, 16) | 百姓 (L); 同胞 (B); 族人 (R) |
| "your people my people, your God my God" (1:16) | 你的百姓[要作]我的百姓，你的*gt*[要作]我的*gt* (L); 如今是 / 如今也是 (B); 从今天起……也是 (R); drafted fresh |
| "May the LORD do so to me, and more" (1:17) | 愿*dn*这样对待我，并且加倍对待我 (L); 愿*dn*严厉地惩罚我，而且罚得更重 (B); a promise, then 愿{{deity_pronoun:他\|祂}}严厉地惩罚我 (R) |
| hesed between people (1:8; 3:10) | 慈爱 (L); 恩待 / 恩情 (B); 善待 / 恩情 (R) |
| the LORD's hesed (2:20) | 慈爱 (L, B); 始终不变的爱 (R) |

| the harvesters; Boaz's young men; young women | 收割的人; 年轻人 (L), 年轻男仆 (B), 年轻男工 (R); 女仆 (L, B), 年轻女子 (R); the whole crew (2:21) 工人 (B, R) |
| "find favor in your eyes" | 在……眼中得恩宠 (L, as Genesis 18:3); 恩待 (B) |
| "The LORD be with you" / "The LORD bless you" (2:4) | 愿*dn*与你们同在！ / 愿*dn*赐福给你（您）！ |

| the generations of Perez (4:18) | 世系 (L, as Genesis 11:10); 后代 (B); 家谱 (R); "fathered" 生了 (L, B), 是……的父亲 (R) |
| Perez, Hezron, Ram, Amminadab, Nahshon, Salmon, Boaz, Obed, Jesse, David (4:18-22) | 法勒斯, 希斯仑, 兰, 亚米拿达, 拿顺, 撒门, 波阿斯, 俄备得, 耶西, 大卫 (Matthew 1:3-6); Salmah (4:20) 撒玛 at L only (uncertain), 撒门 at B and R; Rachel, Leah 拉结, 利亚; Tamar, Judah 他玛, 犹大 |

Address: Naomi and her daughters-in-law say 你 to each other at every level (uncertain: 您 from the daughters-in-law is possible at B and R); Boaz says 你 and 女儿 to Ruth; Ruth says 您 to Boaz at B and R, with 我主 (L), 大人 (B), 先生 (R) for "my lord"; the harvesters say 您 to Boaz at B and R.

### 1 Samuel

1 Samuel keeps the glossary's forms for every name and term above, and the
forms of Joshua, Judges and Ruth for places, refrains and oaths ("judged
Israel" 4:18; 7:6, 15 as Judges; "as the LORD lives" as Ruth 3:13 and Judges
8:19; the self-curse as Ruth 1:17; "the Spirit rushed upon" as Judges 14:6).
The women's song of 18:7 is quoted at 21:12 (English 21:11) and 29:5 in the
same words at each level. Hannah's song (2:1-10) and the familiar lines of
7:12, 8:7, 12:3, 12:23, 15:22, 16:7 and 17:45-47 are drafted fresh, with any
clause that came close to the Union Version reworded. 13:14 keeps the wording
of Acts 13:22 for "a man according to his heart" at each level, turned to the
third person (*dn* = `{{divine_name:上主|雅威}}`, *gt* = `{{god_term:神|上帝}}`,
*he* = `{{deity_pronoun:他|祂}}`):

| English | Literal | Balanced | Readable |
|---|---|---|---|
| Saul has struck down his thousands, and David his ten thousands (18:7; 21:12; 29:5) | 扫罗击杀了他的数千[人]， \q 大卫击杀了他的数万[人] | 扫罗击杀成千， \q 大卫击杀上万 | 扫罗杀了几千人， \q 大卫杀了几万人 |
| the LORD's anointed (12:3, 5; 24:7, 11; 26:9-23) | *dn*所膏立的 | *dn*所膏立的 | *dn*所立的王 |
| as the LORD lives (14:39, 45; 19:6; 20:3, 21; 25:26; 26:10, 16; 28:10; 29:6) | [我指着]永活的*dn*[起誓] | 我指着永活的*dn*起誓 | 我指着永活的*dn*发誓 |
| God do so to me, and more (3:17; 14:44; 20:13; 25:22) | 愿*gt*这样对待[我]，并且加倍对待[我] | 愿*gt*严厉地惩罚我，而且罚得更重 | 愿*gt*严厉地惩罚我 |
| a man according to his heart (13:14) | 一个按着*he*心的人 | 一个和*he*心意相通的人 | 这人想要的，正是*he*想要的 (Acts 13:22) |
| judged Israel (4:18; 7:6, 15; 8:1) | 作以色列的士师 | 作以色列的士师 | 领导以色列; deciding cases (7:16-17) 审判 / 审理案件 / 断案 |
| the Spirit of the LORD / of God rushed upon (10:6, 10; 11:6; 16:13) | *dn* / *gt*的灵猛然降在……身上 (every level) | | |
| Is Saul also among the prophets? (10:11-12; 19:24) | 扫罗也在众先知当中吗？ | 扫罗也是先知中的一员吗？ | 扫罗也成了先知吗？ |

| the capital "He" for God where the English Readable has no span (12:6, 11; 24:16; 25:32; 28:6, 18; 30:23) | *he*, not a repeated divine name, so the divine_name spans match the English |

Address: Saul says 您 to Samuel at B and R (9:21; 13:11; 15; 28:15) and
Samuel says 你 to Saul at every level (15:1); Saul's servant says 您 to him
(9:6-8), as the armor-bearer and soldiers to Jonathan (14:7, 28); subjects
say 您 and 陛下 to the king at B and R (14:36; 16:15-16; 17:32-58; 19:4;
22:12-15; 23:20; 24:9-16; 26:17-24), with 我主我王 (L, B) for "my lord the
king"; David, the commanders and the people of Jabesh say 您 to a foreign
king (11:1; 22:3; 27-29); Abigail says 您 to David at B and R, with 我主 (L)
and 大人 (B, R) for "my lord" (25:24-31, 41), and David says 你 to her;
Hannah says 您 to Eli with 我主 (L), 大人 (B), 先生 (R); Samuel says 您 to
Eli (3:5-8), the people and elders to Samuel (7:8; 8:5; 12:4; 16:4); the
woman of Endor says 你 to the stranger and 您 to Saul once she knows him
(28:21-22). Ahimelech and David say 你 to each other (21:2-10), one man of
standing to another; Saul's officials say 你 to David, speaking for the king
(18:22). Jonathan and David say 你 to each other.

### 2 Samuel

2 Samuel keeps the forms of Genesis to Ruth and the glossary's tables for every
name and term above, and 1 Samuel's forms for the people and places the two
books share (Saul's house, David's wives, Abner, Abiathar, Ahimelech, Ahitub,
Gath, Ziklag, Jabesh-gilead, Gilboa, Beth-shan). Chapter 22 is drafted to be
copied into Psalm 18 wherever the Hebrew is the same (22:50 keeps Romans
15:9's wording); 7:14 keeps Hebrews 1:5's. The lament of 1:19-27, the dirge of
3:33-34, the song of 22 and the last words of 23:1-7 keep the English lines at
each level. David's cry for Absalom (19:1, 5) and the song of 22 are drafted
afresh, and every clause that came out in the Union Version's words was
redrafted. One wording per level for the book's formulas (*dn* =
`{{divine_name:上主|雅威}}`, *gt* = `{{god_term:神|上帝}}`):

| English | Literal | Balanced | Readable |
|---|---|---|---|
| How the mighty have fallen! (1:19, 25, 27) | 勇士们怎么竟倒下了 | 勇士们竟然倒下了 | 勇敢的战士们竟然倒下了 |
| as the LORD lives (2:27; 4:9; 12:5; 14:11; 15:21) | [我指着]永活的*dn*[起誓] | 我指着永活的*dn*起誓 | 我指着永活的*dn*发誓 (as Judges 8:19, Ruth 3:13) |
| Thus may God do to me, and more (3:9, 35; 19:14) | 愿*gt*这样对待我，并且愿{{deity_pronoun:他\|祂}}加倍对待我 | 愿*gt*严厉地惩罚我，而且罚得更重 | 愿*gt*严厉地惩罚我 (as Ruth 1:17) |
| God was entreated for the land (21:14; 24:25) | 就为这地应允了祈求 | 应允了人们为这地所献的祷告 | 应允了人们为这地所作的祷告 |
| My son Absalom! (19:1, 5) | 我的儿子押沙龙！我的儿子，我的儿子押沙龙！ (every level; not the Union Version's 我儿……啊) | | |

Talmai 挞买 and Aiah 亚雅 follow one Hebrew name, one Chinese form, against
the forms some Bibles use in Samuel (达买, 爱雅); Paltiel 帕铁 and Palti 帕提,
Beth-shan 伯珊 and Beth-shean 伯善 follow the two spellings of the Hebrew.

### 1 Kings

1 Kings keeps the glossary's forms for every name and term above, the forms of
Genesis to Ruth for places, refrains and oaths, and 1 and 2 Samuel's forms for
David's house, his officials and offices (史官, 书记, 军队统帅) and address. The
regnal formulas (accession, age and mother, verdict, "walked in the way of",
the sins of Jeroboam, the high places, the source, death and burial,
succession, provoking the LORD to anger, the word of the LORD by his servant
the prophet) are worded exactly as in the 2 Kings section below, at every
level, and every record with the source formula carries the same decision
(the question kept at Balanced; 14:19 has the "behold" form). 11:41 cites
《所罗门事迹录》, the Book of the Acts of Solomon. 2 Chronicles 1-9 and 10-36
retell 1 Kings 3-11 and 12-22: where the Hebrew is the same, they copy this
Chinese (*dn* = `{{divine_name:上主|雅威}}`, *gt* = `{{god_term:神|上帝}}`,
*he* = `{{deity_pronoun:他|祂}}`).

Address: subjects say 您 to the king at B and R with 我主我王 (L, B), 陛下 (R)
(Bathsheba, Nathan, the two mothers, Shimei, the assembly to Rehoboam, Ahab's
officials, the disguised prophet, Naboth); Bathsheba and Solomon say 你 as
mother and son (2:13-22); kings say 你 to each other (Hiram, the queen of
Sheba, Asa and Ben-hadad, Ahab and Jehoshaphat), except Ahab to his overlord
Ben-hadad (20:4, 9) and Ben-hadad's envoys suing for terms (20:32-34), who say
您; the widow of Zarephath, Obadiah and Elisha say 您 to Elijah; Elijah,
Micaiah and the man of God say 你 to the king when they bring the LORD's
word (13:6; 18:17-18; 21:20; 22:16-28), and the king to them.

### 2 Kings

2 Kings keeps the glossary's forms for every name and term above, the forms of
Genesis to Ruth for places, refrains and oaths, and 1 and 2 Samuel's forms for
offices (史官, 书记, 军队统帅) and address. The regnal formulas below are the
book's one wording per level and are binding for 1 Kings and for 1-2
Chronicles, which copy Kings where the Hebrew is the same. 18:13-20:19 is
drafted for Isaiah 36-39 to copy, and 24:18-25:30 for Jeremiah 52 (and 39:1-10),
wherever the Hebrew is the same; 19:21-28 keeps the English lines at each level.
Hezekiah's prayer (19:15-19; 20:3) is drafted afresh, and every clause that came
out in the Union Version's words was redrafted (*dn* =
`{{divine_name:上主|雅威}}`, *gt* = `{{god_term:神|上帝}}`, *he* =
`{{deity_pronoun:他|祂}}`):

| English | Literal | Balanced | Readable |
|---|---|---|---|
| In year N of X king of Judah, Y son of Z became king over Israel in Samaria, N years (3:1; 13:1, 10; 15:8-27; 17:1) | 犹大王X第N年，Z的儿子Y在撒玛利亚登基，作以色列的王，共作王N年 | 犹大王X在位第N年，Z的儿子Y在撒玛利亚登基，作以色列的王，作王N年 | X作犹大王的第N年，Z的儿子Y在撒玛利亚当了以色列的王，统治了N年 |
| In year N of X king of Israel, Y son of Z king of Judah began to reign (8:16, 25; 14:1; 15:1, 32; 16:1; 18:1) | 以色列王X第N年，犹大王Z的儿子Y登基作王 | 以色列王X在位第N年，犹大王Z的儿子Y登基作王 | Z的儿子Y当了犹大王，那是X作以色列王的第N年 |
| He was N years old when he began to reign, and he reigned N years in Jerusalem; his mother's name was M daughter of W of P (8:17, 26; 14:2; 15:2, 33; 18:2; 21:1, 19; 22:1; 23:31, 36; 24:8, 18) | 他登基的时候N岁，在耶路撒冷作王N年。他的母亲名叫M，是P人W的女儿 | as L | 他当王的时候N岁，在耶路撒冷统治了N年。他的母亲是P人W的女儿M |
| He did what was evil / right in the eyes of the LORD | 他做了在*dn*眼中为恶 / 为正的事 | 他做了*dn*看为恶 / 看为正的事 | 他做了*dn*不喜悦 / 喜悦的事 |
| as his father / his fathers had done | 像他父亲X所做的一样; 像他的列祖所做的一样 | 和他父亲X所做的一样; 和他的祖先所做的一样 | 就像他父亲X一样; 就像他的祖先一样 |
| walked in the way of X (8:18, 27; 16:3; 21:21-22; 22:2) | 行X的道路 | 走X的道路 | 像X那样生活 |
| He did not turn aside from the sins of Jeroboam son of Nebat, which he made Israel sin (3:3; 10:29, 31; 13:2, 6, 11; 14:24; 15:9, 18, 24, 28) | 没有偏离尼八的儿子耶罗波安使以色列犯罪的那些罪 | 没有离开尼八的儿子耶罗波安使以色列人犯罪的那些罪 | 没有离开尼八的儿子耶罗波安的罪，就是耶罗波安带领以色列人去犯的那些罪 |
| But the high places were not taken away; the people still sacrificed and made offerings on the high places (12:4; 14:4; 15:4, 35) | 只是丘坛没有废去，百姓仍在丘坛献祭烧香 | 然而丘坛还没有废除，百姓仍旧在丘坛献祭烧香 | 但是山上的祭坛没有被拆除，百姓仍然在山上的祭坛献祭烧香 |
| The rest of the acts of X and all that he did, are they not written in the Book of the Chronicles of the Kings of Judah / Israel? | X其余的事和他所做的一切，不都写在《犹大诸王的史记》上吗？ | as L | X所做的其他事，都记在《犹大诸王的史记》里 |
| … behold, they are written in … (hinnam; 15:11, 15, 26, 31) | ……看哪，都写在《以色列诸王的史记》上 | ……都写在《以色列诸王的史记》上 | ……都记在《以色列诸王的史记》里 |
| X slept with his fathers and was buried with his fathers in the city of David (his father) | X与他的列祖同睡，与他的列祖一同葬在大卫城 (他父亲大卫的城里) | X与他的祖先同睡，和他的祖先一同葬在大卫城 (他祖先大卫的城里) | X去世了，和他的祖先一同葬在大卫城 |
| Y his son reigned in his place | 他的儿子Y接续他作王 | as L | 他的儿子Y接替他当王 |
| provoked the LORD to anger (17:11, 17; 21:6, 15; 22:17; 23:19, 26) | 惹*dn*发怒 | as L | 惹*dn*生气 |
| according to the word of the LORD that he spoke by (the hand of) his servant N the prophet (9:36; 10:10; 14:25; 17:23; 24:2) | 正如*dn*的话，就是*he*借着*he*的仆人先知N的手所说的 | 正如*dn*借着*he*的仆人先知N所说的 | 这正如*dn*借着*he*的仆人先知N所说的 |
| As the LORD lives (2:2-6; 3:14; 4:30; 5:16, 20) | [我指着]永活的*dn*[起誓] | 我指着永活的*dn*起誓 | 我指着永活的*dn*发誓 |
| God do so to me, and more (6:31) | 愿*gt*这样对待我，并且愿*he*加倍对待我 | 愿*gt*严厉地惩罚我，而且罚得更重 | 愿*gt*严厉地惩罚我 |
| My father, my father! The chariots of Israel and its horsemen! (2:12; 13:14) | 我父啊，我父啊！以色列的战车和它的骑兵啊！ | 我父啊，我父啊！以色列的战车和骑兵啊！ | as B |
| Is it peace? (9:17-22, 31) | 是平安吗？ | 是平安吗？ | 你们是为和平而来的吗？ (你 for one) |

The source formula is a question at Balanced too, as the glossary has it, though
the English Balanced states it: the Hebrew asks a question expecting "yes", and
Chinese hears 不都……吗？ as a firm statement. Every record with the formula
carries the same decision. No 这些事 before 不都: the subject is already named.
The death formula has no `gender` span where the English has none (列祖 at L,
祖先 at B). 借着 (never 藉着) for "by, through" at every level.

Terms settled in 2 Kings:

| English | Chinese |
|---|---|
| the sons of the prophets (2; 4:1, 38; 5:22; 6:1; 9:1) | 先知的子弟 (L); 先知团 / 先知团的人 (B); 一群先知 / 那群先知 (R) |
| skin disease (5; 7:3-8; 15:5) | 严重的皮肤病; 患严重皮肤病的人, every level, as Leviticus 13-14 and Luke 4:27, with 麻风 named in a decision |
| Baal-zebub (1:2-16) | 巴力西卜, kept apart from the Gospels' 别西卜 (Beelzebul), with a decision |
| high places; Asherah pole; standing stones; host of heaven | 丘坛 (L, B), 山上的祭坛 (R); 亚舍拉木柱 (L, B), 用来敬拜亚舍拉的木柱 (R); 立石 (L, B), (敬拜用的)石柱 (R); 天上的万象 (L, B), 太阳、月亮和星星 (R) |
| the chest for the money (12:9-10) | 箱子 |
| commanders of hundreds (11:4-19) | 百人的首领 (L); 百人的官长 (B); 军官 (R), as 1 Samuel's 千人的首领 / 千人的官长; 百夫长 is kept for the New Testament's centurions (2 Samuel 18:1 has 百夫长, for a reviewer to bring into line) |
| the Carites; the runners (11:4, 19) | 迦利人; 侍卫 |
| chief of the guard (rav-tabbachim; 25:8-20) | 护卫长 (L, B); 卫队长 (R) |
| over the house (10:5; 18:18, 37; 19:2) | 管理王宫的 (L); 王宫总管 (B, R) |
| secretary; recorder (18:18; 22:3) | 书记 (L, B), 王室书记 (R); 史官 |
| the Tartan, the Rab-saris, the Rabshakeh (18:17) | 他珥探, 拉伯撒利, 拉伯沙基 (L); 大元帅, 宦官长, 首席长官 (B); 军队统帅, 王宫官员的首领, 亚述指挥官 (R); the Rabshakeh through 18-19: 拉伯沙基 / 首席长官 / 亚述指挥官 |
| the Fuller's Field (18:17) | 漂布田 (L, B); 洗布场 (R) |
| the head priest; the second priest (25:18) | 首席祭司 (L, B), 大祭司 (R); 次席祭司 (L), 副祭司 (B) |
| silver and gold by weight | units spans at B and R, only the measure in the span: shekel 11.5克 (`{{units:约575克\|五十舍客勒}}银子`); talent 约34公斤 (`{{units:约10吨\|三百他连得}}银子`); the money is weighed, so no wages |
| seah; kab (6:25; 7:1-18) | 细亚 (`{{units:约7升\|一细亚}}`); 卡布, a new transliteration (`{{units:约0.3升\|四分之一卡布}}`) |

### 1 Chronicles

1 Chronicles keeps the glossary's forms for every name and term above, the
forms of Genesis to Ruth for the patriarchs, tribes, clans and towns (1:1-54 as
Genesis 5, 10, 11, 25 and 36; the high priests of 5:29-41 and 6:35-38 as
Exodus, Numbers and Samuel), and the Kings and Chronicles formulas (Book notes)
in 29:26-30. The retellings of Samuel (10 = 1 Samuel 31; 11:1-9 = 2 Samuel
5:1-10; 11:10-41 = 2 Samuel 23:8-39; 13-14 = 2 Samuel 6:1-11 and 5:11-25;
15:25-16:3 = 2 Samuel 6:12-19; 17 = 2 Samuel 7; 18-20 = 2 Samuel 8, 10, 11:1,
12:26-31, 21:18-22; 21 = 2 Samuel 24) copy the harmonised Samuel Chinese at
each level wherever the Hebrew is the same, and differ, with a decision naming
the difference, where it is not (21:1 撒但 for 2 Samuel 24:1's *dn*; 13-16
*gt* where Samuel has *dn*; 17:14 "my house and my kingdom"; 18:12 Abishai and
Edom; 20:5 Lahmi). 29:27 copies 1 Kings 2:11. The minor names of the
genealogies are conventional forms, recorded in the decisions where they first
appear.

| English | Literal | Balanced | Readable |
|---|---|---|---|
| the sons of X: A, B and C (1-9) | X的儿子是A、B和C (X的儿子： before a "his son" chain or ordinals) | X的儿子是A、B和C | X的儿子是A、B和C |
| X begot Y | X生Y | X是Y的父亲 | X是Y的父亲 |
| "A his son, B his son" chains (3:10-14; 4:25-26; 5:4-5; 6:5-15, 35-38; 7:20-27; 8:37; 9:43) | 他的儿子A，他的儿子B | A的儿子B，B的儿子C | A的儿子B，B的儿子C |
| father of [a town] (2:24-52; 4:3-21; 8:29; 9:35) | X的父亲 | X的父亲 | 建立X的人 |
| mighty men of valor (gibbore chayil) | 大能的勇士 | 英勇的战士 | 勇敢的战士 |
| heads of fathers' houses | 父家的首领 | 家族的首领 | 家族的领袖 |
| commanders of thousands / hundreds | 千人的首领 / 百人的首领 | 千人的官长 / 百人的官长 | 统领一千人和一百人的军官 |
| officials (sarim of Israel) | 官长 | 官员 | 领袖 |

| the bread of the arrangement (9:32; 23:29; 28:16) | 陈设饼 (28:16 成排[饼]) | 陈设饼 | 摆在*gt*面前的饼 |
| temple servants (9:2) | 圣殿仆役 | 圣殿仆役 | 在圣殿里做杂役的仆人 |
| secretary (18:16; 24:6); scribes (2:55; 27:32) | 书记; 文士 | 书记; 文士 | 王室书记 / 书记; 文士 |

### 2 Chronicles

2 Chronicles keeps the glossary's forms for every name and term above, the
forms of Genesis to Ruth for places, tribes and refrains, 1 Chronicles' forms
for the Levites, singers, gatekeepers and divisions, and the regnal formulas of
the 2 Kings section (accession, age and mother, verdict, "walked in the way
of", high places, death and burial, succession): one wording per level, with
2 Kings' decision texts. Wherever the book retells Kings (1:14-17 = 1 Kings
10:26-29; 2-7 = 1 Kings 5-8; 8-9 = 1 Kings 9-10; 10-11 = 1 Kings 12; 18 = 1 Kings
22; 22:10-23:21 = 2 Kings 11; 24 = 2 Kings 12; 25 = 2 Kings 14; 26-28 = 2 Kings
15-16; 29-32 = 2 Kings 18-20; 33 = 2 Kings 21; 34-35 = 2 Kings 22-23; 36 = 2 Kings
23-25) it copies the Kings Chinese at each level where the Hebrew is the same,
and differs, with a decision naming the difference, where it is not (4:2 "他"
for Hiram; 5:4 the Levites; 6:5-6 "Jerusalem" and "a ruler"; 7:19 "forsake";
18:5 *gt* for "the Lord"; 33:7 "the idol" and *gt*; 34:20 Abdon son of Micah).
6:41-42 (Psalm 132:8-10) keeps the English \q lines at each level, drafted for
the Psalm to copy. 36:22-23 is drafted for Ezra 1:1-3 to copy (*dn* =
`{{divine_name:上主|雅威}}`, *gt* = `{{god_term:神|上帝}}`, *he* =
`{{deity_pronoun:他|祂}}`):

| English | Literal | Balanced | Readable |
|---|---|---|---|
| the book of the kings of Judah and Israel / of Israel and Judah (16:11; 25:26; 27:7; 28:26; 32:32; 35:27; 36:8) | 《犹大和以色列诸王记》 / 《以色列和犹大诸王记》 | as L | as L |
| the book of the kings of Israel (20:34); the matters of the kings of Israel (33:18) | 《以色列诸王记》; 《以色列诸王实录》 | as L | as L |
| the rest of the matters of X … are they not written (9:29; 12:15; 25:26) | X其余的事……不都写在……上吗？ | as L | X所做的其他事……都记在……里 |
| … look, they are written in … (16:11; 20:34; 27:7; 28:26; 32:32; 33:18; 35:27; 36:8) | 看哪，都写在《……》上 | 都写在《……》上 (其余的事, never 事迹) | 都记在《……》里 |
| the words of N the prophet / the seer (9:29; 12:15; 20:34; 33:19), a prophet's writing as a source | 先知N的言语中, no book-title marks | 先知N的记录里 | 记在先知N的记录里 |

| hear from heaven (min-hashamayim, 6:21-39; 7:14) | 从天上倾听 (6:27 在天上, where the Hebrew has no "from"); Kings' "in heaven" 在天上 | as L | as L |

| the people of the land (23:13-21; 26:21; 33:25; 36:1) | 国中的百姓 (33:25; 36:1); 23:13-21 那地所有的百姓, as 2 Kings 11 | 国中(所有)的百姓 | 国中 / 国内(所有)的百姓 |

| Abdon son of Micah (34:20) | 押顿, 米迦 (the prophet is 弥迦); 2 Kings 22:12 has Achbor son of Micaiah 亚革波, 米该雅 |
| Hozai (33:19) | 何赛 |
| Zerah the Cushite; Mareshah; Zephathah (14:8-9) | 古实人谢拉; 玛利沙; 洗法他 |
| Mount Zemaraim (13:4) | 洗玛脸山 |
| Sukkiim (12:3) | 苏基人 |
| Meunites (20:1; 26:7) | 米乌尼人 |
| Oded (15:1; 28:9) | 俄德 |
| Hanani the seer; Jehu son of Hanani (16:7; 19:2; 20:34) | 哈拿尼; 哈拿尼的儿子耶户 |
| Cyrus king of Persia (36:22-23) | 波斯王古列 |

Units, regnal formulas and offices not listed here are those of the 2 Kings
section above. Readers of 1 Kings 9:9 (背弃 B, 离开 R), 2 Kings 22:17 (离开 R),
2 Kings 23:30 (这地的百姓 L) and 2 Kings 25:18 (大祭司 R for the chief priest)
will find this book's forms differ; those verses are for a reviewer to bring
into line.

### Ezra

Ezra keeps the glossary's forms for every name and term above, 1 Chronicles'
forms for the priests, Levites, singers, gatekeepers and families, and the
Persian kings in their Bible forms (古列, 大利乌, 亚达薛西, 亚哈随鲁; the
historians' 居鲁士, 大流士, 薛西斯 only as alternatives in decisions). 1:1-3
copies 2 Chronicles 36:22-23 at each level, differing where the Hebrew does
(1:3 "his God" without *dn*; "to Jerusalem which is in Judah"). Ezra 2 and
Nehemiah 7:6-73 are the same list: the same names, numbers and formulas at
each level, differing only where the Hebrew does (names such as Jorah /
Hariph, Gibbar / Gibeon, the numbers, 2:68-69 / Nehemiah 7:70-72). 2:63
(= Nehemiah 7:65) is drafted fresh, away from the Union Version's 不可吃至圣的物.
The Aramaic letters (4:8-6:18; 7:12-26) take the same vocabulary as the
Hebrew narrative (*dn* = `{{divine_name:上主|雅威}}`, *gt* =
`{{god_term:神|上帝}}`, *he* = `{{deity_pronoun:他|祂}}`):

| English | Literal | Balanced | Readable |
|---|---|---|---|
| Beyond the River (4:10-20; 5:3-6; 6:6-13; 7:21, 25; 8:36) | 河外 | 河外省 (河外省的省长) | 幼发拉底河以西省份 |
| the sons of the exile (4:1; 6:16-21; 8:35; 10:7, 16) | 被掳之人的子孙 (gender span where the English has one) | 被掳归回的人 | 从被掳之地回来的人 |
| the exile (golah: 1:11; 9:4; 10:6, 8) | 被掳之民 / 被掳的人 | 被掳归回的人 | 从被掳之地回来的人 |
| the people of the land (4:4, singular) | 国中的百姓 (as 2 Kings 21:24; 2 Chronicles 36:1) | as L | as L |
| the peoples of the land(s) (3:3; 9:1-2, 11; 10:2, 11) | 各地的民族; 这地的民族 | 各地的民族; 这地的各民族 | 住在这地的人 / 各族 |
| the hand of the LORD his God was on him (7:6, 9, 28; 8:18, 31) | 照着*dn*他的*gt*的手在他身上 (7:9; 8:18 美好的手) | 因为*dn*他的*gt*的手护佑他 (施恩的手) | *dn*他的*gt*一直帮助他 (恩待……，帮助……) |
| the God of heaven (1:2; 5:12; 6:9-10; 7:12, 21, 23) | 天上的*gt* | as L | as L |
| the God of heaven and earth (5:11) | 天地之*gt* | as L | 天地的*gt* |
| "for he is good, his steadfast love toward Israel is forever" (3:11) | 因为*he*是美善的，*he*向以色列的慈爱永不止息 (1 Chronicles 16:34 with "toward Israel") | *he*是美善的；*he*对以色列的慈爱永不止息 | *he*真好！*he*对以色列的爱永远不变 |
| be strong and act (10:4) | 你要刚强，去做 (1 Chronicles 28:10) | 您要刚强，动手去做 | 您要坚强，动手去做吧 |
| foreign women / wives (10:2-44) | 外族女人 | 外族女子为妻 / 外族妻子 | as B |
| tribute, tax and toll (4:13, 20; 7:24) | 贡物、税款和关税 | 贡物、捐税和关税 | 任何税 / 各种税 |
| the most holy things (2:63) | 至圣之物 | 至圣的食物 | 属于祭司的最圣洁的食物 |
| the Tirshatha (2:63) | 提尔沙他 | 省长 | 省长 |
| "your servants" writing to the king; the king (4:11-16; 5:7-17) | 你; 王 | 您; 王 | 您; 陛下 |

Address: officials writing to Artaxerxes and Darius use 您 (B, R) and 陛下 (R);
the kings write to their officials and to Ezra with 你 / 你们; Shecaniah and
the assembly address Ezra with 您 (B, R; 10:4, 12).

### Nehemiah

Nehemiah keeps the glossary's forms for every name and term above, Ezra's
forms for the Persian kings and offices, the returnees, the priests, Levites,
singers, gatekeepers and temple servants, and 1 Chronicles' forms for the
priestly divisions (12:1-21 against 1 Chronicles 24). 7:6-73 is the list of
Ezra 2: the same names, numbers and formulas at each level, differing only
where the Hebrew does (Hariph / Jorah, Gibeon / Gibbar, Binnui / Bani,
Sia / Siaha, Addon / Addan, the numbers, the gatekeepers of 7:45, 7:69-72 /
Ezra 2:68-70); 7:65 copies Ezra 2:63 at every level. The prayers of 1 and 9
keep Exodus 34:6 and Deuteronomy 30:1-4 in their Pentateuch wording
(*dn* = `{{divine_name:上主|雅威}}`, *gt* = `{{god_term:神|上帝}}`, *he* =
`{{deity_pronoun:他|祂}}`, *you* = `{{deity_pronoun:你|祢}}`):

| English | Literal | Balanced | Readable |
|---|---|---|---|
| Beyond the River (2:7, 9; 3:7) | 河外 | 河外省 | 幼发拉底河以西省份 |
| the Tirshatha (7:65, 69; 8:9; 10:2) | 提尔沙他 | 省长 | 省长 |
| the God of heaven (1:4-5; 2:4, 20) | 天上的*gt* | as L | as L |
| the good hand of my God upon me (2:8, 18) | 照着我*gt*美好的手在我身上 | 我*gt*施恩的手护佑我 | 我的*gt*恩待我，帮助我 (as Ezra 7:9; 8:18) |
| Remember me, my God, for good (5:19; 13:31; with 13:14, 22) | 我的*gt*啊，求*you*为我记念……，使我得益处 | 我的*gt*啊，求*you*记念我……，使我蒙福 | 我的*gt*啊，求*you*记住我……，善待我 |
| Remember them / Tobiah, my God (6:14; 13:29) | 求*you*……记念他们 | as L | 求*you*记住…… |
| gracious and compassionate, slow to anger, great in steadfast love (9:17, 31) | 有恩惠，又怜恤，迟于发怒，满有慈爱 | 满有恩惠和慈悲，发怒迟缓，充满慈爱 | 满有仁慈和慈悲……不容易生气……始终不变的爱多么伟大 (Exodus 34:6) |
| your servant(s), Moses your servant (1:6-11; 2:20; 9:14; 10:30) | 仆人 at every level, though the English Literal says "slave" | | |
| the nobles; the officials (2:16; 4:8, 13; 5:7; 7:5) | 贵族; 官长 | 贵族; 官长 | 领袖; 官员 |
| the peoples of the land(s) (9:30; 10:29-32) | 各地的民族; 这地的民族 | as L | 住在这地的人, as Ezra |
| foreign women / wives (13:23-27) | 外族女人 | 外族妻子 | 外族妻子 |
| "May the king live forever" (2:3) | 愿王万岁 | as L | 祝陛下万岁 |

|---|---|

### Esther

Esther keeps the glossary's forms for every name and term above (亚哈随鲁, 以斯帖, 末底改, 哈曼, 书珊, 波斯, 玛代, 印度, 古实; 宦官; 省长; 史记 as the Kings formulas), 1 Chronicles' forms for Mordecai's forebears (睚珥, 示每, 基士), 1 Samuel 15's Agag (亚甲), Genesis 41:42's signet ring (印戒), and 2 Chronicles' words for the king's officers (sar: 首领 L, 官员 B, 领袖 R). The book never names God, and the Chinese adds no god_term or divine_name span: 4:14 "from another place" is 另一个地方 (L), 别处 (B), 别的地方 (R), with a decision saying so. Subjects speaking to the king (Memucan, the attendants, Haman, Esther) say 陛下 at B and R and 您 at R where its English addresses him directly; Literal keeps 王 and the Hebrew third person, and 王啊 with 你 where Esther speaks to him (7:3). Mordecai and Esther say 你 to each other. Settles these:

| English (first verse) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| Ahasuerus (1:1) | 亚哈随鲁, with a decision naming 薛西斯 |
| the provinces from India to Cush (1:1; 8:9) | 从印度直到古实的一百二十七个省 (L); 从印度到古实的一百二十七个省 (B); R 1:1 东起印度、南到埃及以南的古实 as its English |
| Susa the citadel (1:2 and throughout); the city of Susa (3:15; 8:15) | 书珊城堡 (L); 书珊的城堡 (B); 书珊的王家堡垒 (R); 书珊城 (L, B), 书珊城里的人 (R) |
| princes / officials / leaders (sar); the king's servants (1:3) | 首领 (L), 官员 (B), 领袖 (R); 臣仆 (L), 臣子 (B), 官员 (R) |
| nobles (1:3; 6:9); noble ladies (1:18) | 贵族; 贵妇 |
| feast, banquet (mishteh); the feast of wine (5:6; 7:2, 7-8) | 筵席 (L), 宴席 / 盛宴 (B), 宴会 (R); 酒席 (L), 喝酒的时候 (B, R) |
| Vashti (1:9) | 瓦实提 |
| eunuchs (1:10 and throughout) | 宦官 (L, B); 宫中官员 (R) |
| the seven eunuchs (1:10) | 米户曼, 比斯他, 哈波拿 (7:9 too), 比革他, 亚拔他, 西达, 甲迦 |
| the seven princes (1:14) | 甲示拿, 示达, 押玛他, 他施, 米力, 玛西拿, 米母干 |
| crown of royalty (1:11; 2:17; 6:8); royal clothing (5:1; 6:8-11; 8:15) | 王室的冠冕 (L), 后冠 (B, R; the queen's), 王冠 (B, R; on the horse); 王室[的]衣服 (L), 王后的礼服 (B, R; Esther), 王袍 (B, R) |

### Job

Job keeps the forms of the Pentateuch, the Psalter and the prophets for every name and term above (Sheol and the Pit as the table "Sheol and the Pit"; the wicked, the righteous, the upright and hesed as Psalms 1-41; Orion and the Pleiades as Amos 5:8; Rahab and Leviathan as Isaiah 27:1 and 51:9; the satan as Zechariah 3; 1 Corinthians 3:19 at 5:13 and Romans 11:35 at 41:3 where the Hebrew agrees), and settles these:

| English (first verse) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| Job; Uz (1:1) | 约伯; 乌斯地 |
| Eliphaz the Temanite, Bildad the Shuhite, Zophar the Naamathite (2:11) | 提幔人以利法, 书亚人比勒达, 拿玛人琐法 |
| Elihu son of Barachel the Buzite, of the clan of Ram (32:2) | 兰族布斯人巴拉迦别的儿子以利户 (L, B); 布斯人巴拉迦别的儿子……属于兰家族 (R) |
| Sabeans; Chaldeans (1:15-17); Tema, Sheba (6:19) | 示巴人; 迦勒底人; 提玛, 示巴 |
| Jemimah, Keziah, Keren-happuch (42:14) | 耶米玛, 基洗亚, 基连哈朴 |
| blameless (*tam*); integrity (*tummah*) (1:1; 2:3) | 纯全 at every level, for the whole book (1:8; 2:3, 9; 8:20; 9:20-22; 12:4; 22:3; 27:5; 31:6); "upright" 正直 beside it; "innocent" (*naqi*) 无辜 |
| "And X answered and said" (4:1) | X回答说： (L); 于是X回答： (B); 于是X说： (R); with the gentilic where the Hebrew has it (提幔人以利法); Elihu "continued" (36:1) 接着说 / 继续说; God "out of the storm" (38:1; 40:6) 从暴风中 |
| "Job again took up his discourse" (27:1; 29:1) | 约伯又接着讲论，说： (L); 约伯继续他的讲论，说： (B); 约伯又说： (R) |
| the satan / the Accuser (1:6-2:7) | 撒但 (L); 控告者 (B, R), as the English level has a name or a title (Zechariah 3) |
| the sons of God / heavenly beings (1:6; 2:1; 38:7) | *gt*的众子 (L, B, where the English keeps the phrase); 天上的众生 (R); the holy ones (5:1; 15:15) 圣者 / 众圣者 (L, B), 天上的众生 (R) |
| Shaddai, the Almighty (5:17) | 全能者 (L, B); at R 全能的*gt* where the English Readable says "God All-Powerful" (every occurrence in Job), 全能者 otherwise |
| Sheol (7:9) | 阴间 (L, B); 死亡之地 (R) |
| the Pit (*shaḥat*) (17:14; 33:18-30) | 坑 (L); 深坑 (B); 坟墓 (R, "the grave") |
| Abaddon (26:6; 28:22; 31:12) | 亚巴顿 (L, B, as the English keeps the name); 灭亡之地 (R) |
| deep darkness (*tsalmavet*) (3:5) | 死荫 (L, as Amos 5:8 and Psalm 107:10); 最深的黑暗 (B, as Amos 5:8; also R where the English Readable names it, 3:5; 10:22); otherwise R as the English Readable. "Gloom" (*ʾofel*) 幽暗 (L, B) |
| the Bear, Orion, the Pleiades (9:9; 38:31-32) | 大熊星, 参星, 昴星 at every level (Amos 5:8 昴星和参星); "the cluster of the Pleiades" (38:31 B) 昴星的星团; Mazzaroth (38:32) 玛撒录 (L), 众星座 (B), 各组星星 (R) |
| the chambers of the south (9:9) | 南方内室 (L); 南天的星群 (B); 南方天空的星星 (R) |
| Rahab (9:13; 26:12); Leviathan (3:8; 40:25) | 拉哈伯; 利维坦, as Isaiah 27:1 and 51:9; where the English Readable adds "the monster of the sea", 海怪拉哈伯 / 海怪利维坦; the sea monster (*tannin*, 7:12) 海怪 (L), as the English level (B, R) |
| Behemoth (40:15) | 比希莫 |
| wisdom (*ḥokmah*); understanding (*binah*, *tevunah*) (28:12, 20, 28) | 智慧; 悟性 (L, B), 明白事理 (R), as the glossary's Proverbs forms |
| the fear of the Lord (28:28) | 敬畏主 (L, B); 尊敬并畏惧主 (R); 主 for *ʾAdonai*, no span |
| the wicked; the righteous; the upright | 恶人 (L, B), 坏人 (R); 义人 (L, B), 好人 / 走正路的人 (R, as the English Readable); 正直的人 |
| God's hesed (10:12; 37:13); hesed between people (6:14) | 慈爱 (L, B), 始终不变的爱 (R); 慈爱 (L), as the English level (B, R) |
| Redeemer (*goʾel*, 19:25; marked uncertain) | 我的救赎主 (L, B); 那位要为我辩护的（我的救主） (R), as the glossary's Redeemer of God; drafted fresh, apart from the Union Version |
| pronouns for God | a `deity_pronoun` span only where the English level has one; a sentence-initial "He" / "You" the English leaves unspanned is plain 他 / 你 or God is named (42:3-4); God's 我 never takes a span |

Famous passages (1:21; 19:25-27; 28:28; 38:1-11; 42:5-6) are drafted fresh: no clause follows the Union Version (19:27 R, 28:28 L, 38:2 L, 38:7 B and R, 38:10-11, 42:5 R were redrafted where a clause came close to it).

### Psalms 1-41

Book One of the Psalter keeps the glossary's tables for every name and term above (the Psalm titles, musical terms and Selah table; hesed; torah; Sheol and the Pit; nations and peoples; the names of God), copies 2 Samuel 22 into Psalm 18 wherever the Hebrew is the same, and keeps the New Testament's Chinese where it quotes these psalms in a Greek that says what the Hebrew says (2:1-2, 7 in Acts 4 and 13 and Hebrews 1; 8:3, 5-7 in Matthew 21 and Hebrews 2; 14:1-3 in Romans 3; 16:8-11 in Acts 2; 19:5 in Romans 10; 22:2, 19, 23 in Matthew 27, John 19 and Hebrews 2; 31:6 in Luke 23:46; 32:1-2 in Romans 4; 34:9, 13-17 in 1 Peter 2-3; 35:19 in John 15:25; 40:7-9 in Hebrews 10; 41:10 in John 13:18). Pronouns for God take a `deity_pronoun` span only where the English level has one (a sentence-initial He or You is plain 他 / 你, and so is a pronoun Chinese supplies, as in 求你……). Psalms 1, 19, 23, 27 and 32 were drafted afresh, and every clause that came out in the Union Version's words was redrafted. One wording per level (*dn* = `{{divine_name:上主|雅威}}`, *gt* = `{{god_term:神|上帝}}`):

| English | Literal | Balanced | Readable |
|---|---|---|---|
| How blessed (*ʾashrei*; 1:1; 2:12; 33:12; 34:9; 40:5; 41:2) | ……的人何等有福 / 何等有福啊，……的人 | as Literal | ……的人，人生真是美好 (as Daniel 12:12). 32:1-2 keep Romans 4:7-8 |
| For the director (*lamnatseach*) | 交给指挥：X。 / 交给指挥。 | 交给指挥。 | 给诗班指挥的。 |
| A psalm of David (*mizmor ledavid*) | 大卫的诗 | 大卫的诗 | 大卫的歌 |
| Of David (alone); A psalm (alone) | 大卫的; 一首诗 | 大卫的; 一首诗 | 大卫作; 一首歌 |
| Arise, LORD (3:8; 7:7; 9:20; 10:12; 17:13) | 起来，*dn*啊！ | *dn*啊，起来吧！ | *dn*啊，快起来！ |
| How long (*ʿad-ʾanah*, *ʿad-matay*; 4:3; 6:4; 13:2-3; 35:17) | 到几时 | 要到几时 | 还要多久 |
| take refuge in (*ḥasah*) | 投靠 | 以……为避难所 | 跑到……那里寻求保护 |

| the upright (of heart) (*yashar*; 7:11; 11:2; 32:11; 36:11) | 心里正直的人 (L, B); 正直诚实的人 (R); the plain adjective 正直 / 正确 |

| torah of the LORD; testimonies, precepts, commandment, judgments (19:8-10) | as Psalm 119: 律法 (L, B), 教导 (R); 见证 (L), 法度 (B); 训示, 诫命, 判语 (L, B) |

| of the servant of the LORD (18:1; 36:1) | *dn*的仆人大卫的 (L, B); *dn*的仆人大卫作 (R); 18:1 R 服事*dn*的人, as the English Readable |
| on Ayyeleth-hashshahar (22:1) | 按亚耶利沙哈 (L, B); 按“黎明的母鹿”的曲调 (R) |
| a song for the dedication of the house (30:1) | 为献殿而作的歌 (L, B); R says to whom, as the English Readable |
| maskil (32:1) | 马斯基勒 (L, B); 一首教导人的歌 (R) |
| to bring to remembrance (38:1) | 为了提醒 (L); 作记念用 (B); 为了让人记起 (R) |
| Selah (3:3 onward) | 细拉, at the end of its verse at every level |

| English (first verse) | Chinese |
|---|---|

| Cush, a Benjaminite (7:1) | 古实, 便雅悯人 |
| Abimelech (34:1) | 亚比米勒 (as Genesis 20; the title probably names Achish) |
| Jeduthun (39:1) | 耶杜顿 (as 1 Chronicles 16:41) |
| Lebanon; Sirion (29:5-6) | 黎巴嫩; 西连 |
| the wilderness of Kadesh (29:8) | 加低斯的旷野 |

Address: the psalmist says 你 to God at every level, in a `deity_pronoun` span only where the English level spans the pronoun; God's own 我 takes no span. The people say 您 to the king at Balanced and Readable in Psalm 20 (20:2-6), and the psalmist says 你们 to the mockers and the nations. Psalm 18 keeps 2 Samuel 22's address and wording; there the two differ only where the Hebrew differs (18:2, 7, 11-16, 26-29, 33, 36, 42-51), each with a decision, and in the `deity_pronoun` spans, which follow each book's English.

### Psalms 42-72

Book Two of the Psalter keeps Book One's forms for every name and term above (titles and Selah, the wicked and the righteous, enemies and foes, hesed, faithfulness, refuge and fortress, the nations and the peoples, Sheol, salvation at Literal), copies Psalm 14 into Psalm 53 and Psalm 40:14-18 into Psalm 70 wherever the Hebrew is the same, and keeps 71:1-3 in the words of 31:2-4. It keeps the New Testament's Chinese where it quotes these psalms in a Greek that says what the Hebrew says (44:23 in Romans 8:36; 45:7-8 in Hebrews 1:8-9; 69:5 in John 15:25; 69:10 in John 2:17 and Romans 15:3), and translates the Hebrew with a decision naming the New Testament's form where its Greek differs (51:6 and Romans 3:4; 68:19 and Ephesians 4:8, "gave gifts"; 69:22 and Matthew 27:34; 69:23-24 and Romans 11:9-10; 69:26 and Acts 1:20). Psalm 108 copies 57:8-12 and 60:7-14. Psalms 46 and 51 were drafted afresh, and every clause that came out in the Union Version's words was redrafted (46:9, 46:11; 51:5, 51:11, 51:14). The king in Psalm 45 is addressed with `deity_pronoun` spans, as its English and Hebrews 1:8-9 have him. Selah has one decision for the book (44:9). One wording per level (*dn* = `{{divine_name:上主|雅威}}`, *gt* = `{{god_term:神|上帝}}`):

|---|---|---|---|
| of the sons of Korah (42:1; 44-49) | 可拉子孙的 | as Literal | 可拉家族的 ("of"); 可拉家族作 ("by", "from"); 一首配乐的歌，可拉家族作 (48:1, as 88:1) |
| maskil (42:1; 44:1; 45:1; 52-55) | 马斯基勒 | as Literal | ……的歌，用来教导人，使人明白事理; alone 一首歌，用来教导人，使人明白事理 |
| miktam (56-60) | 米克坦 | as Literal | 歌 |
| "Do not destroy" (57-59) | 交给指挥：“不要毁坏。” | 按“不要毁坏”的曲调 | 按“不要毁坏”的曲调 |
| on shoshannim (45:1; 69:1); on shushan eduth (60:1) | 按书珊宁; 按书珊伊杜特 (80:1 按书珊宁伊杜特) | as Literal | 按“百合花”的曲调; 按“盟约的百合花”的曲调 |

| Of David; Of Solomon (68-70; 72:1) | 大卫的; 所罗门的 | as Literal | 大卫作; 所罗门作. "A song (by) David" 大卫的歌 |

### Psalms 73-89

Book Three of the Psalter keeps the glossary's tables and the forms settled in Psalms 1-41 for every name and term above (titles, musical terms and Selah; hesed; torah; Sheol and the Pit; nations and peoples; the names of God). Its retellings keep the Chinese of the books they retell: Psalm 78 the plagues, the sea, the manna and the quail of Exodus (78:13 as Exodus 15:8; 78:45 虫群 / 苍蝇 as Exodus 8), Psalm 81 the Exodus and Meribah (Exodus 17:7), Psalm 83 the names of Judges 4-8 (西西拉, 耶宾, 基顺, 俄立, 西伊伯, 西巴, 撒慕拿) and 1 Samuel 28:7 (隐多珥); Psalm 89 keeps 2 Samuel 7:12-16's verbs where the promise to David is the same (坚立, 稳固, 长存; 杖 and 鞭打; "my loyal love will not depart" as 7:15). Psalm 79:6-7 keeps Jeremiah 10:25, and 86:15 Exodus 34:6. The New Testament's Chinese is kept where its Greek says what the Hebrew says: 78:2 line 1 (Matthew 13:35), 82:6 "我说过：‘你们是神明" (John 10:34), 86:9 (Revelation 15:4: 万国都要来，在你面前敬拜 at L and B, 都要来敬拜你 at R), 89:21 "我寻见了 / 我找到了" (Acts 13:22) and 89:28 地上众君王 (Revelation 1:5); 78:24 keeps John 6:31's frame but says "the grain of heaven" with the Hebrew, where John has "bread". Pronouns for God take a `deity_pronoun` span only where the English level has one; God's own 我 takes none. One wording per level (*dn* = `{{divine_name:上主|雅威}}`, *gt* = `{{god_term:神|上帝}}`):

| Of the sons of Korah | 可拉子孙的 | 可拉子孙的 | 可拉家族作 ("By / From the family of Korah") |

Book Two's titles, not changed here, differ in places: Psalm 60:1 transliterates eduth 伊度 (Psalm 80:1 伊杜特), and Psalms 45:1 and 69:1 write the Readable tune 用“百合花”的曲调 where Book Three writes 按“X”的曲调.

### Psalms 90-106

Book Four of the Psalter keeps the forms of Psalms 1-41 for every name and term above (titles, hesed, the wicked and the righteous, the upright, foes and enemies, refuge and fortress, nations and peoples, the torah words, God's people, "Bless the LORD, my soul"), copies 1 Chronicles 16 where the Hebrew is the same (96 = 1 Chronicles 16:23-33; 105:1-15 = 16:8-22; 106:1, 47-48 = 16:34-36), and keeps the New Testament's Chinese where it quotes these psalms in a Greek that says what the Hebrew says (91:11-12 in Matthew 4:6 and Luke 4:10-11; 95:7-11 in Hebrews 3:7-11; 102:26-28 in Hebrews 1:10-12; 104:4 at Literal, Hebrews 1:7). Where the New Testament follows the Greek (95:8 "the rebellion", 95:10 "I was angry", 102:27 "roll them up", 97:7 "angels", 94:11 in 1 Corinthians 3:20), the psalm is translated from the Hebrew and a decision names the New Testament form. Pronouns for God take a `deity_pronoun` span only where the English level has one, so a copied line can differ from Chronicles in its spans (96:10 R; 105:7, 8, 10, 11, 14; 106:1 R, 48 R), and each copy's decision says so. Psalms 90, 91 and 103 were drafted afresh and checked clause by clause against the Union Version. One wording per level (*dn* = `{{divine_name:上主|雅威}}`, *gt* = `{{god_term:神|上帝}}`):

| English | Literal | Balanced | Readable |
|---|---|---|---|
| *dn* reigns (*YHWH malakh*; 93:1; 96:10; 97:1; 99:1) | *dn*作王 | *dn*是君王 | *dn*是君王 (also for "rules as King", 93:1), as 1 Chronicles 16:31 |
| Of David (101:1; 103:1); A psalm (92:1; 98:1; 101:1) | 大卫的; 一首诗 | 大卫的; 一首诗 | 大卫作; 一首歌 |
| A prayer of Moses, the man of God (90:1) | *gt*的人摩西的祷告 | as Literal | *gt*的人摩西作的祷告 |
| A psalm for thanksgiving (100:1) | 一首称谢的诗 | 一首为称谢而作的诗 | 一首感谢的歌 |
| Sing to *dn* a new song (96:1; 98:1) | 向*dn*唱新的歌 | 要向*dn*唱一首新歌！ | 要向*dn*唱一首新歌！ |
| Bless *dn*, my soul (103:1, 2, 22; 104:1, 35) | 我的灵魂哪，要称颂*dn* | 我的灵魂啊，称颂*dn*吧 | 我要用我的全部生命赞美*dn* |

Address: the psalmist says 你 to God, with a `deity_pronoun` span only where the English level spans the pronoun; a sentence-initial He or You the English leaves plain is a plain 他 / 你, never 这位. God's own 我 (91:14-16; 95:8-11; 105:11, 15) takes no span. Psalm 101 is the king speaking; Psalm 91:9b and 91:14-16 change speaker, as the meaning decisions say.

### Psalms 107-150

Book Five of the Psalter keeps the glossary's tables and the forms settled in Psalms 1-41 for every name and term above (titles, musical terms and Selah; hesed; the torah words of Psalm 119; Sheol; nations and peoples; the names of God). It copies 1 Chronicles 16:34 for the refrain "his loyal love is forever" (107:1; 118:1-4, 29; 136 throughout; 138:8 spoken to God), Psalms 57:8-12 and 60:7-14 into Psalm 108, 115:4-8 into 135:15-18, and 2 Chronicles 6:41-42 into 132:8-10, wherever the Hebrew is the same. 144:3 keeps the frame of 8:5, and 144:1-2, 5-7 the Chinese of Psalm 18. It keeps the New Testament's Chinese where it quotes these psalms in a Greek that says what the Hebrew says: 109:8 in Acts 1:20; 110:1 in Acts 2:34-35 and Hebrews 1:13; 110:4 in Hebrews 5:6; 112:9 in 2 Corinthians 9:9; 118:6 in Hebrews 13:6, second half; 118:22-23, 26 in Matthew 21:42 and 21:9; 140:4 in Romans 3:13. Where the Greek differs (116:10 in 2 Corinthians 4:13; 117:1 in Romans 15:11; 143:2 in Romans 3:20), the Hebrew is translated and a decision names the New Testament form. Pronouns for God take a `deity_pronoun` span only where the English level has one: this applies in the refrain too, so the Readable "他的爱永远不变" is plain. Psalms 121 and 139 and 119:105 were drafted afresh, and every clause that came out in the Union Version's words was redrafted (121:6-7, 139:14, 119:105). One wording per level (*dn* = `{{divine_name:上主|雅威}}`, *gt* = `{{god_term:神|上帝}}`):

| English | Literal | Balanced | Readable |
|---|---|---|---|
| the refrain (*ki leʿolam ḥasdo*) | ……，因为他*是美善的， 他*的慈爱永不止息 (因为 before the refrain where the English says "for") | as Literal | ……，因为他*真好！ 他的爱永远不变 (他 spanned only where the English is) |
| Let Israel say now (118:2-4; 124:1; 129:1) | 让以色列现在说 | 让以色列现在说出来 | as the English level |
| How blessed (*ʾashrei*; 112:1; 119:1-2; 127:5; 128:1; 137:8-9; 144:15; 146:5) | ……的人何等有福 / 何等有福啊，……的人 | as Literal | ……的人，人生真是美好 |
| Hallelu Yah | 你们要赞美*dn* (Yah) | as Literal | 要赞美*dn* (Yah) |
| A song of ascents (120-134) | 上行之诗 | 上行之诗 | 上耶路撒冷朝圣时唱的诗 |
| Of David; Of Solomon (alone) | 大卫的; 所罗门的 | as Literal | 大卫作; 所罗门作 |
| Of David. A psalm. (109:1; 110:1; 139:1) | 大卫的。一首诗。 | as Literal | 大卫作。一首歌。 / 大卫的歌。 as the English |

| English | Chinese |
|---|---|

| Adonai alone (110:5; 130:2-6) | 主, no span |
| the faithful (*ḥasidim*; 116:15; 132:9, 16; 145:10; 148:14; 149:1, 5, 9) | 忠信的人 (L, as 30:5); 忠心的子民 (B, R, "faithful people", as 2 Chronicles 6:41); 忠于……的人 where the English says "loyal to" |
| the upright (*yesharim*; 107:42; 111:1; 112:2, 4; 125:4; 140:14) | 正直的人 (L, B), 心里正直的人 for the upright in heart; 正直诚实的人 (R, "honest people"), 走正路的人 where the English Readable says "people who do what is right" |

| the son of man (144:3) | 人子 (L); `{{gender:凡人\|人的儿子}}` (B); `{{gender:渺小的凡人\|人的儿子}}` (R), as 8:5 |

### Proverbs

Proverbs keeps the Psalter's forms for the shared Hebrew (the wicked 恶人 / 坏人, the righteous 义人 / 走正路的人, the upright, hesed, Sheol and the Pit, how blessed 何等有福 / 人生真是美好), the prophets' massa forms, and the glossary's Proverbs table under Book notes. This section writes that table out per level and settles the rest. Where it differs from the table, it updates it: the rows marked *updates* replace the table's row.

| English (first verse) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| wisdom (*ḥokmah*, 1:2); the wise (*ḥakham*) | 智慧 (every level); 智慧人 (L, B), 有智慧的人 (R); "wise in his own eyes" 自以为有智慧 (B) |
| understanding (*binah*, *tevunah*, 1:2); the discerning (*navon*, *mevin*) | 悟性 (L, B), 明白事理 (R); 有悟性的人 (L, B), 明白事理的人 (R), never 明辨的人 or 见识 for these words |
| knowledge (*daʿat*, 1:4) | 知识 (every level); 认识 for knowing God (2:5; 9:10) |
| discipline, instruction (*musar*, 1:2) | 管教 (L, B) also where the English Balanced says "correction"; 纠正 (R); *updates*: the table's row now applies at B whatever the English word |
| prudence, the prudent (*ʿormah*, *ʿarum*, 1:4) | 精明, 精明的人 (every level) |
| discretion (*mezimmah*, 1:4) | 谋略 (L); 远见 / 周密筹划的能力 / 周密的计划 (B, as the English); 周全的计划 / 好好计划 (R). A person of schemes 心怀诡计的人 (L) |
| sound wisdom (*tushiyyah*, 2:7) | 真智慧 (L, B), also where the English Balanced says "good sense" or "sound judgment" (8:14; 18:1); 明智 or as the English (R) |
| good sense (*sekel*, 12:8) | 见识 |
| the fool: *kesil* (1:22), *ʾewil* (1:7), *nabal* (17:7) | 愚昧人 (L, B); 愚蠢的人 (R), with a decision naming the Hebrew word; folly 愚昧 (L, B), 愚蠢 (R); *updates*: the table's row also covers *nabal* |
| brutish (*baʿar*, 12:1; 30:2) | its own word: 蠢笨的 (L), 愚笨如兽 (B), 笨人 (R) |
| the scoffer (*lets*, 1:22) | 好讥笑人的 (L, B); 爱嘲笑别人的人 (R) |
| the simple (*peti*, 1:4) | 无知的人 (L, B); 容易受骗的人 or as the English (R) |
| the sluggard (*ʿatsel*, 6:6) | 懒惰的人 (every level); "O sluggard" 懒惰的人哪 (L, B), 你这懒惰的人 (R) |
| my son (*beni*, 1:8); sons (4:1) | 我儿 (L, B), 孩子 (R); 众儿子啊 (L), 儿子们 (B), 孩子们 (R) |
| the strange woman (*zarah*, 2:16) | 外女 (L); 不属于你的女人 (B, as the English Balanced "the woman who is not yours"); 别人的妻子 / 放荡的女人 (R, as the English); *updates*: the table's "B, R" becomes R alone |
| the foreign woman (*nokhriyyah*, 2:16) | 外来的女子 (L); 外来的女人 (B); R as the English |
| the righteous; the wicked (2:20-22) | 义人 (L, B), 走正路的人 (R), 好人 where the English Readable says "good people"; 恶人 (L, B), 坏人 (R) |
| the fear of the LORD (1:7) | 敬畏*dn* (L, B); 尊敬并畏惧*dn* (R); 1:7 L "……是知识的起点" |
| an abomination to the LORD (3:32) | *dn*所憎恶的 (L); *dn*所厌恶的 / *dn*厌恶…… (B); *dn*恨恶…… (R) |
| lacking heart (6:32) | 缺少心智 (L); 缺少理智 / 毫无理智 (B); R as the English (没有头脑, 愚蠢的) |
| a wife of strength (12:4; 31:10) | 刚强的妻子 (L); 贤能的妻子 (B); 又坚强又能干的妻子 (R), one form in both verses; 31:29 刚强 / 贤能. Not the Union Version's 才德的妇人 |
| the Holy One (9:10; 30:3) | 那圣者 (L); 那位圣者 (B); 圣洁的*gt* (R) |
| massa (30:1; 31:1) | 默示 (L); 信息 (B, R), uncertain: 玛撒 if it is the people of Genesis 25:14 |
