Repeated proverbs have one Chinese wherever the Hebrew and the English agree: 14:12 = 16:25; 18:8 = 26:22; 21:9 = 25:24; 6:10-11 = 24:33-34 (Literal differs where the Hebrew does); 20:16 = 27:13 (L, B); 22:3 = 27:12; 22:28 = 23:10 (first line, L, B); 9:4 = 9:16; 19:5 = 19:9 and 10:6 = 10:11 (shared line); 19:1 = 28:6 and 8:35 = 18:22 (shared line).

New Testament quotations: 3:11-12 keep Hebrews 12:5's 我儿 and 管教 with verbs from the Hebrew, the decision naming Hebrews' Greek reading; 3:34's second line is James 4:6 (1 Peter 5:5) at each level, the first from the Hebrew; 10:12 keeps 1 Peter 4:8's verb 遮盖 (L, B); 11:31 is translated from the Hebrew, naming 1 Peter 4:18; 25:21-22 keep Romans 12:20 (B word for word); 26:11's dog line is 2 Peter 2:22 at each level. 1:7, 3:5-6, 4:23, 16:18, 22:6 and 31:10-31 are drafted fresh, with no clause from the Union Version.

### Ecclesiastes

Ecclesiastes keeps the glossary's Book notes (柯希列 / 传道者; "under the sun" 日光之下 / 在这世上), the Psalter's forms for the righteous and the wicked, ashrei (10:17), Sheol and hesed, and Proverbs' forms for the fool, the wise and "my son" (12:12). It follows `docs/ecclesiastes-song-terms.md` for the English levels, and settles these. 1:2, 3:1-8 and 12 are drafted fresh, with no clause of the Union Version:

| English (first verse) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| hevel, breath (1:2) | 气息 (L); 一口气 (B), as Psalms 39 and 144; R as the English: 像一口气 with the sense spelled out (转眼就散了, 不会长久, 我抓不住它, 叫人想不通). The glossary's 虚幻 / 一场空 and the familiar 虚空 are not used |
| "this too is breath" (1:14 and through the book) | 这也是气息 (L); 这也不过是一口气 (B); 这也像一口气 (R) |
| havel havalim (1:2; 12:8) | 气息中的气息 (L); 一口气，又一口气 (B); 没有什么能长久！……一切都像一口气，转眼就散了 (R); 12:8 repeats 1:2 at each level |
| Qoheleth (1:1) | 柯希列 (L); 传道者 (B, R), where the English has "the Teacher"; "says Qoheleth" 柯希列说 (L), 传道者这样说 (B, R), not the familiar 传道者说 |
| under the sun (1:3); under the heavens (1:13) | 日光之下 (L, B), 在这世上 (R); 天底下 (L, B), 在这世上 (R) |
| chasing the wind, re'ut / ra'yon ruach (1:14, 17) | 追逐风 (L); 追风 (B); 像是想抓住风一样 (R). 捕风 is not used |
| yitron, gain (1:3); motar, advantage (6:8) | 益处 (L), 收获 (B), 好处 (R); 优势 (L, B), 好处 (R) |
| 'amal, toil (1:3) | 劳碌 (L); 辛苦工作 (B, R) |
| chelek, portion (2:10) | 份 (L); 应得的份 (B); 人生给我（们）的 (R) |
| "God gives" (1:13; 2:26; 3:10; 5:18; 6:2; 8:15; 9:9; 12:7) | *gt*赐给 (L, B); *gt*给 (R) |
| eat and drink and find enjoyment (2:24; 3:13; 5:17) | 吃、喝，并……在劳碌中享受美好 (L); 吃喝，在辛苦工作中享受乐趣 (B); 吃喝，享受自己辛苦工作带来的快乐 (R); "eat, drink and be glad" (8:15) 吃、喝、欢乐 (L), 吃喝、快乐 (B, R) |
| the gift of God (3:13; 5:18) | *gt*所赐的礼物 (L, B); *gt*给的礼物 (R) |
| fear God (3:14; 5:6; 7:18; 8:12-13; 12:13) | 敬畏*gt* (L, B); 尊敬并畏惧*gt* (R), the glossary's forms for the fear of the LORD |
| 'olam, eternity (3:11) | 永恒 (L); 对永恒的意识 (B); 对永远长存之事的渴望 (R); uncertain, as the English; 永生 is not used |
| ruach as life-breath, spirit (3:19-21; 12:7); breath of life (11:5) | 灵 at every level, never 气息; 11:5 气 (L), 生命的气息 (B, R) |
| fate (2:14; 3:19; 9:2-3) | 结局 (L); 命运 (B); "the same thing happens" / 结局 (R) |
| kesil, fool; the wise (2:14) | 愚昧人 (L, B), 愚蠢的人 (R); 智慧人 (L, B), 有智慧的人 (R) |
| folly; madness (1:17) | 愚昧 (L, B), 愚蠢 (R); 疯狂 |
| "I said in my heart" (1:16; 2:1) | 我同我的心说 / 我在心里说 (L); 我对自己说 (B, R) |
| "set my heart" (1:13, 17) | 用心 (L); 专心 (B); 决定 (R) |
| an evil task (1:13; 4:8) | 恶劣差事 (L); 苦差事 (B); 又辛苦又不愉快的差事 / 不愉快的差事 (R) |
| a sickening evil (5:12, 15) | 令人痛心的恶事 (L); 令人痛心的不幸 (B); 非常可悲的事 (R) |
| the times of 3:2-8 | ……的时候和……的时候 (L); 该……的时候，该……的时候 (B); 有……的时候，也有……的时候 (R); the familiar ……有时 is not used |
| woe (4:10; 10:16) | 有祸了 (L, B); 那真是太可怜了 / 你会多么可怜 (R), as the English "how terrible" |
| officials; slaves (10:7, 16-17) | 官员 (L, B), 领袖 (R); 奴隶 at every level |
| your Creator (12:1) | 你的创造者 (L, B); 那位造你的*gt* (R). The "Remember him" the English Readable repeats (12:2, 6) is 要记得*gt* |
| "before" in 12:1-7 | 趁着……还没有 (L, B); 在……以前 (R) |
| goads; one shepherd (12:11) | 刺棒 (L, B), 赶牛用的尖棍子 (R); 一位牧人, no capital |
| my son (12:12) | 我儿 (L, B); 孩子 (R) |
| the conclusion (12:13) | 要敬畏*gt*，持守 / 遵行{{deity_pronoun:他\|祂}}的诫命; 人的全部 (L), 做人的全部意义 (B), 每个人一生的意义所在 (R); 人所当尽的本分 is not used |

Address: the reader is 你 at every level (4:17 onward). The gender spans of the English Literal (a person / a man; children of humankind / sons of men) have no Chinese counterpart: 人, 世人 and the generic 他 are already inclusive, with a setting_alternative decision once in each chapter. Pronouns for God are spanned exactly where the English spans them; where the English starts a sentence with an unspanned "He" (3:11; 7:14; 11:5), Chinese names God again. No new names.

### Song of Songs

The Song of Songs keeps the glossary's forms for every name and term above (Solomon 所罗门, Pharaoh 法老, Jerusalem, Zion, Lebanon 黎巴嫩, Gilead 基列, Damascus 大马士革, Carmel 迦密山; the lovers say 你, as the book note sets; Sheol as Genesis 37:35), and the forms of earlier books for places and plants (Tirzah 得撒 as 1 Kings 14:17; Heshbon 希实本 as Deuteronomy 1:4; Senir 示尼珥 and Hermon 黑门 as Deuteronomy 3:9; Mahanaim 玛哈念 as Genesis 32:3; En-gedi 隐基底 as 1 Samuel 24:1; Sharon 沙仑 as Acts 9:35; Kedar 基达 as Genesis 25:13; nard 哪达 as John 12:3; mandrakes 风茄 as Genesis 30:14; desire 渴望 as Genesis 3:16), and settles these. As in English, no speaker labels are added and speeches take no quotation marks, except a speech quoted inside a speech (2:10-13; 3:3; 5:2-3; 6:10).

| English (first verse) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| The Song of Songs (1:1) | 歌中之歌 (L); 歌中之歌，就是最美的歌 (B); 所有歌中最美的歌 (R); the book's name stays 雅歌 |

| beloved ones (*dodim*, 5:1) | 亲爱的人们 (L); 恋人们 (B, R) |

| my companion / my darling (*raʿyati*, 1:9) | 我的伴侣 (L); 我的爱侣 (B, R); not 佳偶 |

| love (*ʾahavah*: the refrain, 8:6-7) | 爱情; 爱 where Chinese needs it (2:4 the banner, 2:5 sick with love, 3:10) |

| my sister, bride (4:9-5:1) | 我的妹妹，新娘 (L); 我的妹妹，我的新娘 (B); 我那亲如妹妹的新娘 (R); "my sister" alone (5:2) 我的妹妹 (L, B), 我最亲爱的 (R) |
| my perfect one (5:2; 6:9) | 我那完美的人 |

| awaken (8:5, the refrain's verb) | 唤醒 at every level |

The famous lines of 8:6-7 are drafted fresh: no clause follows the traditional Chinese wording. Headings and chapter summaries use the Balanced forms (心上人, 耶路撒冷的女子们).

### Isaiah

Isaiah keeps the glossary's forms for every name and term above, the
prophets' formula table (one wording per level), and the forms of Genesis to
2 Chronicles for places, peoples and kings. 36:1-39:8 copies the harmonised
2 Kings 18:13-20:19 at every level where the English is the same (102 levels
match word for word), and differs only where the Hebrew does (37:25 "water",
2 Kings "foreign water"; 38:1-8, 21-22 against 2 Kings 20:1-11; Hezekiah's
song 38:9-20 is Isaiah's own). New Testament quotations keep the New
Testament's Chinese where the Hebrew says the same and translate the Hebrew,
with a decision naming the New Testament form, where the New Testament follows
the Greek (6:9-10; 7:14; 8:23-9:1; 11:10; 40:3-5; 42:1-4; 49:6; 52:15-53:12;
58:6 and 61:1-2 as Luke 4:18-19; 62:11 as Matthew 21:5). *dn* =
`{{divine_name:上主|雅威}}`, *gt* = `{{god_term:神|上帝}}`.

**Formulas** (the prophets copy these):

| English | Literal | Balanced | Readable |
|---|---|---|---|
| Thus says the LORD (*koh ʾamar YHWH*), also with titles between ("Thus says the LORD, your Redeemer, the Holy One of Israel") | *dn*如此说 (……，如此说) | *dn*这样说 | *dn*说 |
| thus the LORD said to me | *dn*如此对我说 | *dn*这样对我说 | *dn*对我说 |
| declares the LORD (*neʾum YHWH*), before or after the words | 这是*dn*的宣告 | 这是*dn*的话 | *dn*说 |
| says the LORD (*ʾamar YHWH* after the words, 1:11; 33:10; 66:9) | *dn*说 | *dn*说 | *dn*说 |
| says your God / my God (40:1; 54:6; 57:21) | 你们的*gt*说 / 我的*gt*说 | as L | as L |
| for the mouth of the LORD has spoken (1:20; 40:5; 58:14) | 因为*dn*的口已经说了 | as L (40:5 as its English: *dn**他*自己的口说了) | 这是*dn**他*自己说的 ("the LORD himself has said this") |
| massa (oracle) as a heading (13:1; 15:1; 17:1; 19:1; 21:1, 11, 13; 22:1; 23:1; 30:6) | 关于X的默示 | 关于X的信息 | 这是关于X的信息 |
| hoy / oy (woe) | ……有祸了 (有祸了，那些……的人 where the cry comes first) | ……有祸了 | ……真是可悲 ("How terrible it will be for", the English Readable's one form; Matthew 23) |
| in that day (*bayyom hahu*, the future) | 到那日 | 到那日 | 到那时 |
| on that day (past narrative, 22:8, 12) | 那日 | 那天 / 那一天 | 那时 |
| the Lord GOD (*ʾAdonai YHWH*) | 主宰*dn* | 主宰*dn* | 主宰*dn* |
| the Lord GOD of hosts | 主宰万军之*dn* | 主宰万军之*dn* | 统领万军的主宰*dn* |
| the LORD of hosts | 万军之*dn* | 万军之*dn* | 统领万军的*dn* |
| the Lord (*ha-adon*), the LORD of hosts (1:24; 3:1; 10:16, 33; 19:4) | 主，万军之*dn* / 主——万军之*dn* | as L | 主，统领万军的*dn* |
| the Holy One of Israel (of Jacob, 29:23) | 以色列的圣者 (雅各的圣者) at every level; "the Holy One" alone 那圣者 | | |
| the Mighty One of Israel (1:24); of Jacob (49:26; 60:16) | 以色列的大能者; 雅各的大能者, every level | | |
| Redeemer (*goʾel*, of God) | 救赎主 | 救赎主 | 救主; with 就是拯救……的那一位 added where the English Readable adds "the one who rescues" (41:14; 59:20; 63:16) |
| redeem (*gaʾal*); ransom (*padah*) | 救赎; 赎回 | as L | 赎回 / 救; 释放 (35:10 = 51:11) |
| Savior (*moshiaʿ*, of God) | 救主 | 救主 where the English says "Savior"; 拯救……的那一位 where it says "the one who saves" | 拯救……的那一位 |
| a savior, deliverer (a human one, 19:20) | 拯救者 | as L | as L |
| I am the LORD, and there is no other (45:5, 6, 18) | 我是*dn*，再没有别的 | as L | 我是*dn*，再没有别的 ("no other") / 没有别的*gt* ("no other God"), as the English Readable |
| I am God, and there is no other (45:22; 46:9) | 我是*gt*，再没有别的 | as L | 我是*gt*，没有别的*gt* |
| I am he (*ʾani hu*: 41:4; 43:10, 13, 25; 46:4; 48:12; 51:12; 52:6) | 我就是那一位 (46:4 我仍是那一位) | as L | as the English Readable |
| I am the first and I am the last (44:6; 48:12) | 我是最先的，我也是最后的 / 我是最初的，也是最后的 (Revelation 1:17) | | |
| the refrain "For all this his anger has not turned back, and his hand is stretched out still" (5:25; 9:11, 16, 20; 10:4, Hebrew numbering) | 尽管这一切，*他*的怒气没有转回， \q *他*的手还伸着。 | 尽管如此，*他*的怒气并未转回， \q *他*的手依然伸出。 | 可是即使这样，*dn*仍然在发怒， \q 仍然举起手来，要惩罚他们 (5:25; 10:4 要击打他们) / *他*仍然准备伸手击打他们 (9:11, 16, 20), as the English Readable words it two ways |
| There is no peace, says the LORD (my God), for the wicked (48:22 = 57:21) | 没有平安， \q *dn*说，恶人得不着。 | 恶人毫无平安， \q *dn*说。 | 作恶的人没有平安， \q *dn*说。 |
| the ransomed of the LORD will return (35:10 = 51:11) | the same Chinese in both, at every level | | |
| They will not hurt or destroy in all my holy mountain (11:9 = 65:25) | 在我圣洁之山的各处，它们都不作恶，也不行毁坏 | 在我的圣山上，到处都没有伤害，也没有毁灭 | 在我的圣山上，到处都不会再有伤害和毁灭 |

(*他* = `{{deity_pronoun:他|祂}}`.)

**Terms settled in Isaiah:**

| English (Hebrew) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| coastlands (*iyyim*) | 众海岛 (L, B; "the coastlands of the sea" 海中的众海岛); 远方各地 (R, "lands far away"). The singular coast of Philistia and Phoenicia (20:6; 23:2, 6): 海岸 (L), 沿海 (B, R) |
| banner, signal flag (*nes*) | 旗帜 at every level, whether the English says "banner" or "signal flag" (11:10 B 集合旗帜, "rallying flag") |
| daughter Zion | 锡安女子 (L, B), 锡安城的人 (R), as the glossary; 62:11 keeps Matthew 21:5's 锡安的女儿 (L, B), with a decision. Daughter Sidon, Tarshish, Babylon, the Chaldeans: 西顿女子, 他施女子, 巴比伦女子, 迦勒底女子. The daughters of Zion as women (3:16-4:4): 锡安的众女子 (L), 锡安的女子们 (B) |
| the virgin daughter (37:22; 47:1) | 处女 (L, B) |
| strong drink (*shekar*) | 烈酒 where the English says "strong drink", 麦酒 where it says "beer" (5:11, 22; 24:9; 28:7; 29:9; 56:12), as Numbers 6:3 |
| lyre (*kinnor*); harp (*nevel*) | 琴; 瑟, at every level (竖琴 is not used) |
| seraphim (6:2, 6) | 撒拉弗 |
| fiery flying serpent (*saraph meʿofef*, 14:29; 30:6) | 飞翔的火蛇 (L); 有翅膀的火蛇 (B); 会飞的火蛇 (R), with 火蛇 as Numbers 21:6 |
| Sheol | 阴间 (L, B); 死亡之地 (R) |
| the Pit (*bor*, *shaḥat*) | 坑 (L); 深坑 (B); 坟墓 (R); a real pit, cistern or quarry as the sense |
| the shades (*rephaim*) | 阴魂 (L); 死人 (B, R), as the English "the dead" |
| foreigner (*ben-nekar*, *nokri*) | 外族人 (L, B); 外国人 (R) (as 1 Kings 8:41); 别国的人 where the English Readable says "people from other countries" |
| strangers (*zarim*) | 外人 (L); B and R as the English (外族人, 敌人) |
| sojourner (*ger*, 14:1) | 寄居的人 (L, B); 外来的人 (R) |
| the nations (*goyim*) | 列国 (L), 各国 / 万国 (B, R); 外邦人 only where the New Testament's Gentiles are quoted (8:23) |
| Leviathan (27:1); the sea monster (*tannin*) | 利维坦; 海怪 / 怪兽 |
| Rahab, the sea monster (30:7; 51:9) | 拉哈伯, kept apart from Rahab of Jericho 喇合 |
| the young woman (*ʿalmah*, 7:14) | 年轻女子 at every level; Matthew 1:23's 童女 named in a footnoted decision |
| Immanuel | 以马内利 |
| the Branch of the LORD (4:2) | *dn*的苗裔 (L, B); *dn*使它长出来的新枝 (R) |
| a shoot from the stump of Jesse (11:1) | 从耶西的树墩长出的新芽 |
| the Servant (42-53) | 我的仆人; his pronouns 他 with no span, as the English; God's 我 never spanned |
| torah in Isaiah (1:10; 2:3; 8:16, 20; 42:4; 51:4) | 教导 |
| the fear of the LORD | 敬畏*dn* (L, B); 尊敬并畏惧*dn* (R) |
| the house of the LORD (2:2) | *dn*殿 (L, B); *dn*圣殿 (R) |
| new heavens and a new earth (65:17; 66:22) | 新的诸天和新的地 (L); 新天新地 (B); 新的天和新的地 (R) |
| Memphis (Noph, 19:13) | 孟菲斯 (as Jeremiah 2:16; 46) |
| Rabshakeh (36-37) | 拉伯沙基 (L); 首席长官 (B); 亚述指挥官 (R), as 2 Kings 18 |
| the steward over the house; the secretary; the recorder (22:15; 36:3) | 管理王宫的 (L), 王宫总管 (B, R); 书记; 史官, as 2 Kings 18:18 |

**Address and pronouns.** God speaking says 我 with no span; people praying
say `{{deity_pronoun:你|祢}}`. `{{deity_pronoun:他|祂}}` stands where the
English spans a pronoun for God; where the English starts a sentence with an
unspanned "He" (it is capitalised either way), Chinese joins the sentence to
the one before or uses 自己 ("他要照顾自己的百姓") where it reads well, and
otherwise keeps the span at the sentence start, as the style guide section 4
allows (the drafters' decisions say so per chapter). About 240 such
sentence-initial spans remain, mostly at Readable.

**Names not in the tables above**, as Isaiah has them (36-39 as 2 Kings
18-20):

| English (first verse) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| Amoz (1:1) | 亚摩斯, kept apart from the prophet Amos 阿摩司 |

To be settled by reviewers: the Readable Redeemer 救主 (the glossary's form)
against 救赎主 at every level; 我就是那一位 for *ʾani hu* against 我就是祂
(John 4:26); 锡安的女儿 (Matthew 21:5) or 锡安女子 at 62:11; 孟菲斯 or 挪弗 for
Noph.

### Jeremiah

Jeremiah keeps the glossary's forms for every name and term above, the
prophets' formula table, and Isaiah's prophetic vocabulary (woe at Readable
……真是可悲, coastlands 众海岛, the Holy One of Israel 以色列的圣者, Memphis
孟菲斯, jackals 豺) and Lamentations' (daughter Zion, the daughter of my
people, princes). Chapter 52 copies the harmonised 2 Kings 24:18-25:30 at every
level where the English is the same, and differs only where the Hebrew does
(52:6 "in the fourth month", 52:8 "Zedekiah", 52:16 "Nebuzaradan", 52:28-30
the numbers of the exiles, 52:34 "until the day of his death"). The book's
repeated passages have one Chinese where the Hebrew is the same: 6:12-15 =
8:10-12; 6:22-24 = 50:41-43; 10:12-16 = 51:15-19 (51:19 without "Israel");
16:14-15 = 23:7-8; 23:19-20 = 30:23-24; 30:10-11 = 46:27-28; 21:9 = 38:2;
15:2 = 43:11 (in 43:11's order); 49:18 = 50:40; 49:19-21 = 50:44-46 (names
changed); 5:9 = 5:29 = 9:8. *dn* = `{{divine_name:上主|雅威}}`, *gt* =
`{{god_term:神|上帝}}`.

The book's refrains and formulas, one wording per level:

| English | Literal | Balanced | Readable |
|---|---|---|---|
| The word that came to Jeremiah from the LORD (7:1; 11:1; 18:1; 21:1; 30:1; 32:1; 34:1, 8; 35:1; 40:1) | 从*dn*那里临到耶利米的话 | 从*dn*那里有信息临到耶利米 | *dn*对耶利米说 (……说了以下的话 where the sentence goes on) |
| the LORD of hosts, the God of Israel | 万军之*dn*、以色列的*gt* | 万军之*dn*、以色列的*gt* | 统领万军的*dn*、以色列的*gt* |
| the LORD, the God of hosts (5:14; 15:16; 35:17; 38:17; 44:7) | *dn*万军之*gt* | *dn*万军之*gt* | *dn*统领万军的*gt* |
| the Lord GOD of hosts (2:19; 46:10; 49:5; 50:25, 31) | 主宰万军之*dn* | 主宰万军之*dn* | 统领万军的主宰*dn* |
| Ah, Lord GOD! (1:6; 4:10; 14:13; 32:17) | 唉，主宰*dn*啊！ | as L | as L |
| rising early and speaking / sending (7:13, 25; 11:7; 25:3-4; 26:5; 29:19; 32:33; 35:14-15; 44:4) | 一早起来…… | 一再…… | 一次又一次地…… |
| the sword, famine and pestilence | 刀剑、饥荒和瘟疫 (in the Hebrew's order) | 刀剑、饥荒和瘟疫 | 战争、饥荒和疾病 (或 where the English says "or") |
| the stubbornness of their (evil) heart (3:17; 7:24; 9:13; 11:8; 13:10; 16:12; 18:12; 23:17) | 按着自己(邪恶)内心的顽固而行 | 随着自己(邪恶)内心的顽固行事 | 固执地随从自己(邪恶)的心 |
| days are coming (7:32; 9:24; 16:14; 19:6; 23:5, 7; 30:3; 31:27, 31; 33:14; 48:12; 49:2; 51:47, 52) | 看哪，日子将要来到 (Hebrews 8:8) | 日子将到 | 时候要到 (Hebrews 8:8) |
| restore the fortunes (29:14; 30:3, 18; 31:23; 32:44; 33:7, 11, 26; 48:47; 49:6, 39) | 扭转……的命运 | 扭转……的命运 | 使……重新过上好日子 |
| Terror on every side (6:25; 20:3, 10; 46:5; 49:29) | 四面都是惊吓 (20:3 L 玛歌珥米撒毕, as the English) | 四面都是惊吓 | 到处都是恐怖 |
| a horror and a hissing (18:16; 19:8; 25:9, 18; 29:18; 51:37) | 惊骇……嗤笑 | 惊骇……受人讥诮 | 令人惊骇……被人嘲笑 |
| hiss over all its blows (19:8; 49:17; 50:13) | 又因它所受的一切打击发出嘘声 | 又因它所受的一切创伤发出讥诮的嘘声 | 嘲笑它 |
| uproot, tear down, destroy, overthrow, build, plant (1:10; 18:7, 9; 24:6; 31:28, 40; 42:10; 45:4) | 拔除、拆毁、毁灭、推翻、建造、栽种 | as L | as L |
| God relents (niham: 4:28; 15:6; 18:8, 10; 20:16; 26:3, 13, 19; 42:10) | 回心转意 | 回心转意 | 改变心意 |
| you will be my people and I will be your God (7:23; 11:4; 24:7; 30:22; 31:1, 33; 32:38) | 你们要成为我的子民，我要成为你们的*gt* (31:33 属我的子民, as Hebrews 8:10) | 要是 | 会是 |
| as the LORD lives (4:2; 5:2; 12:16; 16:14-15; 23:7-8; 38:16; 44:26) | [我指着]永活的*dn*[起誓] | 我指着永活的*dn*起誓 | 我指着永活的*dn*发誓 |
| as I live (God, 22:24; 46:18) | 我凭着我的生命起誓 | as L | as L |
| I swear by myself (22:5; 49:13) | 我指着自己起誓 | 我凭着自己起誓 | 我凭着自己发誓, as Isaiah 45:23 |
| Shall I not punish them for these things? (5:9, 29; 9:8) | 我不该因这些事惩罚[他们]吗？……我的心不该报复吗？ | 我不该为这些事惩罚他们吗？……我不该报复吗？ | 他们做了这些事，我不该惩罚他们吗？……我不该报应这样的国家吗？ |
| woe (hoy, oy) | ……有祸了 | ……有祸了 | ……真是可悲 ("Woe is me" 我真是可悲; "woe to us" 我们真是可悲); 30:7 "How terrible that day will be" 那日将会多么可怕 |
| the yoke of the king of Babylon (27-28) | 把颈项放在巴比伦王的轭下; 折断巴比伦王的轭 | as L | 把脖子放在…… |

Terms settled in Jeremiah:

| English | Chinese |
|---|---|
| shofar (4:5, 19, 21; 6:1, 17; 42:14; 51:27) | 羊角号 at every level, though the English Literal and Readable say "trumpet" |
| the court of the guard (32-39) | 卫兵院 |
| captain of the guard (Nebuzaradan) | 护卫长 (L, B); 卫队长 / 王的卫队长 (R), as 2 Kings 25 |
| princes (sarim) | 首领 (L); 官员 (B); 领袖 or 官员 (R) as the English Readable says "leaders" or "officials", as Lamentations |
| the daughter of my people (4:11; 6:26; 8:11-23; 9:6; 14:17) | 我百姓的女儿 (L); 我亲爱的百姓 (B); 我的百姓 (R), as Lamentations |
| daughter Zion; daughter Egypt, Babylon, Dibon | 锡安女子 (L, B), 锡安城的人 (R); 埃及女子, 巴比伦女子, 底本女子 (L, B) |
| faithless Israel; faithless children (3); faithless daughter (31:22; 49:4) | 背道的以色列; 背道的儿女 (L, B); 背离我的以色列 / 儿女 (R); 背道的女儿 (31:22), 背道的女子 (49:4) (L, B), 不忠的女儿 / 女子 (R) |
| the queen of heaven (7:18; 44) | 天后 |
| balm (tsori, 8:22; 46:11; 51:8) | 香脂, as Genesis 37:25; 乳香 kept for frankincense (6:20) |
| jackals (9:10; 10:22; 14:6; 49:33; 51:37) | 豺, as Isaiah and Lamentations |
| coastlands (2:10; 31:10); the coastland of Caphtor (47:4) | 众海岛 (L, B), 远方各地 (R); 迦斐托海岸 (L), 沿海 (B) |
| strangers (zarim: 2:25; 30:8; 51:2, 51) | 外人 (L); 外族人 (B, R) where the English says "foreigners" |
| massa as a word-play (23:33-40) | 默示 (L); 信息 (B, R); 23:36 B and R 重担 for "burden" |
| the Branch (23:5; 33:15) | 苗裔 (L, B); 新枝 (R) |
| new covenant (31:31) | 新约 (L, B); 新的盟约 (R), as Hebrews 8:8 |
| den of robbers (7:11) | 强盗的巢穴 (L); 强盗藏身的地方 (B); 强盗躲藏的地方 (R), as the Gospels |
| Rachel weeping (31:15) | Matthew 2:18's Chinese where the Hebrew says the same |
| let the one who boasts (9:23) | 夸口的人要……夸口, the frame of 1 Corinthians 1:31 |

Names (with those of 2 Kings 23-25 for the kings and officials):

| English | Chinese |
|---|---|
| Jehoiachin; Jeconiah; Coniah | 约雅斤 (52:31-33, as 2 Kings 24-25); 耶哥尼雅 (24:1; 27:20; 28:4; 29:2, as Matthew 1:11-12); 哥尼雅 (22:24, 28; 37:1): one king, each Hebrew name kept, with decisions at 22:24, 24:1 and 52:31 |
| Nebuchadnezzar / Nebuchadrezzar | 尼布甲尼撒 for both spellings, with a decision where the Hebrew has r |
| Baruch son of Neriah; Seraiah | 巴录, 尼利亚; 西莱雅 |
| Gedaliah son of Ahikam; Ishmael son of Nethaniah; Johanan son of Kareah | 基大利, 亚希甘; 以实玛利, 尼探雅; 约哈难, 加利亚 |
| Jezaniah (40:8; 42:1); Jaazaniah (35:3) | 耶撒尼亚 (Jeremiah's spelling; 2 Kings 25:23 雅撒尼亚); 雅撒尼亚 |
| Pashhur; Hananiah; Shemaiah; Zephaniah; Irijah; Jehucal | 巴施户珥; 哈拿尼雅; 示玛雅; 西番雅; 伊利雅; 犹甲 |
| Ebed-melech the Cushite; Jonadab son of Rechab | 古实人以伯米勒; 利甲的儿子约拿达 |
| Tahpanhes; Migdol; Memphis; Pathros; Carchemish | 答比匿; 密夺; 孟菲斯; 巴忒罗; 迦基米施 |
| Shallum (22:11) | 沙龙 |
| the Valley of Slaughter (7:32; 19:6) | 杀戮谷 |

Address and pronouns: God speaking says 我 with no span; Jeremiah's prayers
say `{{deity_pronoun:你|祢}}`. Spans stand only where the English level spans
the pronoun: a sentence-initial "He" / "You" that the English leaves unspanned
is plain 他 / 你 in Chinese, or the sentence is joined or reworded (25 levels in
chapters 12, 15, 17, 20, 25, 33, 50, 51). Headings and summaries say 主 for God
and avoid the divine-name and god-term words.

To be settled by reviewers: Readable 一次又一次地 against a shorter 一再 at
every level; 我要是你们的神 (B, from Hebrews 8:10), which can be misread as "if
I were"; the plain pronoun against Isaiah's practice of keeping a span at a
sentence start.

### Lamentations

Lamentations keeps the forms of Isaiah and the glossary for every name and term above (daughter Zion, the prophets' massa and woe, torah as 教导, God's hesed, 主 for ʾAdonai, the LORD's anointed as Samuel), and settles these:

| English (first verse) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| "How ...!" (ʾekhah, 1:1; 2:1; 4:1, 2) | 怎么……竟……！ (L, B); 怎么……竟然……！ (R), as Isaiah 1:21 at L; one form for the book's title word |
| the alphabet poems (1-4) | not imitated; each verse keeps its own content in the English lines, the meaning decisions name the letters (decision at 1:1) |
| the city, Zion, Jerusalem as a woman (1:1) | 她 at every level; R's "the people of Zion" 锡安城的人, then 他们 |
| daughter Zion (1:6) | 锡安女子 (L, B); 锡安城的人 (R); likewise 犹大女子 / 犹大的百姓, 耶路撒冷女子 / 耶路撒冷城的人, 以东女子 / 以东人 |
| virgin daughter Judah, Zion (1:15; 2:13) | 处女犹大女子, 处女锡安女子 (L, B), as Isaiah 37:22; R 犹大的百姓, 亲爱的锡安城的人 |
| the daughter of my people (2:11; 3:48; 4:3, 6, 10) | 我百姓的女儿 (L, as Isaiah 22:4); 我亲爱的百姓 (B); 我的百姓 (R) |
| "Zion cries:" (R, 1:9, 11, 18; 2:20) | 锡安哭喊： with her words unquoted, as the English |
| transgressions (pesha, 1:5) | 过犯 (L); 悖逆 (B); 背叛 / 罪 (R) |
| princes (sar, 1:6; 2:2, 9; 5:12) | 首领 (L); 官员 (B); 领袖 (R); her princes (nazir, 4:7) 首领 (L), 贵族 (B, R) |
| comforter (1:2, 9, 16, 17, 21) | 安慰[她/我]的 (L); 没有人安慰 (B, R) |
| virgins; young women (1:4, 18; 2:10, 21) | 处女 (L); 年轻女子 (B, R) |
| appointed feast (1:4; 2:6, 7, 22) | 节期 (L, B); 节日 / 过节 (B, R) |
| assembly (qahal, 1:10) | 大会 (L); 会众 (B) |
| winepress (1:15) | 榨酒池, as Isaiah 63:2-3 |
| mourning and moaning (2:5) | 哀伤和哀叹 (L, B), the shared 哀 echoing the one root |
| torah (2:9) | 教导 at every level |
| my liver (2:11) | 肝 (L); 心 (B); 悲痛欲绝 (R) |
| massa, oracles (2:14) | 默示 (L); 信息 (B, R) |
| the daughter of your eye (2:18) | 瞳人 (L); 眼睛 (B, R) |
| the man (3:1) | 我[是]那见过苦难的人 (L); speaks as 我 with no quotation marks |
| wormwood (3:15, 19) | 苦艾, as Deuteronomy 29:17 and Revelation 8:11 |
| the LORD's loyal love; mercies; faithfulness (3:22-23, 32) | 慈爱 (L, B), 始终不变的爱 (R); 怜悯; 信实. 3:22-23 drafted fresh: [是]上主的慈爱使我们没有灭绝……一个早晨接一个早晨，[它们]都是新的 (L); 每个清晨，它们都焕然一新 (B); 它们每天早上都是新的 (R) |
| my portion (3:24) | 我的份 (L, B); 我所拥有的一切 (R) |
| the sons of man (3:33) | 人的{{gender:儿女\|儿子}} (L); 世人 (B), 人 (R), no span |
| the Most High (3:35, 38) | 至高者 (L, B); 至高的*gt* (R) |
| terror and pit (3:47) | 惊恐和陷坑 (L), 惊慌和陷坑 (B), as Isaiah 24:17 |
| the pit (bor, 3:53, 55) | 坑 (L), 深坑 (B), 坑 / 坑底 (R): a cistern, not Sheol |
| Fear not (3:57) | 不要怕 (L, B); 不要害怕 (R) |
| jackals (4:3; 5:18); ostriches | 豺, as Isaiah 34:13; 鸵鸟 |
| the sons of Zion (4:2) | 锡安宝贵的{{gender:儿女\|儿子}} (L, B); 锡安城的人 (R) |
| Sodom; Uz; Edom (4:6, 21) | 所多玛; 乌斯 (as Genesis 10:23); 以东 |
| the LORD's anointed (4:20) | *dn*所膏立的 (L, B); 我们的王是*dn*所立的 (R) |
| woe to us (5:16) | 如今我们有祸了 (L); 我们有祸了 (B); 我们真是可悲 (R), as Isaiah's R |
| slaves rule over us (5:8) | 奴隶 at every level: the reversal of rank is the point |
| our fathers (5:7) | 我们的列祖 (L, B); 我们的祖先 (R), no span (the English has none) |

Address: the poet, Zion and the man speak to God with 你 in a span, at every level, and God's pronouns are spanned wherever Chinese uses one, sentence-initial "He" and "You" included (as Isaiah). Headings and summaries say 主 for God and avoid the divine-name and god-term words, since they take no settings.

### Ezekiel

Ezekiel keeps the glossary's forms for every name and term above, the
prophets' formula table (one wording per level), Isaiah's and Jeremiah's
prophetic vocabulary, and the forms of Genesis to 2 Chronicles for places,
peoples and kings. Passages the Hebrew repeats have one Chinese: the
watchman 3:17-21 and 33:1-9; chapter 18 and 33:10-20; 11:19-20 and
36:26-27 (where 11:19 "one heart" and 36:26 "a new heart" differ, so does
the Chinese); the living creatures, wheels and cherubim of chapters 1 and 10;
the glory and its likeness in 1:26-28, 8:2-4, 10, 11:22-23 and 43:2-5. The
prostitution chapters 16 and 23 share one vocabulary and are rendered plainly,
not graphically (16:25-26, 36-37; 23:3, 20-21 need careful review). Famous
verses (11:19-20, 18:4, 18:19-23, 20:11, 20:19, 33:11, 36:25-28, 37:1-14)
were checked against the Union Version and redrafted where a clause matched
it (*谨守遵行我的典章*, *枯干的骸骨啊，要听……的话*, *儿子必不担当父亲的罪孽*).
*dn* = `{{divine_name:上主|雅威}}`, *gt* = `{{god_term:神|上帝}}`.

**Formulas:**

| English | Literal | Balanced | Readable |
|---|---|---|---|
| Thus says the Lord GOD (*koh ʾamar ʾAdonai YHWH*), also where nothing follows (2:4; 3:11, 27) or a prophet claims it falsely (22:28) | 主宰*dn*如此说 | 主宰*dn*这样说 | 主宰*dn*说 |
| Thus says the Lord GOD to / concerning X | 主宰*dn*对X如此说 / 论到X如此说 | 主宰*dn*对X这样说 / 论到X这样说 | 主宰*dn*对X说 / 论到X说 |
| declares the Lord GOD (*neʾum ʾAdonai YHWH*) | 这是主宰*dn*的宣告 | 这是主宰*dn*的话 | 主宰*dn*说 |
| hear the word of the Lord GOD (6:3; 25:3; 36:4) | 要听主宰*dn*的话 | as L | as L |
| the word of the LORD came to me, saying | *dn*的话临到我，说： | *dn*的话临到我： | *dn*对我说： |
| Son of man (*ben adam*) | 人子啊 | 人哪 | 人哪 |
| they / you will know that I am the LORD | 他们（你们）就知道我是*dn* | as L | 他们（你们）就会明白我是*dn* |
| I, the LORD, have spoken | 我*dn*已经说了 | as L | as L |
| I, the LORD, have spoken, and I will do it (17:24; 22:14; 36:36; 37:14) | 我*dn*已经说了，也必做成 | as L | 我*dn*已经说了，也一定会做到 |
| as I live (*ḥai ʾani*) | 我凭着我的生命[起誓] | 我凭着我的生命起誓 | as B (as Jeremiah 22:24) |
| my eye will not pity, nor will I spare (5:11; 7:4, 9; 8:18; 9:10) | 我的眼必不顾惜，我也不会怜悯 | 我不会怜惜你（他们），也不会宽恕你（他们） | 我不会可怜你（他们），也不会对你（他们）仁慈 |
| set your face toward X | 你要面向X | 你要转脸面向X | 你要转向X |
| direct your face toward (4:3, 7) | 定睛对着 | 转脸面向 | 转向 |
| prophesy; prophesy against | 说预言; 说预言攻击 | as L | as L |
| the sword, famine and pestilence | 刀剑、饥荒和瘟疫 | as L | 战争、饥荒和疾病 (as Jeremiah) |
| woe (*hoy*, *oy*) | ……有祸了 | as L | ……真是可悲 |
| hand of the Lord GOD was upon me | 主宰*dn*的手在我身上 / 降在我身上 | 临到我 | *dn*的能力临到我 |

| the nave (*hekhal*); palm trees; pavement | 大殿 (as 1 Kings 6:3); 棕树; 铺石地 (L, B), 石头地面 (R) |

**Address and pronouns.** God speaking says 我 with no span; the prophet
says `{{deity_pronoun:你|祢}}` to God (37:3). `{{deity_pronoun:他|祂}}` stands
only where the English level spans the pronoun: where the English Balanced or
Readable begins a sentence with an unspanned "He" (2:1; 3:1-10; 4:15-16; 37:2,
11; 43:7, 18 and others), Chinese writes plain 他 at that level (decisions at
2:1 and 37:2).

### Daniel

Daniel keeps the glossary's forms for every name and term above, the forms
of Genesis to 2 Chronicles and of Ezra, Nehemiah and Esther for the Persian
court (satraps 总督 as Esther, Cyrus 古列, Ahasuerus 亚哈随鲁), and the
prophets' vocabulary of Isaiah. 2:4b-7:28 is Aramaic; the meaning decisions
say so, and the Chinese is drafted the same way. Chapter 9 is the only chapter
with the divine name (9:2-20), with `divine_name` spans exactly where the
English has them. Records follow the Hebrew and Aramaic verse numbers (4:1 is
the English 4:4; 6:1 the English 5:31), so the references below give both
where they differ. The New Testament's Chinese is kept where it sees these
verses and the Aramaic or Hebrew says the same: 7:13 (Mark 14:62; Revelation
1:13), 7:25 and 12:7 (Revelation 12:14), 9:27, 11:31 and 12:11 (Matthew 24:15;
Mark 13:14), 6:22 (Hebrews 11:33), 12:2 (John 5:28-29), 12:4 and 12:9
(Revelation 10:4; 22:10). *dn* = `{{divine_name:上主|雅威}}`, *gt* =
`{{god_term:神|上帝}}`, *他* = `{{deity_pronoun:他|祂}}`.

The book's court lists and refrains have one wording per level wherever they
return:

| English | Literal | Balanced | Readable |
|---|---|---|---|

| Daniel's prayer (9:4-19) | 9:4 keeps Nehemiah 1:5's Chinese ("the great and awesome God"); God addressed as `{{deity_pronoun:你\|祢}}` throughout, spoken of as *他*; "your servant" 仆人 at every level; forgive 赦免 (L, B), 饶恕 (R) |

Address: subjects say 您 and 陛下 to the king at Balanced and Readable
(王啊 and 你 at Literal), Daniel included, also in his rebuke of Belshazzar
(5:17-28); the kings say 你 to Daniel and his friends; Daniel to the angel
我主 (L, B), 大人 (R), with 您 (10:16-19). Pronouns for God follow the English
spans, and Chinese also spans a pronoun for God that begins a sentence, where
the English has a capital and no span (2:28; 3:28; 6:28 [6:27]; 9:24 R).

Names not in the tables above, as the text has them:

| English (first verse) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| Jehoiakim (1:1) | 约雅敬 |
| Shinar (1:2) | 示拿 (L, B), 巴比伦地区 (R) |
| Ashpenaz (1:3) | 亚施毗拿 |
| Arioch (2:14) | 亚略 |
| the plain of Dura (3:1) | 杜拉平原 |
| Mene, Mene, Tekel, Parsin; Peres (5:25-28) | 弥尼、弥尼、提客勒、法珥新; 毗勒斯 |
| Susa the citadel; Elam; the Ulai canal (8:2) | 书珊城堡; 以拦省; 乌莱运河 |
| Darius son of Ahasuerus, of Median descent (9:1) | 亚哈随鲁的儿子大利乌，玛代人的后裔 |
| the Tigris (10:4) | 底格里斯河 |
| Uphaz (10:5) | 乌法 |
| Kittim (11:30) | 基提 (L, B), 西方各地 (R), as the English Readable |
| Libyans, Cushites (11:43) | 利比亚人, 古实人 |

To be settled by reviewers: 竖琴 for the Aramaic *pesanterin* (3:5),
where Isaiah keeps 琴 and 瑟 for *kinnor* and *nevel* and does not use 竖琴;
占星家 at Readable for the Chaldeans as experts; 法珥新 (5:25) beside
毗勒斯 (5:28) for the two forms of the Aramaic word.

### Hosea

Hosea keeps the glossary's forms for every name and term above, the prophets' formula table (这是*dn*的宣告 / 这是*dn*的话 / *dn*说; 到那日 / 到那时; ……有祸了 / ……真是可悲, as Isaiah), and the forms of Genesis to Isaiah for places and kings (乌西雅, 约坦, 亚哈斯, 希西家; 耶罗波安, 约阿施; 耶户; 吉甲, 伯特利, 基比亚, 拉玛, 米斯巴, 基列, 示剑, 亚割谷, 押玛, 洗扁, 巴力毗珥, 孟菲斯, 亚兰). The marriage and prostitution language follows Ezekiel 16 and 23 in Chinese. New Testament quotations keep the New Testament's Chinese where the Hebrew says the same (2:1 = Romans 9:26; 6:6 = Matthew 9:13 and 12:7; 10:8 = Luke 23:30; 11:1 = Matthew 2:15) and translate the Hebrew, with a decision naming the New Testament form, where it follows the Greek (2:25 Romans 9:25 "蒙爱的"; 13:14 1 Corinthians 15:55 "胜利"; 14:3 Hebrews 13:15 "嘴唇的果子"). Chapter and verse numbers are the Hebrew's (2:1-25 = English 1:10-2:23; 12:1 = 11:12; 14:1 = 13:16).

| English (first verse) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| Hosea son of Beeri (1:1) | 备利的儿子何西阿 |
| Gomer daughter of Diblaim (1:3) | 滴拉音的女儿歌篾 |
| Jezreel; the valley of Jezreel (1:4-5) | 耶斯列; 耶斯列平原 (every level); 2:24 R adds 就是*gt*所栽种的百姓, as the English Readable |
| Lo-ruhamah / No Compassion (1:6) | 罗路哈玛 (L); 不蒙怜悯 (B, R) |

| cult prostitutes (4:14) | 庙妓 (L, B); 在神庙里作妓女的女人 (R) |
| a spirit of prostitution (4:12; 5:4) | 淫乱的灵 (L, B); 想要不忠的欲望 (R) |

| hesed of God (2:21) | 慈爱 (L, B); 始终不变的爱 (R) |

| the Baals; Baal | 众巴力; 巴力 |
| Baal of Peor; the Shameful One (9:10) | 巴力毗珥 (L), 毗珥的巴力 (B), 巴力——毗珥的神明 (R); 那可耻的 (L), 那可耻的神明 (B, R) |
| the grain, the new wine and the fresh oil | 五谷、新酒和新油 (L); 五谷、新酒和橄榄油 (B); 五谷、酒和橄榄油 (R) |

| terebinth (4:13); cypress (14:9) | 笃耨香树; 松树 (uncertain) |

| the God of hosts (12:6) | 万军之*gt* (L, B); 统领万军的*gt* (R) |
| his Maker (8:14) | 造他的那一位 (L, B); 造他们的*gt* (R) |
| "his Lord" (12:15) | 他的主, no span |
| the One on high (7:16; 11:7) | 至高者 (B; L, B in 11:7); 在上的*gt* / 至高的*gt* (R) |

| "Cover us ... Fall on us" (10:8) | Luke 23:30's Chinese at each level, in Hosea's order |
| "Out of Egypt I called my son" (11:1) | Matthew 2:15's Chinese at each level |
| "they sow the wind and reap the whirlwind" (8:7) | 他们种下风，就收割旋风 (L); 他们播种风，必收获狂风 (B); 他们撒下的是风，收回的会是狂风 (R); drafted against the familiar wording |
| bulls of our lips (14:3) | 把嘴唇当作公牛献上 (L); 献上嘴唇，代替公牛 (B, R); decision naming Hebrews 13:15 |

**Address and pronouns.** God speaking says 我 with no span (a decision once per chapter); the prophet's prayer (9:14) and the people's (8:2; 14:3-4) say `{{deity_pronoun:你|祢}}`. Where the English starts a sentence with an unspanned "He" for God, Chinese leaves the subject out or turns the clause (passive in 6:1 R and 8:5; a relative clause in 11:10 B, R) and keeps the span only where it cannot (5:6 R, 6:1 R, 6:3 R, 9:9 B, 14:4 R; a decision each). Hosea says 你 to everyone at every level.

To be settled by reviewers: 非我子民 or a plainer 不是我的子民 for Lo-ammi at R; 怜悯 kept for hesed in 6:6 (the glossary's rule) against the Hebrew's 慈爱 / 忠诚的爱 / 忠心; 庙妓 for the cult prostitutes; 松树 for *berosh*.

### Joel

Joel keeps the glossary's forms for every name and term above, the prophets' formula table (the word of the LORD came to X 临到 (L, B) / 对X说 (R), here as the book's heading; declares the LORD 这是*dn*的宣告 / 这是*dn*的话 / *dn*说, 2:12; in that day 到那日 / 到那时, 3:18), and Isaiah's settled wording: the wail of 13:6 (你们要哀号 / 哀号吧 / 你们要伤心痛哭), the shofar 号角 (L, R) / 羊角号 (B), "there is no other" 再没有别的, "for the LORD has spoken" (22:25; 25:8), strangers 外人 (L) / 外族人 (B). Records follow the Hebrew chapters (JOL.3 = English 2:28-32, JOL.4 = English 3); the reader numbers them as the English. God's "my" and "me", which the English spans, take no span: Chinese has no reverent first person. Where the English Readable or Balanced starts a sentence with an unspanned "He" or "His" (2:13, 2:14, 2:23, 3:16), the Chinese words it without a pronoun (这位*gt*, a passive, or a run-on line), so spans stand only where the English has them. It settles these:

| English (first verse) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| Pethuel (1:1) | 毗土珥 |
| the four locusts: cutting, swarming, hopping, stripping (1:4; 2:25) | 剪咬的蝗虫, 成群的蝗虫, 跳跃的蝗虫, 剥食的蝗虫 (L, B), one form each; 一群又一群的蝗虫 (R) (uncertain: the traditional 剪虫, 蝗虫, 蝻子, 蚂蚱 are the alternative) |
| the day of the LORD (1:15; 2:1, 11, 31; 3:14) | *dn*的日子 at every level; 1:15 repeats Isaiah 13:6's Chinese at each level |
| great and fearful / terrible (2:11, 31) | 可畏 (L); 可怕 (B, R) |
| grain, new wine, fresh oil (1:10; 2:19, 24) | 五谷, 新酒, 新油 (L); 五谷, 新酒, 橄榄油 (B); 粮食, (新)酒, 橄榄油 (R), as Deuteronomy 7:13; 11:14 |
| the early rain and the late rain (2:23) | 早雨和晚雨 (L); 秋雨, 春雨 (B, R), as Deuteronomy 11:14 |
| grain offering and drink offering (1:9, 13; 2:14) | 素祭和奠祭 (L, B); 粮食和酒 (R) |
| ministers of the LORD / of the altar (1:9, 13; 2:17) | *dn*的侍奉者, 祭坛的侍奉者 (L); 事奉*dn*的人, 在祭坛前事奉的人 (B, R) |
| sackcloth (1:8, 13) | 麻布 |
| consecrate a fast; a solemn assembly (1:14; 2:15) | 使禁食分别为圣, 庄严的聚会 (L); 神圣的禁食, 庄严的聚会 (B); 神圣的禁食日……特别聚会 (R), the same in both verses |
| the inhabitants of the land (1:2, 14; 2:1) | 这地所有的居民 (L); 住在这地的众人 (B); 住在这地的每一个人 / 这地所有的人 (R) |
| the animals of the field (1:20; 2:22) | 田野的走兽 (L); 野兽 (B, R) |
| the pastures of the wilderness (1:19-20; 2:22) | 旷野的草场 (L, B); 草原 (R) |
| between the porch and the altar (2:17) | 在前廊和祭坛之间, as Ezekiel 8:16 |
| your / his people (of God) | 子民 (L, B); 百姓 (R) |
| the LORD became jealous for his land (2:18) | 发热心 (L); 心里火热 (B); 深切地关心 (R) (uncertain) |
| gracious and compassionate, slow to anger, abounding in steadfast love (2:13) | Exodus 34:6's Chinese in Joel's order: 有恩惠、有怜恤，迟于发怒，满有慈爱 (L); 满有恩惠和慈悲，发怒迟缓，充满慈爱 (B); 满有仁慈和慈悲，不容易生气……始终不变的爱 (R) |
| the sun and moon grow dark, the stars withdraw their shining (2:10 = 3:15) | 日月昏暗，星宿收回它们的光芒 (L); 日月变暗，星星失去光亮 (B); 太阳和月亮都变黑了，星星也不再发光 (R) |
| "you will know that I am the LORD your God" (2:27; 3:17) | 你们就知道 (L); 那时你们就会知道 (B); 那时你们就会明白 (R) |
| 2:28-32 (= Acts 2:17-21) | Acts' Chinese at each level where the Hebrew says the same (倾注 / 倾倒 my Spirit, 一切血肉之躯 / 全人类 / 世上所有的人, 说预言 / 传讲从*gt*来的信息, 奇事, the sun and moon); the Hebrew where Acts follows the Greek, with decisions: 此后 for "in the last days, says God", old men before young men, no "my" servants and no "and they will prophesy" (2:29), no "above", "signs", "below" and 烟柱 for "vapor of smoke" (2:30), *dn* and 可畏 for "the Lord ... glorious" (2:31) |
| everyone who calls on the name of the LORD will be saved (2:32 = Romans 10:13) | Acts 2:21 and Romans 10:13's Chinese with *dn*: 谁呼求*dn*的名，谁就会得救 (B); 每一个向*dn*呼求帮助的人都会得救 (R); Literal 都要逃脱 for the Hebrew "escape" (footnoted decision) |
| restore the fortunes (3:1) | 扭转……的命运 (L, B); 重新过上好日子 (R), as Jeremiah 29:14 |
| the valley of Jehoshaphat (3:2, 12) | 约沙法谷 at every level (footnoted: "the LORD judges") |
| return your recompense on your head (3:4, 7) | 使你们的报应归到你们的头上 (L); 使你们的所作所为归到你们自己头上 (B); 照你们所做的报应你们 (R) |
| the Greeks (sons of Javan, 3:6) | 希腊人 at every level |
| the Sabeans (3:8) | 示巴人, kept apart from Seba 西巴 |
| consecrate war (3:9) | 使战争分别为圣 (L); 准备圣战 (B, as Jeremiah 51:27); 准备打仗 (R) |
| beat your plowshares into swords (3:10) | Isaiah 2:4's Chinese turned around: 犁铧……剑, 修枝刀……矛 (L); 犁刃……刀剑, 修剪枝条的刀……长矛 (B); 耕地用的犁刃……锤打成刀剑, 修剪葡萄树的刀……长矛 (R) |
| the valley of decision (3:14) | 判决谷 |
| the valley of Shittim (3:18) | 什亭谷, as Numbers 25:1 |
| I will hold innocent their blood (3:21) | 宣告……无罪 (L); 伸冤 (B); 惩罚那些流他们血的人 (R) (uncertain) |

To be settled by reviewers: the four locust names against the traditional 剪虫, 蝗虫, 蝻子, 蚂蚱; 发热心 / 心里火热 against 忌邪 for the LORD's jealousy (2:18); Amos 1:2 and 9:13 (Joel 3:16, 18) should be checked against Joel's Chinese once Amos is drafted.

### Amos

Amos keeps the glossary's forms for every name and term above, the prophets' formula table as Isaiah settled it (*dn*如此说 / 这样说 / 说; 这是*dn*的宣告 / 这是*dn*的话 / *dn*说; 到那日 / 到那时; ……有祸了 / ……真是可悲; 主宰*dn* for the Lord GOD, with a decision at 1:8), and the forms of Genesis to 2 Chronicles for places, peoples and kings (Uzziah 乌西雅, Jeroboam son of Joash 约阿施的儿子耶罗波安, Amaziah 亚玛谢, Hazael 哈薛, Ben-hadad 便哈达, Kir 吉珥, Lebo-hamath 利波哈马, Lo-debar 罗底巴). New Testament quotations: 5:25-27 keeps Acts 7:42-43's frame where the Hebrew agrees and translates the Hebrew's Sikkuth, Kiyyun and "beyond Damascus", with footnoted decisions naming Acts' 摩洛, 理番 and 巴比伦以外; 9:11 keeps Acts 15:16's 大卫那已经倒塌的帐幕 (L, B) / 帐篷 (R); 9:12 translates the Hebrew "possess the remnant of Edom", with a footnoted decision naming Acts 15:17's 好让其余的人寻求主. 5:24 is drafted fresh.

**Refrains and formulas** (one wording per level):

| English | Literal | Balanced | Readable |
|---|---|---|---|
| For three transgressions of X, and for four, I will not turn it back (1:3-2:6, eight times) | 因X的三项过犯， \q 又因四项，我必不使它转回 | 因X的三项罪行， \q 甚至四项，我必不收回惩罚 | X人一再犯罪， \q 所以我必不收回对他们的惩罚 |
| So I will send fire on X, and it will consume the fortresses of Y | 所以我要降火到X， \q 它必吞灭Y的堡垒 (城墙上; 城中的堡垒) | 所以我要降火到X， \q 烧毁Y的堡垒 | 所以我要降火到X， \q 把Y的堡垒烧光 |
| yet you did not return to me, declares the LORD (4:6-11, five times) | 你们仍不归向我，这是*dn*的宣告 | 你们却还是不归向我，这是*dn*的话 | 可是就算这样，你们还是不回到我这里来，*dn*说 |
| the LORD (God of hosts) is his name (4:13; 5:8, 27; 9:6) | *dn*万军之*gt*是*他*的名 | *他*的名是*dn*万军之*gt* | *他*的名字是*dn*统领万军的*gt* |
| the LORD God of hosts; the Lord GOD, the God of hosts (3:13) | *dn*万军之*gt*; 主宰*dn*、万军之*gt* | as L | *dn*统领万军的*gt*; 主宰*dn*、统领万军的*gt* |
| the Lord GOD of hosts (9:5) | 主宰万军之*dn* | as L | 统领万军的主宰*dn* |
| Thus the Lord GOD showed me (the visions, 7:1, 4, 7; 8:1) | 主宰*dn*这样指示我：看哪 | 以下是主宰*dn*指示我的事 | 主宰*dn*（又）让我看见一个异象 |
| never again will I pass by them (7:8; 8:2) | 我必不再饶过他们 | 我不再饶过他们了 | 我不会再放过他们，不惩罚他们了 |
| days are coming (4:2; 8:11; 9:13) | 看哪，日子将到 | 日子快到了 | 那时候快要到了 |
| Seek me / Seek the LORD and live (5:4, 6) | 你们要寻求……，就必存活 | ……，就能存活 | ……，就能活下去 |

(*他* = `{{deity_pronoun:他|祂}}`.)

**Terms and names settled in Amos:**

| English (first verse) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| sheep breeders (*noqdim*, 1:1) | 养羊的人 (L), 养羊人 (B, R); 牧人 kept for shepherds |
| Tekoa (1:1) | 提哥亚 (as 2 Chronicles 11:6) |
| the Valley of Aven; Beth-eden (1:5) | 亚文谷; 伯伊甸 (new) |
| Teman; Bozrah; Rabbah; Kerioth (1:12-2:2) | 提幔; 波斯拉; 拉巴; 加略 (as Jeremiah 48:24) |
| fortresses (*ʾarmenot*) | 堡垒 at every level |
| the covenant of brothers (1:9) | 弟兄的约 (L), 兄弟之间的盟约 (B), 使他们亲如兄弟的盟约 (R) |
| torah (2:4) | 教导 at every level; statutes 律例 (L, B), 规定 (R) |
| Nazirites (2:11) | 拿细耳人 (as Numbers 6) |
| the sons of Israel / of Ammon (L) | 以色列的{{gender:子孙\|儿子}}, 亚扪{{gender:子孙\|众子}}; 以色列人, 亚扪人 (B, R) |
| Harmon (4:3) | 哈门 (new, uncertain; kept apart from Hermon 黑门) |
| thank offering; freewill offerings (4:5) | 感恩祭; 甘心献的礼物 (L, B), 自愿献上的礼物 (R) |
| cleanness of teeth (4:6) | 牙齿干净 (L, B); 挨饿 (R) |
| blight and mildew; the cutting locust (4:9) | 焦枯病和霉烂病; 剪咬的蝗虫 (as Joel 1:4) (L, B), 蝗虫 (R) (Joel 1:4 should agree) |
| a stick snatched from the burning (4:11) | 从火中抽出来的柴头 (L) |
| the virgin Israel (5:2) | 以色列处女 (L), 以色列少女 (B), as Jeremiah 31:4 |
| justice; righteousness (5:7, 24; 6:12) | 公平 (L), 公正 (B, R); 公义 (L, B), 正直的事 (R); wormwood 茵陈 (L), 苦艾 (B), 苦东西 (R) |
| the Pleiades and Orion (5:8) | 昴星和参星 (new, uncertain; Job 9:9 and 38:31 should agree) |
| in the gate (the court) | 城门口 (L), 法庭上 (B, R) |
| the remnant of Joseph (5:15) | 约瑟的余民 (L, B), 剩下的以色列人 (R) |
| solemn assemblies (5:21) | 严肃会 (L), 圣会 (B), 为敬拜而举行的特别聚会 (R) |
| harp (*nevel*, 5:23; 6:5) | 瑟, as Isaiah |
| Sikkuth; Kiyyun (5:26) | 西库特; 基云 (new); Acts 7:43's 摩洛, 理番 named in a footnoted decision |
| Calneh; Hamath; Gath (6:2) | 甲尼 (Genesis 10:10); 哈马; 迦特 |
| Karnaim (6:13) | 加宁 (new); R explains both names in the text |
| the Wadi of the Arabah (6:14) | 亚拉巴溪谷 |
| plumb line (*ʾanakh*, 7:7-8) | 铅垂线 at every level, with a footnoted decision on the meaning decision's reading (alternative "tin") |
| the high places of Isaac (7:9) | 以撒的丘坛 (L, B), 以撒的子孙在山上敬拜的地方 (R) |
| seer (7:12) | 先见 (L, B), 先知 (R) |
| herdsman; dresser of sycamore figs (7:14) | 牧人 (L, B), 养牲畜的 (R); 野无花果树 (L, B), 无花果树 (R); "son of a prophet" 先知的儿子 (L), 先知门徒 (B) |
| summer fruit / end (8:1-2) | 夏天的果子 / 结局; R explains the Hebrew sound-play in the text, L and B in a footnoted decision |
| the new moon (8:5) | 月朔 (L), 新月节 (B, R); ephah and shekel kept without units spans, as the English |
| the pride of Jacob (8:7) | 雅各所引以为傲的 (keeping both readings); 6:8 雅各的骄傲 |
| "As your god lives, Dan" (8:14) | 但哪，我们指着你永活的神明起誓 (no god_term span) |
| the Cushites; Caphtor (9:7) | 古实人; 迦斐托 (L, B), 克里特 (R) |
| the fallen booth of David (9:11) | 大卫那已经倒塌的帐幕 (L, B), 帐篷 (R), as Acts 15:16 |
| restore the fortunes (9:14) | 扭转……的命运 (L), 重新兴旺 (B), 重新过好日子 (R) |

**Address.** Amaziah reports to Jeroboam with 您 at B and R (7:10); Amaziah and Amos say 你 to each other at every level. Amos prays 主宰*dn*啊，请赦免 / 请停止 (7:2, 5) without a pronoun, since the English has no span there. God speaking says 我 with no span; the English "my / Me" spans have no Chinese counterpart (decisions at 1:8, 3:2, 4:6).

### Obadiah

Obadiah keeps the glossary's forms for every name and term above, the
prophets' formula table, and Isaiah's settled forms. Verses 1-9 share their
Hebrew with Jeremiah 49:9-16: each level copies Jeremiah's Chinese where the
Hebrew is the same and differs, with a decision naming Jeremiah's wording,
where it differs (1 "we have heard" against Jeremiah's "I", and the call to
battle; 2 "greatly despised" against "despised among humankind"; 3 without
Jeremiah's "the terror you cause"; 4 the added "among the stars"; 5 thieves
first, and "steal" against Jeremiah's "destroy" 毁坏; 6 drafted fresh, since
Jeremiah 49:10 has other verbs). *dn* = `{{divine_name:上主|雅威}}`.

| English (Hebrew) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| The vision of Obadiah (*ḥazon*, 1) | 俄巴底亚的异象 (L); 俄巴底亚所得的异象 (B); 这是俄巴底亚所看见的异象 (R), as Isaiah 1:1 |
| Thus says the Lord GOD concerning Edom (1) | 主宰*dn*论到以东如此说 (L); 关于以东，主宰*dn*这样说 (B); 关于以东，主宰*dn*说 (R) |
| Mount Esau (8, 9, 19, 21) | 以扫山, every level |
| Edom; Esau; Teman | 以东; 以扫; 提幔 (as Jeremiah 49) |
| understanding (*tevunah*, 7-8) | 悟性 (L, B); 明白事理 (R) |
| the day of his disaster (*ʾed*, three times in 13) | 遭灾的日子, the same each time at every level |
| the day of distress (12, 14) | 患难的日子 |
| do not ... (12-14) | 不要 (L); 不该 (B, R), condemning what was done |
| the day of the LORD (15) | *dn*的日子, with 近了 (L, R) / 临近了 (B), as Isaiah 13:6 |
| all the nations (15-16) | 万国 |
| you have drunk (16, plural) | 你们 |
| possess (*yarash*, 17, 19-20) | 得……为业 (L); 占领 (B; 占有 in 17); 占领 (R; 收回 in 17) |
| the house of Jacob / Joseph / Esau (17-18) | 雅各家, 约瑟家, 以扫家 (R 雅各的子孙, 约瑟的子孙) |
| stubble (18) | 碎秸 (L, B); 干草 (R) |
| the Negev; the Shephelah (19) | 内盖夫 (L, B), 南方内盖夫荒漠 (R); 示非拉 (L), 西部丘陵 (B), 西边丘陵 (R), as Jeremiah 17:26 |
| Zarephath; Sepharad (20) | 撒勒法 (as Luke 4:26); 西法拉 (new) |
| deliverers (21) | 拯救者 (L, B), kept apart from 救主; 拯救`{{god_term:神\|上帝}}`子民的领袖 (R) |
| the kingship (21) | 王权 (L); 国度 (B, R) |

### Jonah

Jonah keeps the glossary's forms for every name and term above and the prophets' formula table ("the word of the LORD came to X" *dn*的话临到X (L, B) / *dn*对X说 (R), 1:1 and 3:1 with 第二次). The records follow the Hebrew numbering (JON.2.1 = English 1:17); the Chinese follows the English levels' text and lines. The prayer (2:3-10) is poetry with the English \q lines; the psalms it echoes (3:9; 31:7, 23; 42:8; 69:2; 120:1) were not yet drafted, so it is drafted fresh and those psalms should match it where the Hebrew is the same. *dn* = `{{divine_name:上主|雅威}}`, *gt* = `{{god_term:神|上帝}}`.

| English (first verse) | Chinese |

| Nineveh; Tarshish; Joppa (1:2-3) | 尼尼微 (Matthew 12:41); 他施; 约帕 (Acts 9) |
| arise (1:2; 1:6; 3:2) | 起来 at every level, so the captain's 起来 echoes the LORD's command |
| Nineveh, the great city (1:2; 3:2; 4:11) | 尼尼微那大城 (L); 尼尼微那座大城 (B); 尼尼微大城 (R) |
| evil (*raʿah*), the book's key word | 恶 at L every time (their evil, this evil, the evil God threatened, 4:1); B and R by sense: 邪恶 / 恶事, 灾祸 / 麻烦, 灾难 |
| went down (1:3, 5) | 下 / 下到 at every level (Jonah's descent) |
| hurl (1:4, 5, 12, 15) | 抛 (L, B); 扔 (R) |

| appointed (the fish, plant, worm, wind: 2:1; 4:6-8) | 安排 (L, B); 派 / 派来 (R) |
| Sheol (2:3); the Pit (2:7) | 阴间 (L, B), 死亡之地 (R); 坑 (L), 深坑 (B), 坟墓 (R) |
| your holy temple (2:5, 8) | `{{deity_pronoun:你|祢}}`的圣殿 |
| empty vanities / idols (2:9) | 虚妄无益之物 (L); 无用偶像 (B); 不能帮助他们的偶像 (R) |

Address: the sailors and Jonah say 你 to each other; prayers to the LORD use `{{deity_pronoun:你|祢}}`; the LORD's 我 has no span. 2:4 opens with God's "You", unspanned in the English only because it is capitalised by position; the Chinese spans it, with an uncertain decision.

### Micah

Micah keeps the glossary's forms for every name and term above, the prophets' formula table, and Isaiah's settled prophetic forms (woe, the LORD of hosts, "the mouth of the LORD has spoken", redeem, daughter Zion, torah 教导). Parallels: 1:1 follows Hosea 1:1's heading pattern; 1:2 reuses 1 Kings 22:28 (L, B); 1:3 reuses Amos 4:13's "heights of the earth" at each level; 1:10 reuses 2 Samuel 1:20; 3:12 copies Jeremiah 26:18 word for word at each level, with "因此，因为你们的缘故" (L, B) / "所以因为你们的缘故" (R) before it; 4:1-3 copies Isaiah 2:2-4 at each level and differs only where Micah's Hebrew does (peoples / nations, "many peoples ... mighty nations far away"); 4:4 reuses 1 Kings 5:5; 6:4 reuses Exodus 20:2; 7:6's last line keeps Matthew 10:36; 7:17 reuses Genesis 3:14; 7:18-20 reuse Exodus 34:6-7. *dn* = `{{divine_name:上主|雅威}}`, *gt* = `{{god_term:神|上帝}}`.

| English (first verse) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| the word of the LORD that came to Micah the Morashtite (1:1) | *dn*的话，就是……临到摩利沙人弥迦的 (L); *dn*的话临到摩利沙的弥迦 (B); 这是*dn*给从摩利沙城来的弥迦的信息 (R), as Hosea 1:1 and Jeremiah 26:18 |
| Moresheth (1:1); Moresheth-gath (1:14) | 摩利沙 (as Jeremiah 26:18); 摩利沙迦特 (not the CUV's 摩利设迦特), kept apart from Mareshah 玛利沙 |
| the town names of 1:10-15 | 迦特; 伯亚弗拉; 沙斐; 撒南; 伯以薛; 玛律; 拉吉; 亚革悉 (Joshua 15:44); 玛利沙 (Joshua 15:44); 亚杜兰. Readable gives the wordplay in the text, with no quotation marks the English lacks: 伯亚弗拉，就是尘土之家; 城名叫美好 (Shaphir); 城名叫出去 (Zaanan); 玛律，就是苦城; 亚革悉，就是谎言城; Mareshah's 名字听起来像“征服者” (the English Readable quotes it). Footnoted decisions |

| "preach" (the verb "drip", 2:6, 11) | 传讲; preacher 传讲者 |

| peshaʿ, transgression | 过犯 (L); 悖逆 (B); 罪 / 悖逆 (R), as Isaiah 59:12 |
| my people (the LORD speaking) | 我的子民 / 我子民 (L, B); 我的百姓 (R); "my" takes no span |
| heads; rulers; justice (3:1, 9) | 首领 (L), 领袖 (B, R); 官长; 公平 (L), 公正 (B, R) |
| seers; diviners (3:7) | 先见; 占卜的 |
| "they consecrate war" (3:5) | 宣告圣战 (L, B); 宣告要与那人争战 (R) |

| daughter Zion; daughter Jerusalem | 锡安女子, 耶路撒冷女子 (L, B); 锡安城的人, 耶路撒冷城的人 (R) |
| the Lord of the whole earth (4:13) | 全地的主 |
| Bethlehem Ephrathah (5:1, English 5:2) | 伯利恒以法他; "small to be among the thousands of Judah" 在犹大的千家中为小 (L), 太小了，算不得犹大的一个宗族 (B), 犹大各宗族中最不重要的城镇之一 (R); translated from the Hebrew, with a footnoted decision naming Matthew 2:6's 在犹大的首领中绝不是最小的 / 牧养我的子民以色列 |

| Nimrod (5:5) | 宁录 |
| "I will cut off" (5:9-12) | 剪除 (L, B) each time; 除掉 / 毁灭 (R) |

| Balak, Balaam son of Beor, Shittim, Gilgal (6:5) | 巴勒; 比珥的儿子巴兰; 什亭; 吉甲 |

| the scant ephah (6:10) | 短小伊法 (L), 短少量器 (B), 太小的量器 (R); no units span, as the English |
| a horror, a hissing (6:16) | 惊骇, 嗤笑 (L); 惊骇, 讥诮 (B); 令人惊骇, 嘲笑 (R), as Jeremiah 18:16 |
| Omri; Ahab | 暗利; 亚哈 |
| the faithful (ḥasid, 7:2) | 忠信的人 (L, B); 忠心的人 (R) |
| 7:6 = Matthew 10:35-36 | the in-laws 媳妇, 婆婆 and the last line as Matthew 10:36 at each level; the first line from the Hebrew, 儿子藐视父亲 (L); no gender span |
| the River (7:12) | 大河 (L); 幼发拉底河 (B, R), as Isaiah 27:12 |
| Carmel (7:14, the garden land) | 迦密 (L, B); 迦密肥沃之地 (R); Bashan, Gilead 巴珊, 基列 |
| "Who is a God like you?" (7:18) | 有哪一位*gt*像`{{deity_pronoun:你|祢}}` at every level, with a footnoted decision on Micah's name ("Who is like the LORD?") |
| bearing iniquity; hesed (7:18, 20) | 担当罪孽 (L), 赦免罪过 (B), 饶恕罪 (R), as Exodus 34:7; 慈爱 (L, B), 始终不变的爱 (R); faithfulness 信实 |

Pronouns for God: a `deity_pronoun` span stands only where the English level has one. Where the English starts a line with an unmarked "He" or "You" for God (2:4, 2:7, 3:8, 4:2-3, 4:12, 6:8, 7:9, 7:18-19), Chinese carries the subject on from the line before, names the LORD (*dn* or 那位*gt*), or puts the line in the passive, with a `word_order` decision; Isaiah 2:3-4's sentence-initial spans are therefore not copied at 4:2 (R) and 4:3 (B).

Address: God speaking says 我 with no span; the prophet and the people pray with `{{deity_pronoun:你|祢}}` (6:9; 7:14-20). No 您 in the book.

To be settled by reviewers: 摩利沙迦特 against the CUV's 摩利设迦特 for Moresheth-gath; 千家 at 5:1 (L) for "the thousands of Judah"; whether the passive in 2:4 and 7:19 (B) reads well, or a plain sentence-initial span is better there.

### Nahum

Nahum keeps the glossary's forms for every name and term above and the prophets' formula table (one wording per level), and the forms of Isaiah, Jeremiah and Ezekiel for shared images. Verse numbers follow the English (the Hebrew's 2:1 is 1:15). New Testament: none quoted. Shared wording: 1:2-3 takes the Chinese of Exodus 20:5 and 34:6-7 at each level; 1:15 takes Isaiah 52:7's at each level (the Readable opening sentence is Isaiah's word for word); 3:5 takes Jeremiah 13:26's for the skirts. It settles these:

| English (first verse) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| The oracle concerning Nineveh (1:1) | 关于尼尼微的默示 (L); 关于尼尼微的信息 (B); 这是关于尼尼微的信息 (R), as Isaiah 13:1 |
| Nahum the Elkoshite (1:1) | 伊勒歌斯人那鸿 (L); 伊勒歌斯的那鸿 (B); 来自伊勒歌斯的那鸿 (R) |
| a jealous and avenging God (1:2) | 忌邪、施行报复的*gt* (L, B), as Exodus 20:5; 要求人专一的*gt*，要惩罚恶行 (R) |
| a master of wrath (1:2) | 烈怒的主人 (L; 主 alone is kept for *ʾAdonai*); 满怀烈怒 (B) |
| slow to anger … will by no means acquit (1:3) | 迟于发怒 … 绝不会宣告[有罪的人]无罪 (L); 发怒迟缓 … 绝不会放过有罪的人 (B); 不容易生气 … 不会让有罪的人不受惩罚 (R), as Exodus 34:6-7 |
| Bashan, Carmel, Lebanon (1:4) | 巴珊, 迦密 (L), 迦密山 (B, R), 黎巴嫩 |
| worthlessness, the worthless one (*beliyyaʿal*, 1:11, 15) | 卑劣无用 (L), 邪恶 (B), 又邪恶又毫无价值 (R) at 1:11; 那卑劣的人 (L, B), 那邪恶的敌人 (R) at 1:15 |
| Thus says the LORD (1:12) | *dn*如此说 / *dn*这样说 / *dn*说, the formula table |
| the feet of him who brings good news, announcing peace (1:15) | 看哪，在山上[有]那传福音之人的脚，他宣告平安 (L); 看！山上有那报好消息之人的脚步！他传扬平安 (B); 你看！一个报好消息的使者正越过山岭而来。他宣告平安 (R) |
| Look, I am against you, declares the LORD of hosts (2:13; 3:5) | 看哪，我与你为敌，这是万军之*dn*的宣告 (L); 我与你为敌，这是万军之*dn*的话 (B); 我与你为敌，统领万军的*dn*说 (R), the same both times, as Jeremiah 50:31 |
| slave women / servant women (2:7) | 女奴 (L); 婢女 (B, R) |
| all faces grow pale (2:10; Joel 2:6) | 脸都变得苍白, for Joel to match |
| the lion, lioness, young lions, cub (2:11-12) | 公狮, 母狮, 少壮狮子, 小狮 (L) / 幼狮 (B, R) |
| Woe, city of bloodshed (3:1) | 有祸了，流人血的城 (L); 这流人血的城有祸了 (B); 这满是杀人凶手的城真是可悲 (R) |
| I will strip up your skirts over your face (3:5) | 掀起你的衣裙，蒙在你脸上 (L) / 把你的衣裙掀起，蒙住你的脸 (B) / 把你的衣裙掀起来，盖在你的脸上 (R), as Jeremiah 13:26 |
| No-amon (3:8) | 挪阿蒙 (L, B), with 阿蒙 as Jeremiah 46:25; 埃及的底比斯城 (R), as Jeremiah 46:25 and Ezekiel 30:14-16 |
| Cush, Egypt, Put, the Libyans (3:9) | 古实, 埃及, 弗, 利比亚人 (L, B), 利比亚 (R) |
| the hopping locust (*yeleq*); the swarming locust (*ʾarbeh*) (3:15-17) | 蝗蝻; 蝗虫 (L, B); R 蝗虫, 蚂蚱; to follow Joel 1:4 once it is settled |
| king of Assyria (3:18) | 亚述王啊, first in its sentence |

Pronouns for God: a `deity_pronoun` span exactly where the English level has one, the same count per level; where the English begins a line with an unspanned "He" or "His" (1:3-4, 1:6, 1:9 and the Readable of 1:2, 1:7-8), Chinese leaves the subject or possessive unsaid. The gods of Assyria (1:14): 神明, no span.

To be settled by reviewers: 挪阿蒙 (a new form) against the older 挪亚们; 蝗蝻 / 蝗虫 against Joel's forms.

### Habakkuk

Habakkuk keeps the glossary's forms for every name and term above and the prophets' formula table (massa 默示 / 信息 at 1:1, the LORD of hosts at 2:13, hoy at 2:6-19, Sheol at 2:5, torah 教导 at 1:4). It reuses Isaiah's and Jeremiah's Chinese wherever it repeats them, and the New Testament's Chinese where the Hebrew says the same: 1:5 keeps Acts 13:41 for "I am doing a work in your days … you would not believe even if someone told you", but translates the Hebrew "among the nations" where Acts follows the Greek "you scoffers" (footnoted decision). It settles these:

| English (first verse) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| the oracle that Habakkuk the prophet saw (1:1) | 先知哈巴谷所看见的默示 (L); ……的信息 (B); 这是先知哈巴谷在异象中领受的信息 (R) |
| the Chaldeans (1:6) | 迦勒底人 (L, B); 巴比伦人 (R) |
| "whose strength is his god" (1:11) | 神明, no god_term span |
| my Holy One (1:12); the Holy One (3:3) | 我的圣者; 那圣者 (L, B); 我圣洁的*gt*, 圣洁的*gt* (R) |
| the righteous will live by his faithfulness (2:4) | 义人却要凭着自己的信实活下去 (L); 义人要凭自己的信实生活 (B); 义人会凭着忠心活着 (R): Romans 1:17's frame, with 信实 / 忠心 for the Hebrew's faithfulness where Romans has 信心 from the Greek, and 自己的 for "his" (no gender span); Hebrews 10:38's 我的义人 is the Greek's and is not used (footnoted decision) |
| the woes (2:6, 9, 12, 15, 19) | 有祸了，那……的人 (L); 那……的人有祸了！ (B); 那……的人真是可悲！ (R), as Isaiah and Jeremiah 22:13; 2:6 opens the taunt with no quotation marks, as the English |
| the line of 2:8 repeated in 2:17 | the same Chinese at each level: 因为人的血，和[向]这地所行的暴力，[向]城和城里一切居民[所行的] (L); 因为你流了人的血，向这地施行暴力，向城和城里所有的居民施行暴力 (B); 你杀了很多人。你对这地施行暴力，也对这城和城里所有的人施行暴力 (R) |
| builds a city with bloodshed (2:12) | 以流血建造城市 (L); 以流人血建造城市 (B); 靠杀人建造城市 (R), for Micah 3:10 to copy (uncertain until Micah is drafted) |
| peoples toil for fire, nations for nothing (2:13) | 万民劳碌，只为火焚，列国疲乏，归于虚空 (L); 万民劳碌，只为火所焚烧；列国筋疲力尽，归于虚空 (B): Jeremiah 51:58's phrases in Habakkuk's order |
| the earth filled with the knowledge of the glory of the LORD (2:14) | Isaiah 11:9's Chinese with 荣耀 added: 大地要充满对*dn*荣耀的认识，如同众水遮盖大海 (L); ……就像大海充满了水 (B) |
| the LORD is in his holy temple; hush (2:20) | *dn*在*他*的圣殿中 (L), 圣殿里 (B, R); 肃静 (L), 静默 (B), 安静下来 (R), for Psalm 11:4 to share |
| Lebanon (2:17) | 黎巴嫩 |
| on shigionoth (3:1) | 示迦约诺 (L, B), formed from the glossary's 示迦约 (shiggaion) (uncertain); 要当作诗歌来唱 (R) |
| Selah (3:3, 9, 13) | 细拉, at the end of the verse as the English |
| Teman; Mount Paran (3:3) | 提幔 (Genesis 36); 巴兰山 (Deuteronomy 33:2) |
| Cushan; Midian (3:7) | 古珊 (as Judges 3:8's 古珊利萨田; uncertain); 米甸; tents 帐棚, kept apart from the tabernacle 帐幕 |
| your anointed (3:13) | *你*的受膏者 (L, B); *你*所拣选的王 (R); your people 子民 (L, B), 百姓 (R) |
| the God of my salvation (3:18) | 我救恩的*gt* (L); *gt*我的救主 (B); 因为*gt*拯救我 (R) |
| GOD my Lord (3:19) | *dn*我的主 (L, the Hebrew's order); 主宰*dn* (B, R) |
| my feet like the does', on my heights (3:19) | 2 Samuel 22:34's Chinese, with 行走 for "tread" |
| For the director: with stringed instruments (3:19) | 交给指挥：用弦乐器 (L); 交给指挥。用弦乐器。 (B); 给诗班指挥的。要用弦乐器演奏。 (R) |

The prayer of chapter 3 (3:2, 3:16-19 above all) is drafted fresh from the Hebrew, and 3:17-18 were checked against the familiar wording and reworded where a clause came close (不开花, 牛栏里没有牛, 在*dn*里欢腾). God's pronouns take the deity_pronoun span also where the English begins a line or sentence with an unspanned "He", "His" or "You" (1:12-14; 3:3-6), as Isaiah; God speaking says 我 with no span. *他* = `{{deity_pronoun:他|祂}}`, *你* = `{{deity_pronoun:你|祢}}`.

### Zephaniah

Zephaniah keeps the glossary's forms, the prophets' formula table and
Isaiah's settled forms. *dn* = `{{divine_name:上主|雅威}}`, *gt* =
`{{god_term:神|上帝}}`, *他* = `{{deity_pronoun:他|祂}}`. 1:14-18 (the
passage behind the Dies irae) and 3:14-17 are drafted afresh from the Hebrew.

| English (Hebrew) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| the heading (1:1) | *dn*的话临到希西家的玄孙、亚玛利雅的曾孙、基大利的孙子、古示的儿子西番雅 (L); B names the generations after 西番雅; R 这是*dn*传给……的信息, as the English Readable |
| declares the LORD (*neʾum*) | 这是*dn*的宣告 (L); 这是*dn*的话 (B); *dn*说 (R) |
| says the LORD (*ʾamar*, 3:20) | *dn*说, every level |
| the Lord GOD (1:7) | 主宰*dn* |
| the LORD of hosts (2:9-10) | 万军之*dn* (L, B); 统领万军的*dn* (R) |
| the day of the LORD; the great day (1:7, 14) | *dn*的日子 (近了 L, R; 临近了 B, as Isaiah 13:6); *dn*的大日子 |
| the day of the LORD's anger / fury (1:18; 2:2-3) | *dn*发怒的日子 / *dn*震怒的日子 (L, B); *dn*显出*他*怒气的日子 (R) |
| in that day (*bayyom hahu*) | 到那日 (L, B); 到那时 (R) |
| at that time (*baʿet hahi*, 1:12; 3:19-20) | 到那时, every level |
| hoy (2:5; 3:1) | ……有祸了 (L, B; 有祸了，那…… where the cry comes first); ……真是可悲 (R) |
| as I live (2:9) | [我]凭我的生命[起誓] (L); 我凭我的生命起誓 (B); 我凭着我的生命起誓 (R), as Ezekiel 5:11 |
| the fire of his / my jealousy (1:18 = 3:8) | 忌妒的火 (L); 忌妒的怒火 (B); 忌妒的怒气要像火一样燃烧 (R), as Ezekiel |
| their silver and gold will not save them (1:18) | Ezekiel 7:19's Chinese at each level |
| "I am, and there is none but me" (2:15) | Isaiah 47:8's Chinese at each level |
| the remnant | 余民 (L, B); 剩下的人 (R) |
| restore fortunes (2:7; 3:20) | 扭转……的命运 (L, B); 使……重新过上好日子 (R) |
| my people | 我的子民 (L, B); 我的百姓 (R) |
| daughter Zion; daughter Jerusalem (3:14) | 锡安女子, 耶路撒冷女子 (L, B); 锡安城的人, 耶路撒冷城的人 (R) |
| torah (3:4) | 教导 |
| correction (*musar*, 3:2, 7) | 管教 |
| idol-priests (*kemarim*, 1:4) | 拜偶像祭司 (L, B); 事奉偶像的祭司 (R), as 2 Kings 23:5 |
| the host of heaven (1:5) | 天上的万象 (L, B); 天上星星 (R) |
| "their king" (*malkam*, 1:5) | 他们的王, with 米勒公 (Milcom, 1 Kings 11:5) named in an uncertain decision |
| coastlands (2:11) | 众海岛 (L, B); 远方各地 (R); the Philistine coast 海岸 (L), 沿海 (B, R) |
| on their dregs (1:12) | 在酒渣上凝结 (L); 像沉在渣滓上变稠的酒 (B), as Jeremiah 48:11 |
| do good, do harm (of God, 1:12) | 降福, 降祸 (L, B) |
| the desert owl and the hedgehog (2:14) | 沙漠鸮和刺猬 (L, B); 沙漠里的猫头鹰和刺猬 (R), as Isaiah 34:11 |
| Do not fear (3:16) | 不要怕 (L, B); 不要害怕 (R) |

**Address and pronouns.** God speaking says 我 with no span (the English
spans "my / me"); *他* only where the English spans a pronoun for God, with
sentences recast at Readable (2:13; 3:5; 3:15) so no unspanned "He" gains a
span.

**Names** (glossary or earlier books unless marked new):

| English (first verse) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| Zephaniah; Cushi; Gedaliah; Amariah; Hezekiah (1:1) | 西番雅; 古示 (Jeremiah 36:14); 基大利; 亚玛利雅; 希西家 |
| Josiah son of Amon (1:1) | 亚们的儿子约西亚, as 2 Kings 21-22 |
| the Fish Gate; the Second Quarter (1:10) | 鱼门 (Nehemiah 3:3); 第二区 (2 Kings 22:14) |
| the Mortar (1:11) | 石臼区 (L, B), 那叫作石臼的城区 (R) — new; *alt.* 玛革提施 |
| Gaza, Ashkelon, Ashdod, Ekron (2:4) | 迦萨, 亚实基伦, 亚实突, 以革伦 |
| the Cherethites (2:5) | 基利提 / 基利提人 |
| Moab, Ammon; Sodom, Gomorrah (2:8-9) | 摩押, 亚扪 (亚扪的子孙 L); 所多玛, 蛾摩拉 |
| Cush, the Cushites (2:12; 3:10) | 古实, 古实人 |
| Assyria, Nineveh (2:13) | 亚述, 尼尼微 |

To be settled by reviewers: 忌妒 (Ezekiel's word) or 忌邪 for God's jealousy;
the Mortar as 石臼区 or a transliteration; 1:15's 黑暗和昏暗……浓云和幽暗 to be
harmonised with Joel 2:2 (drafted in parallel).

### Haggai

Haggai keeps the glossary's forms, the prophets' formula table as Isaiah
settled it, and Ezra's forms for the people and dates it shares with Ezra 5-6.
*dn* = `{{divine_name:上主|雅威}}`, *gt* = `{{god_term:神|上帝}}`.

| English (Hebrew) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| Thus says the LORD of hosts | 万军之*dn*如此说 (L); 万军之*dn*这样说 (B); 统领万军的*dn*说 (R) |
| declares the LORD (of hosts) | 这是(万军之)*dn*的宣告 (L); 这是(万军之)*dn*的话 (B); (统领万军的)*dn*说 (R) |
| says the LORD (*ʾamar*, after the words: 1:8; 2:7, 9) | ……，*dn*说 at every level |
| the word of the LORD came by the hand of Haggai (1:1, 3; 2:1) | *dn*的话借着先知哈该的手临到X (L), 借着先知哈该临到X (B); *dn*借着先知哈该传出信息 (R). Where the Hebrew names nobody it came to (1:3; 2:1): ……传来 (L, B) |
| the word of the LORD came to Haggai (2:10, 20) | *dn*的话临到(先知)哈该 (L, B); *dn*对(先知)哈该说 (R) |
| in the second year of Darius the king (1:1, 15; 2:10) | 大利乌王第二年 (L); 大利乌王在位第二年 (B); 大利乌作王的第二年 (R), as Ezra 4:24 and 6:15 |
| dates (sixth month, first day ...) | 第六个月初一, 第六个月……二十四日 (L); 六月初一, 六月二十四日 (B, R), Ezra's month forms; Literal brackets 个月 and 日 where the Hebrew leaves them out (2:1, 10, 18, 20) |
| Zerubbabel son of Shealtiel, governor of Judah | 犹大省长撒拉铁的儿子所罗巴伯, every level and verse (2:21 犹大省长所罗巴伯) |
| Joshua son of Jehozadak, the high priest | 约萨答的儿子大祭司约书亚; Ezra's Jeshua son of Jozadak is 耶书亚 there (the same man; Haggai has the long form of the name) |
| Haggai; the LORD's messenger (1:13) | 哈该; *dn*的使者 (not 天使) |
| the remnant of the people | 百姓中(所有)余剩的人 (L); 其余(所有)的百姓 (B); 所有剩下的百姓 (R) |
| "set your heart on your ways" (1:5, 7; 2:15, 18) | 你们要用心思量你们的道路 (L); 你们要仔细想想自己是怎样生活的 (B); 你们要仔细想想…… (R); 2:15, 18 用心思量 (L), 仔细想想 (B, R) |
| the house (of the LORD); the people's houses | 殿, 这殿 (L, B), 圣殿 (R); 房屋 / 房子 |
| paneled houses (1:4) | 镶着木板的房屋 (L, B); 墙上镶着木板的好房子 (R) |
| stirred up the spirit (1:14) | 激动……的灵 (L); 激动……的心 (B); 使……都热心工作 (R) |
| be strong (2:4) | 要刚强 (L, B); 要坚强 (R), as Deuteronomy 31 and Joshua 1 |
| glory; peace | 荣耀; 平安 |
| the nations | 列国 (L); 万国 (B, R) |
| once more I will shake the heavens and the earth (2:6 = Hebrews 12:26; 2:21) | 还有一次……我要摇撼 (L); 再有一次……我要摇动 (B); 再有一次……我要震动 (R): Hebrews' words for "once more" and "shake" at each level, the rest from the Hebrew (Hebrews follows the Greek), with a decision |
| instruction (*torah*, 2:11) | 教导 (L, B); 按照律法作出裁决 (R) |
| holy meat; the fold of the garment (2:12) | 圣肉 (L), 分别为圣的肉 (B), 祭肉 (R); 衣襟 |
| unnamed measures (2:16) | 份, bracketed at L; no units span |
| blight and mildew (2:17, = Amos 4:9) | 枯萎病、霉病 (L, B); 病害……干枯发黄 (R); to be matched with Amos 4:9 when it is drafted |
| in that day (2:23) | 到那日 (L, B); 到那时 (R) |
| take you (2:23) | 取你 (L); 起用你 (B, R) |
| signet ring (2:23, reversing Jeremiah 22:24) | 印戒 (L, B); 刻着我印记的戒指 (R), as Jeremiah 22:24 |

**Settings.** God speaking says 我 with no span, so the English spans on
"my", "me" and "mine" (1:9; 2:5, 8, 14, 17, 23) have none in Chinese.
"Each to their own house" (1:9) and "their garment" (2:12) take no gender
span: 各人, 自己 and 有人 already include everyone.

### Zechariah

Zechariah keeps the glossary's forms for every name and term above, the prophets' formula table (one wording per level: *dn*如此说 / *dn*这样说 / *dn*说; 这是*dn*的宣告 / 这是*dn*的话 / *dn*说; *dn*的话临到X / *dn*对X说; 万军之*dn* / 统领万军的*dn*; 到那日 / 到那时; 主宰*dn* at 9:14), Isaiah's woe (有祸了 L, B; ……真是可悲 R, 11:17), and the forms of Ezra for the returned community. New Testament quotations keep the New Testament's Chinese where the Hebrew says the same (3:2 Jude 9; 8:16 Ephesians 4:25 at L; 9:9 Matthew 21:5 and John 12:15; 11:12-13 Matthew 27:9-10; 12:10 John 19:37; 13:7 Mark 14:27) and translate the Hebrew, with a decision naming the New Testament form, where it follows the Greek. 4:6 is drafted fresh, one wording at every level. It settles these:

| English (first verse) | Chinese |
|---|---|
| Zechariah son of Berechiah, son of Iddo (1:1, 7) | 易多的儿子比利家的儿子先知撒迦利亚 (L); 比利家的儿子、易多的孙子 (B, R). Berechiah 比利家 as 1 Chronicles; Matthew 23:35's 巴拉加 (Greek) stays in Matthew |
| the dated headings (1:1, 7; 7:1) | 大利乌第二年第八个月 (L); 大利乌在位第二年的八月 (B); 大利乌作王第二年的八月 (R); Shebat 细罢特月, Kislev 基斯流月; "King Darius" 大利乌王 (7:1) |
| the fasts and their months (7:3, 5; 8:19) | 五月, 七月, 四月, 十月 at every level (the names of the fasts) |
| the angel who talked with me (1:9 and every vision) | 与我说话的天使, every level; the angel of the LORD *dn*的使者 |
| my lord (to the angel) | 我主 (L, B); 大人 (R), as Daniel 10 and 12 |
| behold in the visions; lifted up my eyes | 看哪 (L), left out (B, R); 举目观看 (L), 抬头观看 (B), 抬头一看 (R) |
| myrtle trees (1:8) | 桃金娘树 (not 番石榴, the guava) |
| walk about through the earth (1:10-11; 6:7) | 在地上走来走去 (L); 巡察全地 (B); 走遍全地 (R) |
| jealous for Jerusalem with great jealousy (1:14; 8:2) | 怀着大大的热心 (L); 怀着深切的热爱 (B); 非常在乎……我爱她们，要保护她们 (R) |
| horns; craftsmen (2:1-4) | 角; 匠人 (L, B), 手艺人 (R) |
| measuring line (2:5); "a line will be stretched" (1:16) | 量绳; 准绳 (L, B), 测量绳 (R) |
| Ho! Ho! (2:10-11, a summons) | 喂！喂！ (L); 来吧！ (B); 快！ (R), not 有祸了 |
| daughter Zion; daughter Babylon (2:11, 14) | 锡安女子 (L, B), 锡安城的人 (R); 巴比伦女子 (L, B), 巴比伦 (R); 9:9 锡安的女儿 / 耶路撒冷的女儿 (L, B) as Matthew 21:5 and John 12:15 |
| the pupil of his eye (2:12) | 眼中的瞳孔 (L); 眼珠 (B); 眼睛 (R), as Deuteronomy 32:10 |

(*他* = `{{deity_pronoun:他|祂}}`, *你* = `{{deity_pronoun:你|祢}}`.)

**Pronouns.** God speaking says 我 with no span. Where the English begins a line with an unspanned "He" and the level has no other span (9:4 R; 10:1 R; 10:11 B; 10:3 R), Chinese names the LORD or leaves the pronoun out, so the Chinese spans follow the English's.

To be settled by reviewers: 撒但 at Literal against a translated title (3:1-2); 热心 / 热爱 for the LORD's jealousy (1:14; 8:2); 锡安的女儿 (Matthew 21:5) or 锡安女子 at 9:9; 松树 for berosh (11:2); 4:6's fresh wording; 亚萨勒, 哈得拉, 利坚米勒 as new names.

### Malachi

Malachi keeps the glossary's forms for every name and term above and the prophets' formula table (one wording per level): "Thus says the LORD of hosts" 万军之*dn*如此说 (L) / 万军之*dn*这样说 (B) / 统领万军的*dn*说 (R); "says the LORD (of hosts)" after the words *dn*说 / 万军之*dn*说 (L, B), 统领万军的*dn*说 (R), as Isaiah; *neʾum* (1:2) 这是*dn*的宣告 / 这是*dn*的话 / *dn*说; *massa* (1:1) 默示 (L), 信息 (B, R). The records follow the Hebrew's three chapters; 3:19-24 is 4:1-6 in English and Chinese Bibles (a decision at 3:19 says so). New Testament quotations keep the New Testament's Chinese where the Hebrew says the same (1:2-3 = Romans 9:13; 3:24 = Luke 1:17) and translate the Hebrew where the New Testament follows the Greek (3:1, Matthew 11:10, Mark 1:2, Luke 7:27, "before your face"), with a decision. Famous lines (1:11; 3:2; 3:10; 3:16-17; 3:19-20) are drafted fresh. *dn* = `{{divine_name:上主|雅威}}`, *gt* = `{{god_term:神|上帝}}`, *他* = `{{deity_pronoun:他|祂}}`, *你* = `{{deity_pronoun:你|祢}}`.

| lift up the face (1:8, 9) | 抬起……的脸 (L); 恩待 (B); 接纳 (R) |

Address: God speaking says 我 with no span; the people's 你 and 他 for God take `deity_pronoun` spans where the English has them, and Readable keeps the span at a sentence start where the English starts with an unspanned "He" (3:2-3). To be settled by reviewers: 约 or 盟约 at R for the covenant; the Joel 3:4 wording at 3:23 once Joel is drafted.
